# Change the Landowners' stance on serfdom
effect_reorder_landowner_traits = {
	ig:ig_landowners = {
		# remove_ideology = ideology_stratocratic
		# remove_ideology = ideology_stratocratic_modern
		if		= { limit = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_republican_paternalistic }	remove_ideology = ideology_republican_paternalistic		add_ideology = ideology_republican_paternalistic	}
		else_if	= { limit = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_junker_paternalistic }		remove_ideology = ideology_junker_paternalistic			add_ideology = ideology_junker_paternalistic		}
		else_if	= { limit = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_papal_paternalistic }		remove_ideology = ideology_papal_paternalistic			add_ideology = ideology_papal_paternalistic			}
		else_if	= { limit = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_scholar_paternalistic }		remove_ideology = ideology_scholar_paternalistic		add_ideology = ideology_scholar_paternalistic		}
		if		= { limit = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_hierarchic }					remove_ideology = ideology_hierarchic					add_ideology = ideology_hierarchic					}
		else_if	= { limit = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_hierarchic_modern }			remove_ideology = ideology_hierarchic_modern			add_ideology = ideology_hierarchic_modern			}
		if		= { limit = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_patriarchal }				remove_ideology = ideology_patriarchal					add_ideology = ideology_patriarchal					}
		else_if	= { limit = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_patriarchal_suffrage }		remove_ideology = ideology_patriarchal_suffrage			add_ideology = ideology_patriarchal_suffrage		}
		if		= { limit = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_loyalist }					remove_ideology = ideology_loyalist						add_ideology = ideology_loyalist					}
		if		= { limit = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_pious }						remove_ideology = ideology_pious						add_ideology = ideology_pious						}
		if		= { limit = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_pro_slavery }				remove_ideology = ideology_pro_slavery					add_ideology = ideology_pro_slavery					}
		else_if	= { limit = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_pro_slavery_colonial }		remove_ideology = ideology_pro_slavery_colonial			add_ideology = ideology_pro_slavery_colonial		}
	}
}
effect_modern_hierarchic = {
	if = {
		limit = { any_interest_group = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_hierarchic } }
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = modern_hierarchic_tt
			every_interest_group = {
				limit = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_hierarchic }
				remove_ideology = ideology_hierarchic
				add_ideology = ideology_hierarchic_modern
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = { effect_reorder_landowner_traits = yes }
	}
}
effect_colonialist_industrialists = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_laissez_faire }
		remove_ideology	= ideology_laissez_faire
		add_ideology	= ideology_colonialist
	}
}
effect_reorder_industrialists_traits = {
	ig:ig_landowners = {
		if		= { limit = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_laissez_faire	}	remove_ideology = ideology_laissez_faire	add_ideology = ideology_laissez_faire	}
		else_if	= { limit = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_colonialist		}	remove_ideology = ideology_colonialist		add_ideology = ideology_colonialist		}
		if		= { limit = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_plutocratic		}	remove_ideology = ideology_plutocratic		add_ideology = ideology_plutocratic		}
		else_if	= { limit = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_enclavist		}	remove_ideology = ideology_enclavist		add_ideology = ideology_enclavist		}
		if		= { limit = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_individualist	}	remove_ideology = ideology_individualist	add_ideology = ideology_individualist	}
		else_if	= { limit = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_malthusian		}	remove_ideology = ideology_malthusian		add_ideology = ideology_malthusian		}
	}
}
effect_technocratic_intelligentsia = {	# Unused so far
	if		= { limit = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_republican }			remove_ideology = ideology_republican }
	else_if	= { limit = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_constitutionalist }	remove_ideology = ideology_constitutionalist }
	add_ideology = ideology_technocratic
}
effect_colonial_to_regular_slavery_movement = {	# Might be unused due to refactoring
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_political_movement = {
				is_political_movement_type = movement_pro_slavery
				has_core_ideology = ideology:ideology_pro_slavery_colonial_movement
			}
		}
		random_political_movement = {
			limit = {
				is_political_movement_type = movement_pro_slavery
				has_core_ideology = ideology:ideology_pro_slavery_colonial_movement
			}
			set_core_ideology = ideology_pro_slavery_movement
		}
	}
}
upgrade_liberal_movement_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OWNER = {
				has_technology_researched = feminism
				any_political_movement = {
					is_political_movement_type = movement_liberal
					has_core_ideology = ideology:ideology_liberal_movement
				}
			}
		}
		OWNER = {
			random_political_movement = {
				limit = {
					is_political_movement_type = movement_liberal
					has_core_ideology = ideology:ideology_liberal_movement
				}
				set_core_ideology = ideology_liberal_modern_movement
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_parliamentary_oligarchy = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_law = law_type:law_autocracy
			has_technology_researched = central_archives
			OR = {
				AND = {
					exists = ruler
					ruler ?= { law_stance = { law = law_type:law_technocracy value > neutral } }
				}
				is_enacting_law = law_type:law_technocracy
			}
		}
		activate_law = law_type:law_technocracy
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_law = law_type:law_autocracy
			OR = {
				AND = {
					exists = ruler
					ruler ?= { law_stance = { law = law_type:law_landed_voting value > neutral } }
				}
				is_enacting_law = law_type:law_landed_voting
			}
		}
		activate_law = law_type:law_landed_voting
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_law = law_type:law_autocracy }
		activate_law = law_type:law_oligarchy
	}
}
effect_handle_religion = {
	if		= { limit = { country_has_state_religion = rel:atheist has_variable = meiji_restoration_complete } set_state_religion = rel:shinto }
	else_if	= { limit = { country_has_state_religion = rel:atheist } set_state_religion = root.country_definition.religion }
	if		= { limit = { has_modifier = antireligious_policies } remove_modifier = antireligious_policies }
	if		= {
		limit = {
			has_law = law_millet_system
			country_is_islamic = yes
			NOT = { c:TUR ?= this }
		}
		deactivate_law = law_type:law_millet_system
		activate_law = law_type:law_people_of_the_book
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_law = law_millet_system
			NOR = { c:TUR ?= this country_has_state_religion = rel:sunni }
		}
		deactivate_law = law_type:law_millet_system
		activate_law = law_type:law_state_religion
	}
}
effect_agitator_leadership = {
	remove_character_role = agitator
	add_character_role = politician
	custom_tooltip = {
		text = make_agitator_ig_leader_tt
		set_as_interest_group_leader = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:actor = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_state_atheism }
			NOR = {
				is_interest_group_type = ig_devout
				has_ideology = ideology:ideology_theocrat
				has_ideology = ideology:ideology_integralist
				has_ideology = ideology:ideology_traditionalist
				has_ideology = ideology:ideology_traditionalist_minoritarian
				# law_stance = { law = law_type:law_state_religion value > neutral }
				religion = rel:atheist
				is_historical = yes
			}
		}
		change_character_religion = rel:atheist
	}
}
effect_handle_cost = {	# state_scope	# cost debtor
	save_temporary_scope_value_as = {
		name = adjusted_construction_cost
		value = {
			value		= $cost$			# Building level * cost per level in millions
			multiply	= const_cost_factor
			multiply	=-1000000			#-1M
		}
	}
	custom_tooltip = {
		text = adjusted_construction_cost.tt
		if = {
			limit = { $debtor$ ?= { is_player = yes has_variable = loans_var } }
			$debtor$ ?= { change_variable = { name = loans_var add = PREV.scope:adjusted_construction_cost } }
		}
		else = { $debtor$ ?= { add_treasury = PREV.scope:adjusted_construction_cost } }
	}
}
effect_destroy_building = {
	show_as_tooltip	= { if = { limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = $building$ } }	remove_building = $building$ } }
	hidden_effect	= {																				remove_building = $building$ }
}
# Choo-Choo motherf*cker
effect_calc_transportation_delta = {	# Thx to Pzixel
	hidden_effect = {
		remove_variable = transportation_delta
		remove_variable = transportation_balance_right
		remove_variable = transportation_balance_mid
		if = {
			limit = { sg:transportation = { state_goods_delta < 0 } }
			set_variable = { name = transportation_delta			value =-1000000 }
			set_variable = { name = transportation_balance_right	value =       0 }
			while = {		# use binary search to find the amount at which state_goods_delta is 0
				count = 21	# log2(1000000) + 1
				set_variable = {
					name = transportation_balance_mid
					value = {
						add = {
							value = var:transportation_delta
							add = var:transportation_balance_right
							divide = 2
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { sg:transportation = { state_goods_delta > PREV.var:transportation_balance_mid } }
					set_variable = { name = transportation_delta value = var:transportation_balance_mid }
				}
				else = { set_variable = { name = transportation_balance_right value = var:transportation_balance_mid } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OWNER = { has_technology_researched = mechanized_workshops }
					NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_autarky_farm trigger_subsistence_building_uses_transportation = yes } }
				}
				change_variable = { name = transportation_delta subtract = transportation_from_arable_land }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { OWNER = { has_technology_researched = mechanized_workshops } }
				change_variable = { name = transportation_delta subtract = transportation_from_free_arable_land }
			}
			every_scope_building = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_active_production_method = pm_rail_transport
						has_active_production_method = pm_truck_transport
						has_active_production_method = pm_flatbed_carts
						has_active_production_method = pm_flatbed_trucks
						has_active_production_method = pm_tanker_cars
					}
				}
				STATE ?= { change_variable = { name = transportation_delta subtract = PREV.transportation_usage_potential_building } }
			}
			# here transportation_delta holds the exact value of state_goods_delta (up to integer precision)
		}
	}
}
effect_calc_railways = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_technology_researched = railways }
		every_scope_state = {
			remove_variable = missing_railways
			remove_variable = must_destroy_railway
			effect_calc_transportation_delta = yes
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_railway } } }
				hidden_effect = { create_building = { building = building_railway level = 1 } }
				set_variable = must_destroy_railway
			}
			else = { set_variable = { name = must_destroy_railway value = no } }
			set_variable = {
				name = missing_railways
				value = missing_railways
			}
			hidden_effect = { if = { limit = { var:must_destroy_railway = yes } remove_building = building_railway } }
		}
	}
}
effect_build_railways_state = {
	if = {
		limit = { var:missing_railways > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_railway
		change_variable = { name = missing_railways		subtract = 1 }
		change_variable = { name = transportation_delta	add = transportation_per_railway }
	}
}
effect_build_railways_state_private = {
	if = {
		limit = { var:missing_railways > 0 }
		start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_railway
		change_variable = { name = missing_railways		subtract = 1 }
		change_variable = { name = transportation_delta	add = transportation_per_railway }
	}
}
effect_build_railways = {
	effect_calc_railways = yes
	show_as_tooltip = {
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { var:missing_railways > 0 }
			while = {
				count = var:missing_railways
				start_building_construction = building_railway
			}
		}
	}
	hidden_effect = {
		while = {	# Striped
			limit = { any_scope_state = { var:missing_railways > 0 } }
			every_scope_state = { effect_build_railways_state = yes }
		}
	}
}
effect_build_railways_private = {
	effect_calc_railways = yes
	show_as_tooltip = {
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { var:missing_railways > 0 }
			while = {
				count = var:missing_railways
				start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_railway
			}
		}
	}
	hidden_effect = {
		while = {	# Striped
			limit = { any_scope_state = { var:missing_railways > 0 } }
			every_scope_state = { effect_build_railways_state_private = yes }
		}
	}
}

	every_scope_state = {

		limit = { is_coastal = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_port } } }
			hidden_effect = { create_building = { building = building_port level = 1 } }
			set_variable = must_destroy_port
		}
		else = { set_variable = { name = must_destroy_port value = no } }
	}
	set_variable = {
		name = missing_convoys
		value = {
			value = max_convoys_required
			if = { limit = { NOT = { THIS ?= market.OWNER } } add = market.OWNER.max_convoys_required }
			min = 0
		}
	}
	ordered_scope_state = {
		limit = { has_building = building_port }
		order_by = b:building_port.level_after_queued_constructions
		position = 0
		save_scope_as = port_state
	}

		name = missing_ports
		value = {
			value	= max_convoys_required
			if = { limit = { NOT = { THIS ?= market.OWNER } } add = market.OWNER.max_convoys_required }
			divide	= scope:port_state.convoys_per_port
			round = yes
		}
	}

effect_calc_admins = {	# New Version - Potentially fixes case where more than one state fulfills trigger_bureacracy_admin_state
	if = {
		limit = { has_technology_researched = tech_bureaucracy }
		set_variable = {
			name = missing_bureacracy
			value = OWNER.max_bureaucracy_required
		}
		every_scope_state = {
			remove_variable = missing_admins
			remove_variable = must_destroy_admin
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_government_administration } } }
				hidden_effect = { create_building = { building = building_government_administration level = 1 } }
				set_variable = must_destroy_admin
			}
			else = { set_variable = { name = must_destroy_admin value = no } }
			if = {
				limit = { trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = yes } # tax_capacity_balance < 0 - Removed - Prevents Bureacracy top-off otherwise
				set_variable = {
					name = missing_admins
					value = {
						value = missing_admins_tax
						if = {
							limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_government_administration occupancy < 0.95 level > 0 } }
							subtract = {	# Level * (1 - Occupancy)
								value		= 1
								subtract	= b:building_government_administration.occupancy
								multiply	= b:building_government_administration.level
							}
						}
						if = {
							limit = { b:building_government_administration = { is_under_construction = yes } }
							subtract = queued_admin_levels
						}
						round = yes
						min = 0
						if = { limit = { var:must_destroy_admin = yes } min = 1 }	# Every inc. state needs a building_government_administration
					}
				}
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = missing_bureacracy subtract = PREV.missing_admins_tax_bur } }
			}
			else = { set_variable = { name = missing_admins value = 0 } }
			hidden_effect = { if = { limit = { var:must_destroy_admin = yes } remove_building = building_government_administration } }
		}
		while = {
			limit = { var:missing_bureacracy > 0 }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { trigger_bureacracy_admin_state = yes OWNER.var:missing_bureacracy > 0 }
				change_variable = { name = missing_admins add = 1 }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = missing_bureacracy subtract = PREV.bureacracy_per_admin } }
			}
		}
		# every_scope_state = { custom_label = debug_label_admin }
	}
}
effect_build_admin_state = {
	start_building_construction = building_government_administration
	if = { limit = { has_variable = missing_admins } change_variable = { name = missing_admins subtract = 1 } }
	OWNER = { change_variable = { name = missing_bureacracy subtract = PREV.bureacracy_per_admin } }
}
effect_make_admins = {
	effect_calc_admins = yes
	show_as_tooltip = {
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { var:missing_admins > 0 }
			while = {
				count = var:missing_admins
				start_building_construction = building_government_administration
			}
		}
	}
	hidden_effect = {
		while = {	# Striped
			limit = { any_scope_state = { var:missing_admins > 0 } }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { var:missing_admins > 0 }
				effect_build_admin_state = yes
			}
		}
	}
}
# Spicy Lightning
effect_calc_electricity_delta = {	# Thx to Pzixel
	hidden_effect = {
		remove_variable = electricity_delta
		remove_variable = electricity_balance_right
		remove_variable = electricity_balance_mid
		set_variable = { name = electricity_delta			value =-1000000 }
		set_variable = { name = electricity_balance_right	value =       0 }
		while = {		# use binary search to find the amount at which state_goods_delta is 0
			count = 21	# log2(1000000) + 1
			set_variable = {
				name = electricity_balance_mid
				value = {
					add = {
						value = var:electricity_delta
						add = var:electricity_balance_right
						divide = 2
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { sg:electricity = { state_goods_delta > PREV.var:electricity_balance_mid } }
				set_variable = { name = electricity_delta value = var:electricity_balance_mid }
			}
			else = { set_variable = { name = electricity_balance_right value = var:electricity_balance_mid } }
		}
		change_variable = { name = electricity_delta add = queued_electricity }
		if = {
			limit = {
				total_queued_railway_levels > 0
				is_production_method_active = { building_type = building_railway production_method = pm_electric_rails }
			}
			subtract = {
				value		= total_queued_railway_levels
				# multiply	= 1	# per level
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				total_queued_railway_levels > 0
				OR = {
					is_production_method_active = { building_type = building_railway production_method = pm_electric_trains }
					is_production_method_active = { building_type = building_railway production_method = pm_maglev_trains }
				}
			}
			subtract = {
				value		= total_queued_railway_levels
				multiply	= 5	# per level
				if = { limit = { is_production_method_active = { building_type = building_railway production_method = pm_high_speed_rail } } multiply = 1.5 }
			}
		}
		# here electricity_delta holds the exact value of state_goods_delta (up to integer precision)
		# set_variable = { name  = electricity_delta value = sg:electricity.state_goods_delta }		# Doesn't work. Thx PDX
	}
}
effect_build_electricity = {
	if = {
		limit = { any_scope_state = { is_production_method_active = { building_type = building_autarky_greenhouse	production_method = pm_autark_greenhouse } } }
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = effect_build_electricity_green.tt
			effect_calc_electricity_delta = yes
			while = {
				limit = { any_scope_state = { var:electricity_delta < 0 } }
				every_scope_state = {
					limit = {
						var:electricity_delta < 0
						any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_autarky_greenhouse has_active_production_method = pm_autark_greenhouse OR = { occupancy > 0.95 level = 0 } }
					}
					start_building_construction = building_autarky_greenhouse
					change_variable = { name = electricity_delta	add = power_per_autark_greenhouse }
				}
				every_scope_state = {
					limit = {
						var:electricity_delta < 0
						trigger_power_plant_allowed = yes
					}
					effect_build_powerplants_state = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		effect_build_powerplants = yes
		# custom_tooltip = {
			# text = effect_build_electricity.tt
			# effect_calc_electricity_delta = yes
			# while = {
				# limit = { any_scope_state = { var:electricity_delta < 0 } }
				# every_scope_state = {
					# limit = {
						# var:electricity_delta < 0
						# any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_power_plant OR = { occupancy > 0.95 level = 0 } }
					# }
					# start_building_construction = building_power_plant
					# change_variable = { name = electricity_delta	add = power_per_plant }
				# }
			# }
		# }
	}
}

effect_calc_powerplants = {
	every_scope_state = {
		remove_variable = missing_powerplants
		remove_variable = missing_autarky_greenhouse_electricity_state
		remove_variable = must_destroy_powerplant
		remove_variable = must_destroy_autarky_greenhouse
	}
	if = {
		limit = { any_scope_state = { is_production_method_active = { building_type = building_autarky_greenhouse	production_method = pm_autark_greenhouse } } }
		every_scope_state = {
			effect_calc_electricity_delta = yes
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_autarky_greenhouse } } }
				hidden_effect = { create_building = { building = building_autarky_greenhouse level = 1 } }
				set_variable = must_destroy_autarky_greenhouse
			}
			else = { set_variable = { name = must_destroy_autarky_greenhouse value = no } }
			set_variable = {
				name = missing_autarky_greenhouse_electricity_state
				value = missing_autarky_greenhouse_electricity_state
			}
			hidden_effect = { if = { limit = { var:must_destroy_autarky_greenhouse = yes } remove_building = building_autarky_greenhouse } }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_technology_researched = electrical_generation }
		every_scope_state = {
			effect_calc_electricity_delta = yes
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_power_plant } } }
				hidden_effect = { create_building = { building = building_power_plant level = 1 } }
				set_variable = must_destroy_powerplant
			}
			else = { set_variable = { name = must_destroy_powerplant value = no } }
			set_variable = {
				name = missing_powerplants
				value = missing_powerplants
			}
			hidden_effect = { if = { limit = { var:must_destroy_powerplant = yes } remove_building = building_power_plant } }
		}
	}
	else = { every_scope_state = { set_variable = { name = missing_powerplants value = 0 } } }
}
effect_build_powerplants_state = {
	if = {
		limit = { var:missing_powerplants > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_power_plant
		change_variable = { name = missing_powerplants	subtract = 1 }
		change_variable = { name = electricity_delta	add = power_per_plant }
	}
}
effect_build_powerplants_state_private = {
	if = {
		limit = { var:missing_powerplants > 0 }
		start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_power_plant
		change_variable = { name = missing_powerplants	subtract = 1 }
		change_variable = { name = electricity_delta	add = power_per_plant }
	}
}

effect_build_infrastructure = {
	effect_calc_railways	= yes
	effect_calc_powerplants	= yes
	show_as_tooltip = {
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { OR = { var:missing_railways > 0 var:missing_powerplants > 0 } }
			if = {
				limit = { var:missing_railways > 0 }
				while = {
					count = var:missing_railways
					start_building_construction = building_railway
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { var:missing_powerplants > 0 }
				while = {
					count = var:missing_powerplants
					start_building_construction = building_power_plant
				}
			}
		}
	}
	hidden_effect = {
		while = {	# Striped
			limit = { any_scope_state = { OR = { var:missing_railways > 0 var:missing_powerplants > 0 } } }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { OR = { var:missing_railways > 0 var:missing_powerplants > 0 } }
				effect_build_railways_state		= yes
				effect_build_powerplants_state	= yes
			}
		}
	}
}

effect_build_infrastructure_mixed = {
	effect_calc_railways	= yes
	effect_calc_powerplants	= yes
	show_as_tooltip = {
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = { var:missing_railways > 0 var:missing_powerplants > 0 }
				trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = { var:missing_railways > 0 }
				while = {
					count = var:missing_railways
					start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_railway
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { var:missing_powerplants > 0 }
				while = {
					count = var:missing_powerplants
					start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_power_plant
				}
			}
		}
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = { var:missing_railways > 0 var:missing_powerplants > 0 }
				trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = no
			}
			if = {
				limit = { var:missing_railways > 0 }
				while = {
					count = var:missing_railways
					start_building_construction = building_railway
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { var:missing_powerplants > 0 }
				while = {
					count = var:missing_powerplants
					start_building_construction = building_power_plant
				}
			}
		}
	}
	hidden_effect = {
		while = {	# Striped
			limit = { any_scope_state = { OR = { var:missing_railways > 0 var:missing_powerplants > 0 } } }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					OR = { var:missing_railways > 0 var:missing_powerplants > 0 }
					trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = yes
				}
				effect_build_railways_state_private		= yes
				effect_build_powerplants_state_private	= yes
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					OR = { var:missing_railways > 0 var:missing_powerplants > 0 }
					trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = no
				}
				effect_build_railways_state				= yes
				effect_build_powerplants_state			= yes
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_build_trade = {
	show_as_tooltip = {
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { missing_trade > 0 }
			while = {
				count = missing_trade
				start_building_construction = building_trade_center
			}
		}
	}
	hidden_effect = {
		every_scope_state = {	# Otherwise the interrupt doesn't work correctly
			limit = { missing_trade > 0 }
			set_variable = {
				name = missing_trade_var
				value = missing_trade
			}
		}
		every_scope_state = {	# Otherwise the interrupt doesn't work correctly
			limit = { missing_trade < 1 }
			set_variable = { name = missing_trade_var value = 0 }
		}
		while = {	# Striped
			limit = { any_scope_state = { var:missing_trade_var > 0 } }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { var:missing_trade_var > 0 }
				start_building_construction = building_trade_center
				change_variable = { name = missing_trade_var subtract = 1 }
			}
		}
	}
}

effect_seed_const = {
	show_as_tooltip = {
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = yes is_under_colonization = no var:const_to_seed > 0 }	# Hokkaido is incorporated AND under colonisation at game start.
			while = {
				count = var:const_to_seed
				start_building_construction = building_construction_sector
			}
		}
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = no is_under_colonization = no var:const_to_seed > 0 }
			while = {
				count = var:const_to_seed
				start_building_construction = building_construction_sector
			}
		}
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = { is_under_colonization = yes is_treaty_port = yes }
				state_population > 40000
				var:const_to_seed > 0
			}
			while = {
				count = var:const_to_seed
				start_building_construction = building_construction_sector
			}
		}
	}
	while = {	# Striped
		limit = { any_scope_state = { var:const_to_seed > 0 } }
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = yes is_under_colonization = no is_treaty_port = no var:const_to_seed > 0 }	# NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_construction_sector is_under_construction = yes } }
			start_building_construction = building_construction_sector
			change_variable = { name = const_to_seed subtract = 1 }
		}
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = no is_treaty_port = no is_under_colonization = no var:const_to_seed > 0 }	# NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_construction_sector is_under_construction = yes } }
			start_building_construction = building_construction_sector
			change_variable = { name = const_to_seed subtract = 1 }
		}
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { OR = { is_under_colonization = yes is_treaty_port = yes } state_population > 40000 var:const_to_seed > 0 }			# NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_construction_sector is_under_construction = yes } }
			start_building_construction = building_construction_sector
			change_variable = { name = const_to_seed subtract = 1 }
		}
	}
	# effect_fix_construction_goods_shortage = yes
}
effect_calc_const = {
	every_scope_state = {
		remove_variable = const_to_build
		remove_variable = const_to_seed
	}
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = {
			# is_treaty_port = no
			# OR = {
				# is_split_state = no
				# is_under_colonization = no
				# is_largest_state_in_region = yes
			# }
			# trigger_microstate = no
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = const_to_build
			value = {
				value = modifier:state_building_construction_sector_max_level_add
				subtract = b:building_construction_sector.level_after_queued_constructions
				min = 0
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = const_to_seed
			value = {
				value = 5
				subtract = b:building_construction_sector.level_after_queued_constructions
				min = 0
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_build_const = {
	effect_calc_const = yes
	show_as_tooltip = {
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = yes is_under_colonization = no var:const_to_build > 0 }	# Hokkaido is incorporated AND under colonisation at game start.
			while = {
				count = var:const_to_build
				start_building_construction = building_construction_sector
			}
		}
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = no is_under_colonization = no is_treaty_port = no var:const_to_build > 0 }
			while = {
				count = var:const_to_build
				start_building_construction = building_construction_sector
			}
		}
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = { is_under_colonization = yes is_treaty_port = yes }
				state_population > 40000
				var:const_to_build > 0
			}
			while = {
				count = var:const_to_build
				start_building_construction = building_construction_sector
			}
		}
	}
	while = {	# Striped
		limit = { any_scope_state = { var:const_to_build > 0 } }
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = yes is_under_colonization = no is_treaty_port = no var:const_to_build > 0 }	# NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_construction_sector is_under_construction = yes } }
			start_building_construction = building_construction_sector
			change_variable = { name = const_to_build subtract = 1 }
		}
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = no is_treaty_port = no is_under_colonization = no var:const_to_build > 0 }	# NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_construction_sector is_under_construction = yes } }
			start_building_construction = building_construction_sector
			change_variable = { name = const_to_build subtract = 1 }
		}
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { OR = { is_under_colonization = yes is_treaty_port = yes } state_population > 40000 var:const_to_build > 0 }			# NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_construction_sector is_under_construction = yes } }
			start_building_construction = building_construction_sector
			change_variable = { name = const_to_build subtract = 1 }
		}
	}
	# effect_fix_construction_goods_shortage = yes
}
effect_calc_unis = {
	ordered_scope_state = {  
		limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_university } }
		order_by = b:building_university.level_after_queued_constructions
		position = 0
		save_temporary_scope_as = highest_uni_state
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:highest_uni_state }
		set_variable = {
			name = highest_uni_level
			value = {
				value		= scope:highest_uni_state.b:building_university.level_after_queued_constructions
				divide		=	5	# Yes, it has to be this complicated, the built-in rounding function truncates.
				ceiling		= yes	# Yes, it has to be this complicated, the built-in rounding function truncates.
				multiply	=	5	# Yes, it has to be this complicated, the built-in rounding function truncates.
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:highest_uni_state.b:building_university.level_after_queued_constructions >= 5
						NOT = {
							any_scope_building = {
								is_building_type = building_university
								state = { trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = yes }	# Otherwise breaks when any unincorporated state has a university
								level_after_queued_constructions < scope:highest_uni_state.b:building_university.level_after_queued_constructions
							}
						}
					}
					add		=   5
				}
				min			=   5
				max			= 100
			}
		}
	}
	else = { set_variable = { name = highest_uni_level value = 5 } }
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = { trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = yes }
		set_variable = {
			name = unis_to_build
			value = {
				value = ROOT.var:highest_uni_level
				subtract = b:building_university.level_after_queued_constructions
				min = 0
			}
		}
	}
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = { trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = no }
		set_variable = {
			name = unis_to_build
			value = {
				value = 5
				subtract = b:building_university.level_after_queued_constructions
				min = 0
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_build_unis = {
	effect_calc_unis = yes
	show_as_tooltip = {
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = yes var:unis_to_build > 0 }
			# order_by = state_population
			while = {
				count = var:unis_to_build
				start_building_construction = building_university
			}
		}
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = no var:unis_to_build > 0 }
			# order_by = state_population
			while = {
				count = var:unis_to_build
				start_building_construction = building_university
			}
		}
	}
	hidden_effect = {
		while = {	# Striped
			limit = { any_scope_state = { var:unis_to_build > 0 } }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = yes var:unis_to_build > 0 }
				start_building_construction = building_university
				change_variable = { name = unis_to_build subtract = 1 }
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = no var:unis_to_build > 0 }
				start_building_construction = building_university
				change_variable = { name = unis_to_build subtract = 1 }
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_fix_literacy_state = {
	if		= { limit = { modifier:state_education_access_add >= 1.00 }	every_scope_pop = { limit = { literacy_rate < 1.00 } set_pop_literacy = { literacy_rate = { value = 1.00 } } } }
	else_if	= { limit = { modifier:state_education_access_add >= 0.90 }	every_scope_pop = { limit = { literacy_rate < 0.90 } set_pop_literacy = { literacy_rate = { value = 0.90 } } } }
	else_if	= { limit = { modifier:state_education_access_add >= 0.80 }	every_scope_pop = { limit = { literacy_rate < 0.80 } set_pop_literacy = { literacy_rate = { value = 0.80 } } } }
	else_if	= { limit = { modifier:state_education_access_add >= 0.70 }	every_scope_pop = { limit = { literacy_rate < 0.70 } set_pop_literacy = { literacy_rate = { value = 0.70 } } } }
	else_if	= { limit = { modifier:state_education_access_add >= 0.60 }	every_scope_pop = { limit = { literacy_rate < 0.60 } set_pop_literacy = { literacy_rate = { value = 0.60 } } } }
	else_if	= { limit = { modifier:state_education_access_add >= 0.50 }	every_scope_pop = { limit = { literacy_rate < 0.50 } set_pop_literacy = { literacy_rate = { value = 0.50 } } } }
	else_if	= { limit = { modifier:state_education_access_add >= 0.40 }	every_scope_pop = { limit = { literacy_rate < 0.40 } set_pop_literacy = { literacy_rate = { value = 0.40 } } } }
	else_if	= { limit = { modifier:state_education_access_add >= 0.30 }	every_scope_pop = { limit = { literacy_rate < 0.30 } set_pop_literacy = { literacy_rate = { value = 0.30 } } } }
	else_if	= { limit = { modifier:state_education_access_add >= 0.20 }	every_scope_pop = { limit = { literacy_rate < 0.20 } set_pop_literacy = { literacy_rate = { value = 0.20 } } } }
	else_if	= { limit = { modifier:state_education_access_add >= 0.10 }	every_scope_pop = { limit = { literacy_rate < 0.10 } set_pop_literacy = { literacy_rate = { value = 0.10 } } } }
}
effect_fix_literacy_peasants_state = {
	if		= { limit = { peasant_education_access >= 1.00 }			every_scope_pop = { limit = { is_pop_type = peasants literacy_rate < 1.00 } set_pop_literacy = { literacy_rate = { value = 1.00 } } } }
	else_if	= { limit = { peasant_education_access >= 0.90 }			every_scope_pop = { limit = { is_pop_type = peasants literacy_rate < 0.90 } set_pop_literacy = { literacy_rate = { value = 0.90 } } } }
	else_if	= { limit = { peasant_education_access >= 0.80 }			every_scope_pop = { limit = { is_pop_type = peasants literacy_rate < 0.80 } set_pop_literacy = { literacy_rate = { value = 0.80 } } } }
	else_if	= { limit = { peasant_education_access >= 0.70 }			every_scope_pop = { limit = { is_pop_type = peasants literacy_rate < 0.70 } set_pop_literacy = { literacy_rate = { value = 0.70 } } } }
	else_if	= { limit = { peasant_education_access >= 0.60 }			every_scope_pop = { limit = { is_pop_type = peasants literacy_rate < 0.60 } set_pop_literacy = { literacy_rate = { value = 0.60 } } } }
	else_if	= { limit = { peasant_education_access >= 0.50 }			every_scope_pop = { limit = { is_pop_type = peasants literacy_rate < 0.50 } set_pop_literacy = { literacy_rate = { value = 0.50 } } } }
	else_if	= { limit = { peasant_education_access >= 0.40 }			every_scope_pop = { limit = { is_pop_type = peasants literacy_rate < 0.40 } set_pop_literacy = { literacy_rate = { value = 0.40 } } } }
	else_if	= { limit = { peasant_education_access >= 0.30 }			every_scope_pop = { limit = { is_pop_type = peasants literacy_rate < 0.30 } set_pop_literacy = { literacy_rate = { value = 0.30 } } } }
	else_if	= { limit = { peasant_education_access >= 0.20 }			every_scope_pop = { limit = { is_pop_type = peasants literacy_rate < 0.20 } set_pop_literacy = { literacy_rate = { value = 0.20 } } } }
	else_if	= { limit = { peasant_education_access >= 0.10 }			every_scope_pop = { limit = { is_pop_type = peasants literacy_rate < 0.10 } set_pop_literacy = { literacy_rate = { value = 0.10 } } } }
}
effect_spawn_peasants_state = {
	if = {
		limit = { trigger_valid_pop_spawn_state = yes }
		# set_local_variable = { name = peasants_size value = peasant_pop_size }	# anti-lag measure
		if		= { limit = { is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india }															create_pop	= {	culture = anglo_indian		religion = atheist	pop_type = peasants	size = peasant_pop_size } }
		else_if	= { limit = { is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_africa }															create_pop	= {	culture = african			religion = atheist	pop_type = peasants	size = peasant_pop_size } }
		else_if	= { limit = { is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_china }															create_pop	= {	culture = han				religion = atheist	pop_type = peasants	size = peasant_pop_size } }
		else_if	= { limit = { is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_russia }															create_pop	= {	culture = east_german		religion = atheist	pop_type = peasants	size = peasant_pop_size } }
		else_if	= { limit = { OWNER = { any_primary_culture = { trigger_european_jew = yes } } }											create_pop	= {	culture = ashkenazi			religion = jewish	pop_type = peasants	size = peasant_pop_size } }
		else_if	= { limit = { OWNER = { any_primary_culture = { trigger_arabic_jew = yes } } }												create_pop	= {	culture = sephardic			religion = jewish	pop_type = peasants	size = peasant_pop_size } }
		else_if	= { limit = { OWNER = { any_primary_culture = { has_discrimination_trait = tradition_russosphere } } }						create_pop	= {	culture = east_german		religion = atheist	pop_type = peasants	size = peasant_pop_size } }
		else_if	= { limit = { OWNER = { any_primary_culture = { has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_european } } }				create_pop	= {	culture = north_german		religion = atheist	pop_type = peasants	size = peasant_pop_size } }
		else_if	= {
			limit = {
				OWNER = {
					any_primary_culture = { has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_east_asian }
					has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_state_atheism
				}
			}																																create_pop	= {	culture = japanese			religion = atheist	pop_type = peasants	size = peasant_pop_size }
		}
		else_if	= { limit = { OWNER = { any_primary_culture = { has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_east_asian } } }			create_pop	= {	culture = japanese			religion = shinto	pop_type = peasants	size = peasant_pop_size } }
		else_if	= { limit = { OWNER = { any_primary_culture = { has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_north_asian } } }			create_pop	= {	culture = siberian			religion = atheist	pop_type = peasants	size = peasant_pop_size } }
		else_if	= { limit = { OWNER = { any_primary_culture = { has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_central_asian } } }			create_pop	= {	culture = kazak				religion = atheist	pop_type = peasants	size = peasant_pop_size } }
		else_if	= { limit = { OWNER = { any_primary_culture = { has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_south_asian } } }			create_pop	= {	culture = anglo_indian		religion = atheist	pop_type = peasants	size = peasant_pop_size } }
		else_if	= { limit = { OWNER = { any_primary_culture = { has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_southeast_asian } } }		create_pop	= {	culture = thai				religion = atheist	pop_type = peasants	size = peasant_pop_size } }
		else_if	= { limit = { OWNER = { any_primary_culture = { has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_african } } }				create_pop	= {	culture = african			religion = atheist	pop_type = peasants	size = peasant_pop_size } }
		else_if	= { limit = { OWNER = { any_primary_culture = { has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_middle_eastern } } }		create_pop	= {	culture = turkish			religion = atheist	pop_type = peasants	size = peasant_pop_size } }
		else_if	= { limit = { OWNER = { any_primary_culture = { has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_indigenous_oceanic } } }	create_pop	= {	culture = polynesian		religion = atheist	pop_type = peasants	size = peasant_pop_size } }
		else_if	= { limit = { OWNER = { any_primary_culture = { has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_indigenous_american } } }	create_pop	= {	culture = siouan			religion = atheist	pop_type = peasants	size = peasant_pop_size } }
		else	= {																															create_pop	= {	culture = promethean		religion = atheist	pop_type = peasants	size = peasant_pop_size } }
		# remove_local_variable = peasants_size
	}
}
effect_spawn_peasants = {
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = { trigger_valid_pop_spawn_state = yes }
		effect_spawn_peasants_state = yes
	}
}
effect_spawn_slaves_state = {
	if = {
		limit = { peasant_vacancy > 0 }
		# set_local_variable = { name = slaves_size value = slaves_pop_size }	# anti-lag measure
		if		= { limit = { OWNER = { any_primary_culture = { has_discrimination_trait = lusophone } } }									create_pop	= {	culture = afro_brazilian	religion = atheist	pop_type = slaves	size = slaves_pop_size } }
		else_if	= { limit = { is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_north_america }													create_pop	= {	culture = afro_american		religion = atheist	pop_type = slaves	size = slaves_pop_size } }
		else_if	= { limit = { is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india }															create_pop	= {	culture = anglo_indian		religion = atheist	pop_type = slaves	size = slaves_pop_size } }
		else	= {																															create_pop	= { culture = african			religion = atheist	pop_type = slaves	size = slaves_pop_size } }
		# remove_local_variable = slaves_size
	}
}
effect_spawn_slaves = {
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = { trigger_valid_pop_spawn_state = yes }
		effect_spawn_slaves_state = yes
	}
}
effect_spawn_slaves_colonies = {
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = {
			trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = no
			trigger_valid_pop_spawn_state = yes
		}
		effect_spawn_slaves_state = yes
		if = {
			limit = { OWNER = { has_law:law_type:law_slavery_banned } }
			liberate_slaves = yes
		}
	}
}
effect_spawn_slaves_greener_grass = {
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = {
			has_decree = decree_greener_grass_campaign
			trigger_valid_pop_spawn_state = yes
		}
		effect_spawn_slaves_state = yes
	}
}

	else_if	= { limit = { owner = { any_primary_culture = { has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_african } } }				create_pop	= {	culture = african		religion = atheist	pop_type = academics	literacy_rate = 1.0		size = 400000 } }
	else_if	= { limit = { owner = { any_primary_culture = { has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_middle_eastern } } }		create_pop	= {	culture = sephardic		religion = jewish	pop_type = academics	literacy_rate = 1.0		size = 400000 } }
	else_if	= { limit = { owner = { any_primary_culture = { has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_indigenous_oceanic } } }	create_pop	= {	culture = polynesian	religion = atheist	pop_type = academics	literacy_rate = 1.0		size = 400000 } }
	else_if	= { limit = { owner = { any_primary_culture = { has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_indigenous_american } } }	create_pop	= {	culture = siouan		religion = atheist	pop_type = academics	literacy_rate = 1.0		size = 400000 } }

effect_seed_autarky_farms_state = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_autarky_farm } } }
		start_building_construction = building_autarky_farm
	}
}
effect_seed_autarky_farms_private_state = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_autarky_farm } } }
		start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_autarky_farm
	}
}
effect_seed_autarky_greenhouses_state = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOR = { trigger_inhospitable_terrain = yes any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_autarky_greenhouse } } }
		start_building_construction = building_autarky_greenhouse
	}
}
effect_seed_autarky_greenhouses_private_state = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOR = { trigger_inhospitable_terrain = yes any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_autarky_greenhouse } } }
		start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_autarky_greenhouse
	}
}
effect_seed_autarky_farms				= { every_scope_state = { effect_seed_autarky_farms_state				= yes } }
effect_seed_autarky_farms_private		= { every_scope_state = { effect_seed_autarky_farms_private_state		= yes } }
effect_seed_autarky_greenhouses			= { every_scope_state = { effect_seed_autarky_greenhouses_state			= yes } }
effect_seed_autarky_greenhouses_private	= { every_scope_state = { effect_seed_autarky_greenhouses_private_state	= yes } }
effect_seed_infrastructure_state = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_port } } is_coastal = yes		OWNER ?= { has_technology_researched = navigation } }
		start_building_construction = building_port
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_railway } }					OWNER ?= { has_technology_researched = railways } }
		start_building_construction = building_railway
		b:building_railway = { set_subsidized = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_power_plant } }
			trigger_power_plant_allowed = yes
			OWNER ?= { has_technology_researched = electrical_generation }
		}
		start_building_construction = building_power_plant
	}
}
effect_seed_infrastructure_private_state = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_port } } is_coastal = yes		OWNER ?= { has_technology_researched = navigation } }
		start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_port
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_railway } }					OWNER ?= { has_technology_researched = railways } }
		start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_railway
		b:building_railway = { set_subsidized = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_power_plant } }
			trigger_power_plant_allowed = yes
			OWNER ?= { has_technology_researched = electrical_generation }
		}
		start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_power_plant
	}
}
effect_seed_infrastructure = {
	market_capital = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_trade_center } }
				trade_center_construction_allowed = yes
				OWNER = { OR = { has_law = law_type:law_laissez_faire has_law = law_type:law_extraction_economy } }
			}
			start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_trade_center
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_trade_center } }
				trade_center_construction_allowed = yes
			}
			start_building_construction = building_trade_center
		}
	}
	every_scope_state = {
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_government_administration } }	OWNER = { has_technology_researched = tech_bureaucracy } trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = yes }
			start_building_construction = building_government_administration
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_university } }					OWNER = { has_technology_researched = academia } trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = yes }
			start_building_construction = building_university
		}
		if = {
			limit = { OWNER = { OR = { has_law = law_type:law_laissez_faire has_law = law_type:law_extraction_economy } } }
			effect_seed_infrastructure_private_state = yes
		}
		else = { effect_seed_infrastructure_state = yes }
	}
}
effect_deplete_resources_state = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOR = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_gold_field } any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_gold_mine } }
			has_potential_resource = building_gold_mine
			OWNER = { has_technology_researched = prospecting }
		}
		force_resource_discovery = building_gold_mine
	}
	if = { limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_gold_field } } force_resource_depletion = building_gold_field }
}
effect_seed_logging = {
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_logging_camp		} }	has_potential_resource = building_logging_camp }
		start_building_construction = building_logging_camp
	}
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_rubber_plantation	} } has_potential_resource = building_rubber_plantation			OWNER = { has_technology_researched = rubber_mastication	} }
		force_resource_discovery = building_rubber_plantation
		start_building_construction = building_rubber_plantation
	}
}
effect_seed_logging_private = {
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_logging_camp		} }	has_potential_resource = building_logging_camp }
		start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_logging_camp
	}
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_rubber_plantation	} } has_potential_resource = building_rubber_plantation			OWNER = { has_technology_researched = rubber_mastication	} }
		force_resource_discovery = building_rubber_plantation
		start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_rubber_plantation
	}
}
effect_seed_resources_state = {
	effect_deplete_resources_state	= yes
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_logging_camp		} }	has_potential_resource = building_logging_camp }
		start_building_construction = building_logging_camp
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_gold_mine			} }	has_potential_resource = building_gold_mine			OWNER = { has_technology_researched = prospecting	} }
		start_building_construction = building_gold_mine
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_coal_mine			} }	has_potential_resource = building_coal_mine			OWNER = { has_technology_researched = shaft_mining	} }
		start_building_construction = building_coal_mine
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_iron_mine			} }	has_potential_resource = building_iron_mine			OWNER = { has_technology_researched = shaft_mining	} }
		start_building_construction = building_iron_mine
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_lead_mine 			} }	has_potential_resource = building_lead_mine			OWNER = { has_technology_researched = shaft_mining	} }
		start_building_construction = building_lead_mine
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_sulfur_mine		} }	has_potential_resource = building_sulfur_mine		OWNER = { has_technology_researched = shaft_mining	} }
		start_building_construction = building_sulfur_mine
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_rubber_plantation	} } has_potential_resource = building_rubber_plantation	OWNER = { has_technology_researched = rubber_mastication	} }
		force_resource_discovery = building_rubber_plantation
		start_building_construction = building_rubber_plantation
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_oil_rig			} } has_potential_resource = building_oil_rig			OWNER = { has_technology_researched = pumpjacks	} }
		force_resource_discovery = building_oil_rig
		start_building_construction = building_oil_rig
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_fishing_wharf		} }	has_potential_resource = building_fishing_wharf															is_coastal = yes }
		start_building_construction = building_fishing_wharf
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_whaling_station	} }	has_potential_resource = building_whaling_station	OWNER = { has_technology_researched = navigation }	is_coastal = yes }
		start_building_construction = building_whaling_station
	}
}

effect_seed_greenhouses_state = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_autarky_greenhouse } } }
		start_building_construction = building_autarky_greenhouse
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_cotton_greenhouse } }		trigger_cotton_greenhouse_allowed = yes }
		start_building_construction = building_cotton_greenhouse
	}
	if = {	# Crucial for states with insufficient logging camps
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_logging_greenhouse } }		trigger_logging_greenhouse_allowed = yes }
		start_building_construction = building_logging_greenhouse
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_dye_greenhouse } }			trigger_dye_greenhouse_allowed = yes }
		start_building_construction = building_dye_greenhouse
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_silk_greenhouse } }		trigger_silk_greenhouse_allowed = yes }
		start_building_construction = building_silk_greenhouse
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_sugar_greenhouse } }		trigger_sugar_greenhouse_allowed = yes }
		start_building_construction = building_sugar_greenhouse
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_banana_greenhouse } }		trigger_banana_greenhouse_allowed = yes }
		start_building_construction = building_banana_greenhouse
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_vineyard_greenhouse } }	trigger_vineyard_greenhouse_allowed = yes }
		start_building_construction = building_vineyard_greenhouse
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_coffee_greenhouse } }		trigger_coffee_greenhouse_allowed = yes }
		start_building_construction = building_coffee_greenhouse
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_tea_greenhouse } }			trigger_tea_greenhouse_allowed = yes }
		start_building_construction = building_tea_greenhouse
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_opium_greenhouse } }		trigger_opium_greenhouse_allowed = yes }
		start_building_construction = building_opium_greenhouse
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_tobacco_greenhouse } }		trigger_tobacco_greenhouse_allowed = yes }
		start_building_construction = building_tobacco_greenhouse
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_rubber_greenhouse } }		trigger_rubber_greenhouse_allowed = yes }
		start_building_construction = building_rubber_greenhouse
	}
}
effect_seed_greenhouses_private_state = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_autarky_greenhouse } } }
		start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_autarky_greenhouse

	}

	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_sugar_greenhouse } }		trigger_sugar_greenhouse_allowed = yes }
		start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_sugar_greenhouse
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_banana_greenhouse } }		trigger_banana_greenhouse_allowed = yes }
		start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_banana_greenhouse
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_vineyard_greenhouse } }	trigger_vineyard_greenhouse_allowed = yes }
		start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_vineyard_greenhouse
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_coffee_greenhouse } }		trigger_coffee_greenhouse_allowed = yes }
		start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_coffee_greenhouse
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_tea_greenhouse } }			trigger_tea_greenhouse_allowed = yes }
		start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_tea_greenhouse
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_opium_greenhouse } }		trigger_opium_greenhouse_allowed = yes }
		start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_opium_greenhouse
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_tobacco_greenhouse } }		trigger_tobacco_greenhouse_allowed = yes }
		start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_tobacco_greenhouse
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_rubber_greenhouse } }		trigger_rubber_greenhouse_allowed = yes }
		start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_rubber_greenhouse
	}
}
effect_seed_industry_state = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_tooling_workshop } }			OWNER = { has_technology_researched = manufacturies } }
		start_building_construction = building_tooling_workshop
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_glassworks } }					OWNER = { has_technology_researched = manufacturies } }
		start_building_construction = building_glassworks
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_textile_mill } }				OWNER = { has_technology_researched = manufacturies } }
		start_building_construction = building_textile_mill
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_furniture_manufactory } }	OWNER = { has_technology_researched = manufacturies } }
		start_building_construction = building_furniture_manufactory
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_paper_mill } }				OWNER = { has_technology_researched = manufacturies } }
		start_building_construction = building_paper_mill
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_food_industry } }				OWNER = { has_technology_researched = manufacturies } }
		start_building_construction = building_food_industry
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_charcoal_burner } }			OWNER = { has_technology_researched = manufacturies } }
		start_building_construction = building_charcoal_burner
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_steel_mill } }				OWNER = { has_technology_researched = steelworking } }
		start_building_construction = building_steel_mill
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_chemical_plant } }			OWNER = { has_technology_researched = distillation } }
		start_building_construction = building_chemical_plant
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_explosives_factory } }			OWNER = { has_technology_researched = distillation } }
		start_building_construction = building_explosives_factory
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_synthetic_oil_plant } }		OWNER = { has_technology_researched = distillation trigger_forbidden_heavy_industry = no } }
		start_building_construction = building_synthetic_oil_plant
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_motor_industry } }				OWNER = { has_technology_researched = atmospheric_engine trigger_forbidden_heavy_industry = no } }
		start_building_construction = building_motor_industry
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_automotive_industry } }
			OWNER = { OR = { has_technology_researched = combustion_engine has_technology_researched = electric_railway }
			trigger_forbidden_heavy_industry = no }
		}
		start_building_construction = building_automotive_industry
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_synthetics_plant } }			OWNER = { has_technology_researched = aniline trigger_forbidden_heavy_industry = no } }
		start_building_construction = building_synthetics_plant
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_electrics_industry } }			OWNER = { has_technology_researched = telephone trigger_forbidden_heavy_industry = no } }
		start_building_construction = building_electrics_industry
	}
}

effect_seed_inf_resources = {
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = { is_treaty_port = no }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_infres_iron_mine		} }	OWNER = { has_technology_researched = shaft_mining } }
			start_building_construction = building_infres_iron_mine
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_infres_lead_mine		} }	OWNER = { has_technology_researched = shaft_mining } }
			start_building_construction = building_infres_lead_mine
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_logging_greenhouse	} } }
			start_building_construction = building_logging_greenhouse
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_infres_fishing_wharf } }														is_coastal = yes }
			start_building_construction = building_infres_fishing_wharf
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_infres_whaling_station } }	OWNER = { has_technology_researched = navigation }	is_coastal = yes }
			start_building_construction = building_infres_whaling_station
		}
	}
}

effect_seed_resources = {
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = { has_decree = decree_encourage_resource_industry }
		effect_seed_resources_state = yes
	}
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = {
			trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = yes	# We don't want ownership buildings in colonies
			NOT = { has_decree = decree_encourage_resource_industry }
		}
		effect_seed_resources_state = yes
	}
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = {
			trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = no
			NOT = { has_decree = decree_encourage_resource_industry }
		}
		effect_seed_resources_state = yes
	}
}
effect_seed_resources_private = {
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = { has_decree = decree_encourage_resource_industry }
		effect_seed_resources_private_state = yes
	}
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = {
			trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = yes	# We don't want ownership buildings in colonies
			NOT = { has_decree = decree_encourage_resource_industry }
		}
		effect_seed_resources_private_state = yes
	}
}
effect_seed_greenhouses = {
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = { has_decree = decree_encourage_agricultural_industry is_treaty_port = no }
		effect_seed_greenhouses_state = yes
	}
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = {
			trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = yes	# We don't want ownership buildings in colonies
			trigger_inhospitable_terrain = no
			NOT = { has_decree = decree_encourage_agricultural_industry }
			trigger_if = { limit = { is_capital = no } NOT = { trigger_microstate = yes } }
		}
		effect_seed_greenhouses_state = yes
	}
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = {
			trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = no
			trigger_inhospitable_terrain = no
			NOT = { has_decree = decree_encourage_agricultural_industry }
			trigger_if = { limit = { is_capital = no } NOT = { trigger_microstate = yes } }
		}
		effect_seed_greenhouses_state = yes
	}
}
effect_seed_greenhouses_private = {
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = { has_decree = decree_encourage_agricultural_industry is_treaty_port = no }
		effect_seed_greenhouses_private_state = yes
	}
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = {
			trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = yes	# We don't want ownership buildings in colonies
			NOR = {
				trigger_inhospitable_terrain = yes
				has_decree = decree_encourage_agricultural_industry
			}
			trigger_if = { limit = { is_capital = no } NOT = { trigger_microstate = yes } }
		}
		effect_seed_greenhouses_private_state = yes
	}
}
effect_seed_industry = {
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = { has_decree = decree_encourage_manufacturing_industry is_treaty_port = no }
		effect_seed_industry_state = yes
	}
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = {
			trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = yes
			NOT = { has_decree = decree_encourage_manufacturing_industry }
			trigger_if = { limit = { is_capital = no } NOT = { trigger_microstate = yes } }
		}
		effect_seed_industry_state = yes
	}
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = { is_incorporated = yes NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_art_academy } }				OWNER = { has_technology_researched = romanticism } }
		start_building_construction = building_art_academy
	}
}
effect_seed_industry_private = {
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = { has_decree = decree_encourage_manufacturing_industry is_treaty_port = no }
		effect_seed_industry_private_state = yes
	}
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = {
			trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = yes
			NOT = { has_decree = decree_encourage_manufacturing_industry }
			trigger_if = { limit = { is_capital = no } NOT = { trigger_microstate = yes } }
		}
		effect_seed_industry_private_state = yes
	}
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = { is_incorporated = yes NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_art_academy } }				OWNER = { has_technology_researched = romanticism } }
		start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_art_academy
	}
}
effect_set_mil_industry_scope = {
	if = {
		limit = { THIS ?= c:USA any_scope_state = { state_region = s:STATE_NEW_YORK } }
		s:STATE_NEW_YORK.region_state:USA = {
			save_scope_as = military_industry_state
			save_scope_as = shipyards_state
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { THIS ?= c:CHI any_scope_state = { state_region = s:STATE_GUANGDONG } }
		s:STATE_GUANGDONG.region_state:CHI	= {
			save_scope_as = military_industry_state
			save_scope_as = shipyards_state
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			THIS ?= market.OWNER
			any_scope_state = {
				custom_tooltip = { text = market_capital.tt THIS ?= OWNER.market_capital }
				is_coastal = yes
			}
		}
		market_capital = {
			save_scope_as = military_industry_state
			save_scope_as = shipyards_state
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { THIS ?= market.OWNER any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_shipyard } }
		market_capital = { save_scope_as = military_industry_state }
		ordered_scope_state = { limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_shipyard } } order_by = b:building_shipyard.level_after_queued_constructions position = 0 save_scope_as = shipyards_state }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { capital = { is_coastal = yes } }
		capital = {
			save_scope_as = military_industry_state
			save_scope_as = shipyards_state
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_shipyard } }
		capital = { save_scope_as = military_industry_state }
		ordered_scope_state = { limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_shipyard } } order_by = b:building_shipyard.level_after_queued_constructions position = 0 save_scope_as = shipyards_state }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { any_scope_state = { is_coastal = yes has_decree = decree_encourage_manufacturing_industry } }
		capital = { save_scope_as = military_industry_state }
		random_scope_state = { limit = { is_coastal = yes has_decree = decree_encourage_manufacturing_industry } save_scope_as = shipyards_state }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { any_scope_state = { is_coastal = yes modifier:building_shipyard_throughput_add > OWNER.modifier:building_shipyard_throughput_add } }
		capital = { save_scope_as = military_industry_state }
		ordered_scope_state = { limit = { is_coastal = yes } order_by = modifier:building_shipyard_throughput_add position = 0 save_scope_as = shipyards_state }
	}
	else = {
		capital = { save_scope_as = military_industry_state }
		random_scope_state = { limit = { is_coastal = yes } save_scope_as = shipyards_state }
	}
}
effect_seed_military_industry = {
	effect_set_mil_industry_scope = yes
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { has_law = law_type:law_laissez_faire has_law = law_type:law_extraction_economy } }
		scope:military_industry_state = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_tooling_workshop } }						OWNER ?= { has_technology_researched = manufacturies } }
				start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_tooling_workshop
			}
		}
		scope:military_industry_state = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_paper_mill } }								OWNER ?= { has_technology_researched = manufacturies } }
				start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_paper_mill
			}
		}
		scope:shipyards_state = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_shipyard } }	is_coastal = yes			OWNER ?= { has_technology_researched = navigation } }
				start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_shipyard
			}
		}
		scope:military_industry_state = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_steel_mill } }								OWNER ?= { has_technology_researched = steelworking } }
				start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_steel_mill
			}
		}
		scope:military_industry_state = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_chemical_plant } }							OWNER ?= { has_technology_researched = distillation } }
				start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_chemical_plant
			}
		}
		scope:military_industry_state = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_explosives_factory } }						OWNER ?= { has_technology_researched = distillation } }
				start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_explosives_factory
			}
		}
		scope:military_industry_state = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_arms_industry } }							OWNER ?= { has_technology_researched = gunsmithing } }
				start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_arms_industry
			}
		}
		scope:military_industry_state = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_artillery_foundry } }						OWNER ?= { has_technology_researched = artillery } }
				start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_artillery_foundry
			}
		}
		scope:military_industry_state = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_munition_plant } }							OWNER ?= { has_technology_researched = percussion_cap	NOT = { has_law = law_type:law_industry_banned } } }
				start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_munition_plant
			}
		}
		scope:shipyards_state = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_military_shipyard } }	is_coastal = yes	OWNER ?= { has_technology_researched = navigation } }
				start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_military_shipyard
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		scope:military_industry_state = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_tooling_workshop } }						OWNER ?= { has_technology_researched = manufacturies } }
				start_building_construction = building_tooling_workshop
			}
		}
		scope:military_industry_state = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_paper_mill } }								OWNER ?= { has_technology_researched = manufacturies } }
				start_building_construction = building_paper_mill
			}
		}
		scope:shipyards_state = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_shipyard } }	is_coastal = yes			OWNER ?= { has_technology_researched = navigation } }
				start_building_construction = building_shipyard
			}
		}
		scope:military_industry_state = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_steel_mill } }								OWNER ?= { has_technology_researched = steelworking } }
				start_building_construction = building_steel_mill
			}
		}
		scope:military_industry_state = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_chemical_plant } }							OWNER ?= { has_technology_researched = distillation } }
				start_building_construction = building_chemical_plant
			}
		}
		scope:military_industry_state = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_explosives_factory } }						OWNER ?= { has_technology_researched = distillation } }
				start_building_construction = building_explosives_factory
			}
		}
		scope:military_industry_state = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_arms_industry } }							OWNER ?= { has_technology_researched = gunsmithing } }
				start_building_construction = building_arms_industry
			}
		}
		scope:military_industry_state = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_artillery_foundry } }						OWNER ?= { has_technology_researched = artillery } }
				start_building_construction = building_artillery_foundry
			}
		}
		scope:military_industry_state = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_munition_plant } }							OWNER ?= { has_technology_researched = percussion_cap	NOT = { has_law = law_type:law_industry_banned } } }
				start_building_construction = building_munition_plant
			}
		}
		scope:shipyards_state = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_military_shipyard } }	is_coastal = yes	OWNER ?= { has_technology_researched = navigation } }
				start_building_construction = building_military_shipyard
			}
		}
	}
}
