effect_remove_rubber_state = {
	if = { limit = { rubber_refund > 0 } custom_tooltip = { text = rubber_refund.tt OWNER = { add_investment_pool = PREV.rubber_refund } } }
	effect_destroy_building = { building = building_rubber_greenhouse }
}
effect_remove_rubber = {
	every_scope_state = { effect_remove_rubber_state = yes }
}
effect_remove_pp_state = {
	if = { limit = { pp_refund > 0 } custom_tooltip = { text = pp_refund.tt OWNER = { add_investment_pool = PREV.pp_refund } } }
	if = { limit = { has_building = building_power_plant }	remove_building = building_power_plant }
}
effect_remove_pp = {
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = { trigger_power_plant_allowed = no }
		effect_remove_pp_state = yes
	}
}
effect_calc_autarky_target = {
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_autarky_greenhouse	} }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_autarky_farm can_queue_building_levels = 1 } } }
			set_variable = {
				name = autarky_greenhouse_build_target
				value = {
					value		= arable_land
					min			= 100
					min			= {
						value	= state_unemployed_population
						divide	= 1000
						divide	= peasant_capacity_autarky_greenhouse
					}
					round_to	= 10
					subtract	= b:building_autarky_greenhouse.level_after_queued_constructions
					subtract = {	# Level * (1 - Occupancy)
						value		= 1
						subtract	= b:building_autarky_greenhouse.occupancy
						multiply	= b:building_autarky_greenhouse.level
					}
					min			=  0
				}
			}
		}
		else = { set_variable = { name = autarky_greenhouse_build_target value = 0 } }
		if = { limit = { var:autarky_greenhouse_build_target < 0 } remove_variable = autarky_greenhouse_build_target }
	}
}
effect_build_autarky_farms_state = {
	if = {
		limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_autarky_farm can_queue_building_levels = 1 } }
		start_building_construction = building_autarky_farm
		PREV = { change_variable = { name = counter add = 1 } }
	}
}
effect_build_autarky_farms_private_state = {
	if = {
		limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_autarky_farm can_queue_building_levels = 1 } }
		start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_autarky_farm
		PREV = { change_variable = { name = counter add = 1 } }
	}
}
effect_build_autarky_greenhouses_state = {
	if = {
		limit = { var:autarky_greenhouse_build_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_autarky_greenhouse
	}
	change_variable = { name = autarky_greenhouse_build_target subtract = 1 }
}
effect_build_autarky_greenhouses_private_state = {
	if = {
		limit = { var:autarky_greenhouse_build_target > 0 }
		start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_autarky_greenhouse
	}
	change_variable = { name = autarky_greenhouse_build_target subtract = 1 }
}
effect_build_autarky_farms = {
	hidden_effect = {
		effect_calc_autarky_target = yes
		set_variable = { name = counter value = 0 }	# Precaution to prevent game-freezes taking too long
		every_scope_state = { effect_delete_finite_agro_state = yes }
		effect_seed_autarky_farms = yes
		while = {	# Striped
			limit = {
				any_scope_state = { free_arable_land > 0 }
				var:counter < 1000
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { free_arable_land > 0 }
				effect_build_autarky_farms_state = yes
			}
		}
	}
	custom_label = lag_warning
	show_as_tooltip = {
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { free_arable_land > 0 }
			if = {
				limit = { autarky_farms_queued > 0 }
				while = {
					count = autarky_farms_queued
					start_building_construction = building_autarky_farm
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_build_autarky_farms_private = {
	hidden_effect = {
		effect_calc_autarky_target = yes
		set_variable = { name = counter value = 0 }	# Precaution to prevent game-freezes taking too long
		every_scope_state = { effect_delete_finite_agro_state = yes }
		effect_seed_autarky_farms_private = yes
		while = {	# Striped
			limit = {
				any_scope_state = { free_arable_land > 0 }
				var:counter < 1000
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						free_arable_land > 0
						any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_autarky_greenhouse	level < STATE.arable_land }
					}
				}
				effect_build_autarky_farms_private_state = yes
			}
		}
	}
	custom_label = lag_warning
	show_as_tooltip = {
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					free_arable_land > 0
					any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_autarky_greenhouse	level < STATE.arable_land }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { autarky_farms_queued > 0 }
				while = {
					count = autarky_farms_queued
					start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_autarky_farm
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_build_autarky_greenhouses = {
	hidden_effect = {
		effect_calc_autarky_target = yes
		set_variable = { name = counter value = 0 }	# Precaution to prevent game-freezes taking too long
		every_scope_state = {
			effect_delete_finite_agro_state = yes
			effect_seed_autarky_greenhouses_state = yes
		}
		while = {	# Striped
			limit = { any_scope_state = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_autarky_farm is_under_construction = no } var:autarky_greenhouse_build_target > 0 } }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_autarky_farm is_under_construction = no }
					var:autarky_greenhouse_build_target > 0
				}
				effect_build_autarky_greenhouses_state = yes
			}
		}
	}
	custom_label = lag_warning
	show_as_tooltip = {
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_autarky_greenhouse	} var:autarky_greenhouse_build_target > 0 }
			if = {
				limit = { var:autarky_greenhouse_build_target > 0 }
				while = {
					count = var:autarky_greenhouse_build_target
					start_building_construction = building_autarky_greenhouse
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

effect_build_logging = {
	show_as_tooltip = {
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { has_decree = decree_encourage_resource_industry trigger_build_logging_state = yes }
			while = {
				limit = { trigger_build_logging_state = yes }
				effect_build_logging_state = yes
			}
		}
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_decree = decree_encourage_resource_industry } trigger_build_logging_state = yes }
			while = {
				limit = { trigger_build_logging_state = yes }
				effect_build_logging_state = yes
			}
		}
	}
	hidden_effect = {
		while = {	# Striped
			limit = { any_scope_state = { has_decree = decree_encourage_resource_industry trigger_build_logging_state = yes } }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { has_decree = decree_encourage_resource_industry }	# NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_construction_sector is_under_construction = yes } }
				effect_build_logging_state = yes
			}
		}
		while = {	# Striped
			limit = { any_scope_state = { NOT = { has_decree = decree_encourage_resource_industry } trigger_build_logging_state = yes } }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_decree = decree_encourage_resource_industry } }
				effect_build_logging_state = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

			limit = { any_scope_state = { has_decree = decree_encourage_resource_industry trigger_build_fishing_state = yes } }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { has_decree = decree_encourage_resource_industry }	# NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_construction_sector is_under_construction = yes } }
				effect_build_fishing_private_state = yes
			}
		}
		while = {	# Striped
			limit = { any_scope_state = { NOT = { has_decree = decree_encourage_resource_industry } trigger_build_fishing_state = yes } }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_decree = decree_encourage_resource_industry } }
				effect_build_fishing_private_state = yes
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_build_mining_state = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_potential_resource = building_gold_mine		any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_gold_mine			can_queue_building_levels = 1 }	OWNER = { has_technology_researched = prospecting } }
		start_building_construction = building_gold_mine
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_potential_resource = building_coal_mine		any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_coal_mine			can_queue_building_levels = 1 }	OWNER = { has_technology_researched = shaft_mining } }
		start_building_construction = building_coal_mine
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_potential_resource = building_iron_mine		any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_iron_mine			can_queue_building_levels = 1 }	OWNER = { has_technology_researched = shaft_mining } }
		start_building_construction = building_iron_mine
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_potential_resource = building_lead_mine		any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_lead_mine			can_queue_building_levels = 1 }	OWNER = { has_technology_researched = shaft_mining } }
		start_building_construction = building_lead_mine
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_potential_resource = building_sulfur_mine		any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_sulfur_mine			can_queue_building_levels = 1 }	OWNER = { has_technology_researched = shaft_mining } }
		start_building_construction = building_sulfur_mine
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_potential_resource = building_oil_rig	any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_oil_rig				can_queue_building_levels = 1 }	OWNER = { has_technology_researched = pumpjacks } }
		start_building_construction = building_oil_rig
	}
}

effect_build_resources_state = {
	effect_build_logging_state			= yes
	effect_build_mining_state			= yes
	effect_build_fishing_state			= yes
}
effect_build_resources_private_state = {
	effect_build_logging_private_state	= yes
	effect_build_mining_private_state	= yes
	effect_build_fishing_private_state	= yes
}
effect_build_resources = {
	effect_seed_resources = yes
	effect_calc_railways = yes
	custom_tooltip = {
		text = effect_build_resources.tt
		while = {	# Striped
			limit = { any_scope_state = { has_decree = decree_encourage_resource_industry trigger_build_resources_state = yes } }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { has_decree = decree_encourage_resource_industry }	# NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_construction_sector is_under_construction = yes } }
				effect_build_resources_state = yes
				effect_build_railways_state = yes
			}
		}
		while = {	# Striped
			limit = { any_scope_state = { NOT = { has_decree = decree_encourage_resource_industry } trigger_build_resources_state = yes } }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_decree = decree_encourage_resource_industry } }
				effect_build_resources_state = yes
				effect_build_railways_state = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

	effect_destroy_building = { building = building_millet_farm }
	effect_destroy_building = { building = building_livestock_ranch }
}

effect_refund_greenhouses_state = {
	if = {
		limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_vineyard_greenhouse } }
		remove_building = building_vineyard_greenhouse
	}
	if = {
		limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_coffee_greenhouse } }
		remove_building = building_coffee_greenhouse
	}
	if = {
		limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_cotton_greenhouse } }
		remove_building = building_cotton_greenhouse
	}
	if = {
		limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_opium_greenhouse } }
		remove_building = building_opium_greenhouse
	}
	if = {
		limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_tea_greenhouse } }
		remove_building = building_tea_greenhouse
	}
	if = {
		limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_tobacco_greenhouse } }
		remove_building = building_tobacco_greenhouse
	}
	if = {
		limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_sugar_greenhouse } }
		remove_building = building_sugar_greenhouse
	}
	if = {
		limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_banana_greenhouse } }
		remove_building = building_banana_greenhouse
	}
	if = {
		limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_silk_greenhouse } }
		remove_building = building_silk_greenhouse
	}
	if = {
		limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_logging_greenhouse } }
		remove_building = building_logging_greenhouse
	}
	if = {
		limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_rubber_greenhouse } }
		remove_building = building_rubber_greenhouse
	}
	if = { limit = { greenhouse_refund > 0 } custom_tooltip = { text = greenhouse_refund.tt OWNER = { add_investment_pool = PREV.greenhouse_refund } } }
	hidden_effect = {
		remove_building = building_vineyard_greenhouse
		remove_building = building_coffee_greenhouse
		remove_building = building_cotton_greenhouse
		remove_building = building_opium_greenhouse
		remove_building = building_tea_greenhouse
		remove_building = building_tobacco_greenhouse
		remove_building = building_sugar_greenhouse
		remove_building = building_banana_greenhouse
		remove_building = building_silk_greenhouse
		remove_building = building_logging_greenhouse
		remove_building = building_rubber_greenhouse
	}
}
effect_delete_inhospitable = {
	if = {
		limit = { any_scope_state = { count > 2 } }
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { trigger_inhospitable_terrain = yes }
			effect_refund_greenhouses_state = yes
		}
	}
}
effect_calc_mil_refund = {
	set_variable = {
		name = mil_refund
		value = {
			value = 0
			if = {
				limit = { has_building = building_arms_industry }
				add = {
					value = b:building_arms_industry.level
					multiply = construction_cost_high
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_building = building_artillery_foundry }
				add = {
					value = b:building_artillery_foundry.level
					multiply = construction_cost_high
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_building = building_munition_plant }
				add = {
					value = b:building_munition_plant.level
					multiply = construction_cost_very_high
				}
			}
			multiply = 1000	# 1k/point
		}
	}
}
effect_calc_ship_refund = {
	set_variable = {
		name = ship_refund
		value = {
			value = 0
			if = {
				limit = { has_building = building_shipyard }
				add = {
					value = b:building_shipyard.level
					multiply = construction_cost_high
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_building = building_military_shipyard }
				add = {
					value = b:building_military_shipyard.level
					multiply = construction_cost_high
				}
			}
			multiply = 1000	# 1k/point
		}
	}
}
effect_delete_mil_non_capital = {
	effect_set_mil_industry_scope = yes
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = { NOT = { THIS ?= scope:military_industry_state } }
		show_as_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_arms_industry } }
				remove_building = building_arms_industry
			}
			if = {
				limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_artillery_foundry } }
				remove_building = building_artillery_foundry
			}
			if = {
				limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_munition_plant } }
				remove_building = building_munition_plant
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			remove_building = building_arms_industry
			remove_building = building_artillery_foundry
			remove_building = building_munition_plant
		}
		if = { limit = { var:mil_refund > 0 } custom_tooltip = { text = mil_refund.tt OWNER = { add_investment_pool = PREV.var:mil_refund } } }
		remove_variable = mil_refund
	}
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = { NOT = { THIS ?= scope:shipyards_state } }
		show_as_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_shipyard } }
				remove_building = building_shipyard
			}
			if = {
				limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_military_shipyard } }
				remove_building = building_military_shipyard
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			remove_building = building_shipyard
			remove_building = building_military_shipyard
		}
		if = { limit = { var:ship_refund > 0 } custom_tooltip = { text = ship_refund.tt OWNER = { add_investment_pool = PREV.var:ship_refund } } }
		remove_variable = ship_refund
	}
}
effect_refund_industry_state = {
	effect_calc_mil_refund	= yes
	effect_calc_ship_refund = yes
	show_as_tooltip = {
		if = {
			limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_food_industry } }
			remove_building = building_food_industry
		}
		if = {
			limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_textile_mill } }
			remove_building = building_textile_mill
		}
		if = {
			limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_furniture_manufactory } }
			remove_building = building_furniture_manufactory
		}
		if = {
			limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_glassworks } }
			remove_building = building_glassworks
		}
		if = {
			limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_tooling_workshop } }
			remove_building = building_tooling_workshop
		}
		if = {
			limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_paper_mill } }
			remove_building = building_paper_mill
		}
		if = {
			limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_chemical_plant } }
			remove_building = building_chemical_plant
		}
		if = {
			limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_explosives_factory } }
			remove_building = building_explosives_factory
		}
		if = {
			limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_synthetics_plant } }
			remove_building = building_synthetics_plant
		}
		if = {
			limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_steel_mill } }
			remove_building = building_steel_mill
		}
		if = {
			limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_motor_industry } }
			remove_building = building_motor_industry
		}
		if = {
			limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_automotive_industry } }
			remove_building = building_automotive_industry
		}
		if = {
			limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_electrics_industry } }
			remove_building = building_electrics_industry
		}
		if = {
			limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_shipyard } }
			remove_building = building_shipyard
		}
		if = {
			limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_military_shipyard } }
			remove_building = building_military_shipyard
		}
		if = {
			limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_arms_industry } }
			remove_building = building_arms_industry
		}
		if = {
			limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_artillery_foundry } }
			remove_building = building_artillery_foundry
		}
		if = {
			limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_munition_plant } }
			remove_building = building_munition_plant
		}
		if = {
			limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_synthetic_oil_plant } }
			remove_building = building_synthetic_oil_plant
		}
		if = {
			limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_charcoal_burner } }
			remove_building = building_charcoal_burner
		}
		if = {
			limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_art_academy } }
			remove_building = building_art_academy
		}
		if = {
			limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_trade_center } is_treaty_port = no }
			remove_building = building_trade_center
		}
	}
	hidden_effect = {	# Remove anyways to prevent ghost buildings
		remove_building = building_food_industry
		remove_building = building_textile_mill
		remove_building = building_furniture_manufactory
		remove_building = building_glassworks
		remove_building = building_tooling_workshop
		remove_building = building_paper_mill
		remove_building = building_chemical_plant
		remove_building = building_explosives_factory
		remove_building = building_synthetics_plant
		remove_building = building_steel_mill
		remove_building = building_motor_industry
		remove_building = building_automotive_industry
		remove_building = building_electrics_industry
		remove_building = building_shipyard
		remove_building = building_military_shipyard
		remove_building = building_arms_industry
		remove_building = building_artillery_foundry
		remove_building = building_munition_plant
		remove_building = building_synthetic_oil_plant
		remove_building = building_charcoal_burner
		remove_building = building_art_academy
		if = { limit = { is_treaty_port = no } remove_building = building_trade_center }
	}
	if = { limit = { ind_refund > 0 } custom_tooltip = { text = ind_refund.tt OWNER = { add_investment_pool = PREV.ind_refund } } }
	if = { limit = { var:mil_refund > 0 } custom_tooltip = { text = mil_refund.tt OWNER = { add_investment_pool = PREV.var:mil_refund } } }
	remove_variable = mil_refund
	if = { limit = { var:ship_refund > 0 } custom_tooltip = { text = ship_refund.tt OWNER = { add_investment_pool = PREV.var:ship_refund } } }
	remove_variable = ship_refund
}
effect_delete_unincorporated_industry = {
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = { trigger_delete_unincorporated_industry = yes }
		effect_refund_industry_state = yes
	}
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = { is_under_colonization = yes }
		if = { limit = { has_building = building_port } save_temporary_scope_value_as = { name = port_level value = b:building_port.level } }
		remove_building = building_port
		create_building = { building = building_port level = scope:port_level }
	}
}
effect_delete_trade_state = {
	if = { limit = { trade_refund > 0 } custom_tooltip = { text = trade_refund.tt OWNER ?= { add_investment_pool = PREV.trade_refund } } }
	effect_destroy_building = { building = building_trade_center }
}
effect_delete_invalid_trade = {
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = {
			trigger_invalid_trade_center = yes
			any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_trade_center }
		}
		effect_delete_trade_state = yes
	}
}
# Industry
effect_calc_industry_target = {
	if = { limit = { NOT = { exists = $target_value$ } } target_value = OWNER.economy_of_scale_target }
	every_scope_state = {
		remove_variable = food_target
		remove_variable = textile_target
		remove_variable = furniture_target
		remove_variable = glass_target
		remove_variable = tools_target
		remove_variable = paper_target
		remove_variable = fertilizer_target
		remove_variable = explosives_target
		remove_variable = synth_dye_target
		remove_variable = steel_target
		remove_variable = motor_target
		remove_variable = automotive_target
		remove_variable = electronics_target
		remove_variable = charcoal_target
		remove_variable = synth_oil_target
		remove_variable = arts_target
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_technology_researched = manufacturies }
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = yes }
			set_variable = {
				name = food_target
				value = {
					value = $target_value$
					subtract = b:building_food_industry.level
					if = {
						limit = { b:building_food_industry = { is_under_construction = yes } }
						subtract = {
							value		= b:building_food_industry.level_after_queued_constructions
							subtract	= b:building_food_industry.level
						}
					}
					min = 0
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = textile_target
				value = {
					value = $target_value$
					subtract = b:building_textile_mill.level
					if = {
						limit = { b:building_textile_mill = { is_under_construction = yes } }
						subtract = {
							value		= b:building_textile_mill.level_after_queued_constructions
							subtract	= b:building_textile_mill.level
						}
					}
					min = 0
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = furniture_target
				value = {
					value = $target_value$
					subtract = b:building_furniture_manufactory.level
					if = {
						limit = { b:building_furniture_manufactory = { is_under_construction = yes } }
						subtract = {
							value		= b:building_furniture_manufactory.level_after_queued_constructions
							subtract	= b:building_furniture_manufactory.level
						}
					}
					min = 0
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = glass_target
				value = {
					value = $target_value$
					subtract = b:building_glassworks.level
					if = {
						limit = { b:building_glassworks = { is_under_construction = yes } }
						subtract = {
							value		= b:building_glassworks.level_after_queued_constructions
							subtract	= b:building_glassworks.level
						}
					}
					min = 0
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = tools_target
				value = {
					value = $target_value$
					subtract = b:building_tooling_workshop.level
					if = {
						limit = { b:building_tooling_workshop = { is_under_construction = yes } }
						subtract = {
							value		= b:building_tooling_workshop.level_after_queued_constructions
							subtract	= b:building_tooling_workshop.level
						}
					}
					min = 0
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = paper_target
				value = {
					value = $target_value$
					subtract = b:building_paper_mill.level
					if = {
						limit = { b:building_paper_mill = { is_under_construction = yes } }
						subtract = {
							value		= b:building_paper_mill.level_after_queued_constructions
							subtract	= b:building_paper_mill.level
						}
					}
					min = 0
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { OWNER = { has_technology_researched = distillation } }
				set_variable = {	# intensive_agriculture
					name = fertilizer_target
					value = {
						value = $target_value$
						subtract = b:building_chemical_plant.level
						if = {
							limit = { b:building_chemical_plant = { is_under_construction = yes } }
							subtract = {
								value		= b:building_chemical_plant.level_after_queued_constructions
								subtract	= b:building_chemical_plant.level
							}
						}
						min = 0
					}
				}
				set_variable = {	# intensive_agriculture
					name = explosives_target
					value = {
						value = $target_value$
						subtract = b:building_explosives_factory.level
						if = {
							limit = { b:building_explosives_factory = { is_under_construction = yes } }
							subtract = {
								value		= b:building_explosives_factory.level_after_queued_constructions
								subtract	= b:building_explosives_factory.level
							}
						}
						min = 0
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { OWNER = { has_technology_researched = steelworking } }
				set_variable = {
					name = steel_target
					value = {
						value = $target_value$
						subtract = b:building_steel_mill.level
						if = {
							limit = { b:building_steel_mill = { is_under_construction = yes } }
							subtract = {
								value		= b:building_steel_mill.level_after_queued_constructions
								subtract	= b:building_steel_mill.level
							}
						}
						min = 0
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { OWNER = { has_technology_researched = atmospheric_engine	trigger_forbidden_heavy_industry = no } }
				set_variable = {
					name = motor_target
					value = {
						value = $target_value$
						subtract = b:building_motor_industry.level
						if = {
							limit = { b:building_motor_industry = { is_under_construction = yes } }
							subtract = {
								value		= b:building_motor_industry.level_after_queued_constructions
								subtract	= b:building_motor_industry.level
							}
						}
						min = 0
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { OWNER = { OR = { has_technology_researched = combustion_engine has_technology_researched = electric_railway }	trigger_forbidden_heavy_industry = no } }
				set_variable = {
					name = automotive_target
					value = {
						value = $target_value$
						subtract = b:building_automotive_industry.level
						if = {
							limit = { b:building_automotive_industry = { is_under_construction = yes } }
							subtract = {
								value		= b:building_automotive_industry.level_after_queued_constructions
								subtract	= b:building_automotive_industry.level
							}
						}
						min = 0
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { OWNER = { has_technology_researched = aniline				trigger_forbidden_heavy_industry = no } }
				set_variable = {
					name = synth_dye_target
					value = {
						value = $target_value$
						subtract = b:building_synthetics_plant.level
						if = {
							limit = { b:building_synthetics_plant = { is_under_construction = yes } }
							subtract = {
								value		= b:building_synthetics_plant.level_after_queued_constructions
								subtract	= b:building_synthetics_plant.level
							}
						}
						min = 0
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { OWNER = { has_technology_researched = telephone			trigger_forbidden_heavy_industry = no } }
				set_variable = {
					name = electronics_target
					value = {
						value = $target_value$
						subtract = b:building_electrics_industry.level
						if = {
							limit = { b:building_electrics_industry = { is_under_construction = yes } }
							subtract = {
								value		= b:building_electrics_industry.level_after_queued_constructions
								subtract	= b:building_electrics_industry.level
							}
						}
						min = 0
					}
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = charcoal_target
				value = {
					value = $target_value$
					subtract = b:building_charcoal_burner.level
					if = {
						limit = { b:building_charcoal_burner = { is_under_construction = yes } }
						subtract = {
							value		= b:building_charcoal_burner.level_after_queued_constructions
							subtract	= b:building_charcoal_burner.level
						}
					}
					min = 0
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { OWNER = { has_technology_researched = distillation trigger_forbidden_heavy_industry = no } }
				set_variable = {
					name = synth_oil_target
					value = {
						value = $target_value$
						subtract = b:building_synthetic_oil_plant.level
						if = {
							limit = { b:building_synthetic_oil_plant = { is_under_construction = yes } }
							subtract = {
								value		= b:building_synthetic_oil_plant.level_after_queued_constructions
								subtract	= b:building_synthetic_oil_plant.level
							}
						}
						min = 0
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_incorporated = yes OWNER = { has_technology_researched = romanticism } }
				set_variable = {
					name = arts_target
					value = {
						value = $target_value$
						subtract = b:building_art_academy.level
						if = {
							limit = { b:building_art_academy = { is_under_construction = yes } }
							subtract = {
								value		= b:building_art_academy.level_after_queued_constructions
								subtract	= b:building_art_academy.level
							}
						}
						min = 0
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_build_industry_state = {
	if = {
		limit = { var:tools_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_tooling_workshop
		change_variable = { name = tools_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:glass_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_glassworks
		change_variable = { name = glass_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:textile_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_textile_mill
		change_variable = { name = textile_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:furniture_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_furniture_manufactory
		change_variable = { name = furniture_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:paper_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_paper_mill
		change_variable = { name = paper_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:food_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_food_industry
		change_variable = { name = food_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:charcoal_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_charcoal_burner
		change_variable = { name = charcoal_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:steel_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_steel_mill
		change_variable = { name = steel_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:fertilizer_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_chemical_plant
		change_variable = { name = fertilizer_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:explosives_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_explosives_factory
		change_variable = { name = explosives_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:synth_oil_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_synthetic_oil_plant
		change_variable = { name = synth_oil_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:motor_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_motor_industry
		change_variable = { name = motor_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:automotive_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_automotive_industry
		change_variable = { name = automotive_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:synth_dye_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_synthetics_plant
		change_variable = { name = synth_dye_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:electronics_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_electrics_industry
		change_variable = { name = electronics_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:arts_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_art_academy
		change_variable = { name = arts_target subtract = 1 }
	}
}

		every_scope_state = {
			if = {
				limit = { var:tools_target > 0 }
				while = { count = var:tools_target			start_building_construction = building_tooling_workshop }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { var:glass_target > 0 }
				while = { count = var:glass_target			start_building_construction = building_glassworks }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { var:textile_target > 0 }
				while = { count = var:textile_target		start_building_construction = building_textile_mill }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { var:furniture_target > 0 }
				while = { count = var:furniture_target		start_building_construction = building_furniture_manufactory }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { var:paper_target > 0 }
				while = { count = var:paper_target			start_building_construction = building_paper_mill }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { var:food_target > 0 }
				while = { count = var:food_target			start_building_construction = building_food_industry }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { var:charcoal_target > 0 }
				while = { count = var:charcoal_target		start_building_construction = building_charcoal_burner }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { var:steel_target > 0 }
				while = { count = var:steel_target			start_building_construction = building_steel_mill }
			}
			if = {

			}
			if = {

			}

				while = { count = var:motor_target 			start_building_construction = building_motor_industry }
			}

				while = { count = var:automotive_target		start_building_construction = building_automotive_industry }

			if = {

				while = { count = var:electronics_target	start_building_construction = building_electrics_industry }

				limit = { var:arts_target > 0 }
				while = { count = var:arts_target			start_building_construction = building_art_academy }
			}
		}
	}
}

effect_build_industry_gov = {
	if = {
		limit = { any_scope_state = { count > 2 } }
		set_variable = { name = counter value = 0 }	# Precaution to prevent game-freezes taking too long
		while = {	# Striped
			limit = { any_scope_state = { has_decree = decree_encourage_manufacturing_industry trigger_build_industry_state = yes } var:counter < 1000 }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { has_decree = decree_encourage_manufacturing_industry }
				effect_build_industry_state = yes
				PREV = { change_variable = { name = counter add = 1 } }
			}
		}
		while = {	# Striped
			limit = { any_scope_state = { NOT = { has_decree = decree_encourage_manufacturing_industry } trigger_build_industry_state = yes } var:counter < 1000 }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = yes
					NOT = { has_decree = decree_encourage_manufacturing_industry }
					trigger_if = { limit = { is_capital = no } trigger_microstate = no }
				}
				effect_build_industry_state = yes
				PREV = { change_variable = { name = counter add = 1 } }
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		while = {	# Striped
			limit = { any_scope_state = { trigger_build_industry_state = yes } }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = yes
					trigger_if = { limit = { is_capital = no } trigger_microstate = no }
				}
				effect_build_industry_state = yes
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_build_industry_ivp = {
	if = {
		limit = { any_scope_state = { count > 2 } }
		set_variable = { name = counter value = 0 }	# Precaution to prevent game-freezes taking too long
		while = {	# Striped
			limit = { any_scope_state = { has_decree = decree_encourage_manufacturing_industry trigger_build_industry_state = yes } var:counter < 1000 }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { has_decree = decree_encourage_manufacturing_industry }
				effect_build_industry_private_state = yes
				PREV = { change_variable = { name = counter add = 1 } }
			}
		}
		while = {	# Striped
			limit = { any_scope_state = { NOT = { has_decree = decree_encourage_manufacturing_industry } trigger_build_industry_state = yes } var:counter < 1000 }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = yes
					NOT = { has_decree = decree_encourage_manufacturing_industry }
					trigger_if = { limit = { is_capital = no } NOT = { trigger_microstate = yes } }
				}
				effect_build_industry_private_state = yes
				PREV = { change_variable = { name = counter add = 1 } }
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		while = {	# Striped
			limit = { any_scope_state = { trigger_build_industry_state = yes } }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = yes
					trigger_if = { limit = { is_capital = no } NOT = { trigger_microstate = yes } }
				}
				effect_build_industry_private_state = yes
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_build_industry = {			# VERY HEAVY - EXECUTE WITH CAUTION
	effect_seed_industry			= yes
	effect_calc_industry_target		= yes
	custom_tooltip = {
		text = effect_build_industry.tt
		effect_build_industry_gov = yes
	}
	effect_build_industry_tooltip = yes
}
effect_build_industry_private = {	# VERY HEAVY - EXECUTE WITH CAUTION
	effect_seed_industry_private	= yes
	effect_calc_industry_target		= yes
	custom_tooltip = {
		text = effect_build_industry.tt
		effect_build_industry_ivp = yes
	}
	effect_build_industry_private_tooltip = yes
}

# Plantations
effect_calc_greenhouses_target = {	# country scope
	if = { limit = { NOT = { exists = $target_value$ } } target_value = economy_of_scale_target }
	every_scope_state = {
		remove_variable = agro_target
		remove_variable = sugar_target
		remove_variable = fruit_target
		remove_variable = wine_target
		remove_variable = coffee_target
		remove_variable = cotton_target
		remove_variable = tea_target
		remove_variable = dye_target
		remove_variable = silk_target
		remove_variable = opium_target
		remove_variable = tobacco_target
		remove_variable = logging_target
		remove_variable = rubber_target
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_technology_researched = urban_planning }
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				trigger_if = { limit = { OWNER = { any_scope_state = { count > 2 } } } trigger_inhospitable_terrain = no }
				is_treaty_port = no
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = agro_target
				value = {
					value = $target_value$
					subtract = b:building_autarky_greenhouse.level_after_queued_constructions
					min = 0
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { trigger_sugar_greenhouse_allowed = yes }
				set_variable = {
					name = sugar_target
					value = {
						value = $target_value$
						subtract = b:building_sugar_greenhouse.level_after_queued_constructions
						min = 0
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { trigger_banana_greenhouse_allowed = yes }
				set_variable = {
					name = fruit_target
					value = {
						value = $target_value$
						subtract = b:building_banana_greenhouse.level_after_queued_constructions
						min = 0
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { trigger_vineyard_greenhouse_allowed = yes }
				set_variable = {
					name = wine_target
					value = {
						value = $target_value$
						subtract = b:building_vineyard_greenhouse.level_after_queued_constructions
						min = 0
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { trigger_coffee_greenhouse_allowed = yes }
				set_variable = {
					name = coffee_target
					value = {
						value = $target_value$
						subtract = b:building_coffee_greenhouse.level_after_queued_constructions
						min = 0
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { trigger_cotton_greenhouse_allowed = yes }
				set_variable = {
					name = cotton_target
					value = {
						value = $target_value$
						subtract = b:building_cotton_greenhouse.level_after_queued_constructions
						min = 0
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { trigger_logging_greenhouse_allowed = yes }
				set_variable = {
					name = logging_target
					value = {
						value = $target_value$
						subtract = b:building_logging_greenhouse.level_after_queued_constructions
						min = 0
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { trigger_rubber_greenhouse_allowed = yes }
				set_variable = {
					name = rubber_target
					value = {
						value = $target_value$
						subtract = b:building_rubber_greenhouse.level_after_queued_constructions
						min = 0
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { trigger_tea_greenhouse_allowed = yes }
				set_variable = {
					name = tea_target
					value = {
						value = $target_value$
						subtract = b:building_tea_greenhouse.level_after_queued_constructions
						min = 0
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { trigger_dye_greenhouse_allowed = yes }
				set_variable = {
					name = dye_target
					value = {
						value = $target_value$
						subtract = b:building_dye_greenhouse.level_after_queued_constructions
						min = 0
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { trigger_silk_greenhouse_allowed = yes }
				set_variable = {
					name = silk_target
					value = {
						value = $target_value$
						subtract = b:building_silk_greenhouse.level_after_queued_constructions
						min = 0
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { trigger_opium_greenhouse_allowed = yes }
				set_variable = {
					name = opium_target
					value = {
						value = $target_value$
						subtract = b:building_opium_greenhouse.level_after_queued_constructions
						min = 0
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { trigger_tobacco_greenhouse_allowed = yes }
				set_variable = {
					name = tobacco_target
					value = {
						value = $target_value$
						subtract = b:building_tobacco_greenhouse.level_after_queued_constructions
						min = 0
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_build_greenhouses_state = {
	if = {
		limit = { var:agro_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_autarky_greenhouse
		change_variable = { name = agro_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:sugar_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_sugar_greenhouse
		change_variable = { name = sugar_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:fruit_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_banana_greenhouse
		change_variable = { name = fruit_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:wine_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_vineyard_greenhouse
		change_variable = { name = wine_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:coffee_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_coffee_greenhouse
		change_variable = { name = coffee_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:cotton_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_cotton_greenhouse
		change_variable = { name = cotton_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:tea_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_tea_greenhouse
		change_variable = { name = tea_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:dye_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_dye_greenhouse
		change_variable = { name = dye_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:silk_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_silk_greenhouse
		change_variable = { name = silk_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:opium_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_opium_greenhouse
		change_variable = { name = opium_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:tobacco_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_tobacco_greenhouse
		change_variable = { name = tobacco_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:logging_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_logging_greenhouse
		change_variable = { name = logging_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:rubber_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_rubber_greenhouse
		change_variable = { name = rubber_target subtract = 1 }
	}
}

effect_build_greenhouses_tooltip = {
	show_as_tooltip = {
		every_scope_state = {
			if = {
				limit = { var:agro_target > 0 }
				while = { count = var:agro_target				start_building_construction = building_autarky_greenhouse }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { var:wine_target > 0 }
				while = { count = var:wine_target				start_building_construction = building_vineyard_greenhouse }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { var:coffee_target > 0 }
				while = { count = var:coffee_target				start_building_construction = building_coffee_greenhouse }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { var:cotton_target > 0 }
				while = { count = var:cotton_target				start_building_construction = building_cotton_greenhouse }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { var:tea_target > 0 }
				while = { count = var:tea_target				start_building_construction = building_tea_greenhouse }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { var:sugar_target > 0 }
				while = { count = var:sugar_target				start_building_construction = building_sugar_greenhouse }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { var:fruit_target > 0 }
				while = { count = var:fruit_target				start_building_construction = building_banana_greenhouse }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { var:dye_target > 0 }

			if = {

effect_build_greenhouses_gov = {
	if = {
		limit = { any_scope_state = { count > 2 } }
		set_variable = { name = counter value = 0 }	# Precaution to prevent game-freezes taking too long
		while = {	# Striped
			limit = { any_scope_state = { has_decree = decree_encourage_agricultural_industry trigger_build_greenhouses_state = yes } var:counter < 1000 }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { has_decree = decree_encourage_agricultural_industry }	# NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_construction_sector is_under_construction = yes } }
				effect_build_greenhouses_state = yes
				PREV = { change_variable = { name = counter add = 1 } }
			}
		}
		while = {	# Striped
			limit = { any_scope_state = { NOT = { has_decree = decree_encourage_agricultural_industry } trigger_build_greenhouses_state = yes } var:counter < 1000 }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					trigger_inhospitable_terrain = no
					NOT = { has_decree = decree_encourage_agricultural_industry }
					trigger_microstate = no
				}
				effect_build_greenhouses_state = yes
				PREV = { change_variable = { name = counter add = 1 } }
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		while = {	# Striped
			limit = { any_scope_state = { trigger_build_greenhouses_state = yes } }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					# trigger_inhospitable_terrain = no	# If you only have one or two states, it doesn't matter.
					# trigger_microstate = no
				}
				effect_build_greenhouses_state = yes
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_build_greenhouses_ivp = {
	if = {
		limit = { any_scope_state = { count > 2 } }
		set_variable = { name = counter value = 0 }	# Precaution to prevent game-freezes taking too long
		while = {	# Striped
			limit = { any_scope_state = { has_decree = decree_encourage_agricultural_industry trigger_build_greenhouses_state = yes } var:counter < 1000 }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { has_decree = decree_encourage_agricultural_industry }	# NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_construction_sector is_under_construction = yes } }
				effect_build_greenhouses_private_state = yes
				PREV = { change_variable = { name = counter add = 1 } }
			}
		}
		while = {	# Striped
			limit = { any_scope_state = { NOT = { has_decree = decree_encourage_agricultural_industry } trigger_build_greenhouses_state = yes } var:counter < 1000 }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = yes	# We don't want ownership buildings in colonies
					trigger_inhospitable_terrain = no
					NOT = { has_decree = decree_encourage_agricultural_industry }
					trigger_microstate = no
				}
				effect_build_greenhouses_private_state = yes
				PREV = { change_variable = { name = counter add = 1 } }
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		while = {	# Striped
			limit = { any_scope_state = { trigger_build_greenhouses_state = yes } }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					# trigger_inhospitable_terrain = no	# If you only have one or two states, it doesn't matter.
					# trigger_microstate = no
				}
				effect_build_greenhouses_private_state = yes
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_build_greenhouses = {	# VERY HEAVY - EXECUTE WITH CAUTION
	effect_seed_greenhouses			= yes
	effect_calc_greenhouses_target	= yes
	custom_tooltip = {
		text = effect_build_greenhouses.tt
		effect_build_greenhouses_gov = yes
	}
	effect_build_greenhouses_tooltip = yes
}
effect_build_greenhouses_private = {	# VERY HEAVY - EXECUTE WITH CAUTION
	effect_seed_greenhouses			= yes
	effect_calc_greenhouses_target	= yes
	custom_tooltip = {
		text = effect_build_greenhouses.tt
		effect_build_greenhouses_ivp = yes
	}
	effect_build_greenhouses_private_tooltip = yes
}

# Resources
effect_calc_inf_resources_target = {
	if = { limit = { NOT = { exists = $target_value$ } } target_value = OWNER.economy_of_scale_target }
	every_scope_state = {
		remove_variable = inf_wood_target
		remove_variable = inf_iron_target
		remove_variable = inf_lead_target
		remove_variable = inf_fish_target
		remove_variable = inf_whale_target
	}
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = { NOT = { is_treaty_port = yes } }
		set_variable = {
			name = inf_wood_target
			value = {
				value = $target_value$
				subtract = b:building_logging_greenhouse.level
				if = { limit = { b:building_logging_greenhouse = { is_under_construction = yes } } subtract = 1 }
				min = 0
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { OWNER = { has_technology_researched = shaft_mining } }
			set_variable = {
				name = inf_iron_target
				value = {
					value = $target_value$
					subtract = b:building_infres_iron_mine.level
					if = { limit = { b:building_infres_iron_mine = { is_under_construction = yes } } subtract = 1 }
					min = 0
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = inf_lead_target
				value = {
					value = $target_value$
					subtract = b:building_infres_lead_mine.level
					if = { limit = { b:building_infres_lead_mine = { is_under_construction = yes } } subtract = 1 }
					min = 0
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_sea_adjacent = yes }
			set_variable = {
				name = inf_fish_target
				value = {
					value = $target_value$
					subtract = b:building_infres_fishing_wharf.level
					if = { limit = { b:building_infres_fishing_wharf = { is_under_construction = yes } } subtract = 1 }
					min = 0
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { OWNER = { has_technology_researched = navigation } }
				set_variable = {
					name = inf_whale_target
					value = {
						value = $target_value$
						subtract = b:building_infres_whaling_station.level
						if = { limit = { b:building_infres_whaling_station = { is_under_construction = yes } } subtract = 1 }
						min = 0
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_build_inf_resources_state = {
	if = {
		limit = { var:inf_wood_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_logging_greenhouse
		change_variable = { name = inf_wood_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:inf_iron_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_infres_iron_mine
		change_variable = { name = inf_iron_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:inf_lead_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_infres_lead_mine
		change_variable = { name = inf_lead_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:inf_fish_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_infres_fishing_wharf
		change_variable = { name = inf_fish_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:inf_whale_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_infres_whaling_station
		change_variable = { name = inf_whale_target subtract = 1 }
	}
}

effect_build_inf_resources_gov = {
	if = {
		limit = { any_scope_state = { count > 2 } }
		set_variable = { name = counter value = 0 }	# Precaution to prevent game-freezes taking too long
		while = {	# Striped
			limit = { any_scope_state = { has_decree = decree_encourage_resource_industry trigger_build_inf_resources_state = yes } var:counter < 1000 }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { has_decree = decree_encourage_resource_industry }	# NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_construction_sector is_under_construction = yes } }
				effect_build_inf_resources_state = yes
				PREV = { change_variable = { name = counter add = 1 } }
			}
		}
		while = {	# Striped
			limit = { any_scope_state = { NOT = { has_decree = decree_encourage_resource_industry } trigger_build_inf_resources_state = yes } var:counter < 1000 }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					NAND = {
						has_decree = decree_encourage_resource_industry
						trigger_microstate = yes
					}
				}
				effect_build_inf_resources_state = yes
				PREV = { change_variable = { name = counter add = 1 } }
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		while = {	# Striped
			limit = { any_scope_state = { trigger_build_inf_resources_state = yes } }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { trigger_if = { limit = { is_capital = no } NOT = { trigger_microstate = yes } } }
				effect_build_inf_resources_state = yes
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_build_inf_resources_ivp = {
	if = {
		limit = { any_scope_state = { count > 2 } }
		set_variable = { name = counter value = 0 }	# Precaution to prevent game-freezes taking too long
		while = {	# Striped
			limit = { any_scope_state = { has_decree = decree_encourage_resource_industry trigger_build_inf_resources_state = yes } var:counter < 1000 }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { has_decree = decree_encourage_resource_industry }	# NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_construction_sector is_under_construction = yes } }
				effect_build_inf_resources_private_state = yes
				PREV = { change_variable = { name = counter add = 1 } }
			}
		}
		while = {	# Striped
			limit = { any_scope_state = { NOT = { has_decree = decree_encourage_resource_industry } trigger_build_inf_resources_state = yes } var:counter < 1000 }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					NAND = {
						has_decree = decree_encourage_resource_industry
						trigger_microstate = yes
					}
				}
				effect_build_inf_resources_private_state = yes
				PREV = { change_variable = { name = counter add = 1 } }
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		while = {	# Striped
			limit = { any_scope_state = { trigger_build_inf_resources_state = yes } }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { trigger_if = { limit = { is_capital = no } NOT = { trigger_microstate = yes } } }
				effect_build_inf_resources_private_state = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

		effect_build_inf_resources_gov	= yes

effect_calc_mil_industry = {	# This one does not delete buildings, so you can fire it as many times as you want.
	set_variable = { name = arms_level	value = 0 }
	set_variable = { name = arty_level	value = 0 }
	set_variable = { name = ammo_level	value = 0 }
	set_variable = { name = ship_level	value = 0 }
	set_variable = { name = wship_level	value = 0 }
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = { NOT = { THIS ?= scope:military_industry_state } }
		if = {
			limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_arms_industry } }
			OWNER = { change_variable = { name = arms_level		add = PREV.b:building_arms_industry.level } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_artillery_foundry } }
			OWNER = { change_variable = { name = arty_level		add = PREV.b:building_artillery_foundry.level } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_munition_plant } }
			OWNER = { change_variable = { name = ammo_level		add = PREV.b:building_munition_plant.level } }
		}
		effect_calc_mil_refund = yes
	}
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = { NOT = { THIS ?= scope:shipyards_state } }
		if = {
			limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_shipyard } }
			OWNER = { change_variable = { name = ship_level		add = PREV.b:building_shipyard.level } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_military_shipyard } }
			OWNER = { change_variable = { name = wship_level	add = PREV.b:building_military_shipyard.level } }
		}
		effect_calc_ship_refund = yes
	}
}
effect_build_mil_industry_private_state = {
	show_as_tooltip = {
		if = {
			limit = { OWNER.var:arms_level	> 0 }
			while = {
				count = OWNER.var:arms_level
				start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_arms_industry
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { OWNER.var:arty_level	> 0 }
			while = {
				count = OWNER.var:arty_level
				start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_artillery_foundry
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { OWNER.var:ammo_level	> 0 }
			while = {
				count = OWNER.var:ammo_level
				start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_munition_plant
			}
		}
	}
	hidden_effect = {
		while = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					OWNER.var:arms_level	> 0
					OWNER.var:arty_level	> 0
					OWNER.var:ammo_level	> 0
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { OWNER.var:arms_level	> 0 }
				start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_arms_industry
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = arms_level	subtract = 1 } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { OWNER.var:arty_level	> 0 }
				start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_artillery_foundry
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = arty_level	subtract = 1 } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { OWNER.var:ammo_level	> 0 }
				start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_munition_plant
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = ammo_level	subtract = 1 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

	if = {
		limit = {

				has_law = law_type:law_laissez_faire

				# has_company = company_type:company_la_rosada

				# has_company = company_type:company_skoda

				# has_company = company_type:company_rossi
				# has_company = company_type:company_trubia
				# has_company = company_type:company_zastava
				# has_company = company_type:company_saint_etienne
				# has_company = company_type:company_izhevsk_arms_plant
				# has_company = company_type:company_basic_metalworks
			}
		}
		scope:military_industry_state	= { effect_build_mil_industry_private_state	= yes }
	}
	else = { scope:military_industry_state	= { effect_build_mil_industry_state		= yes } }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_law = law_type:law_laissez_faire
				has_law = law_type:law_extraction_economy
				# has_company = company_type:company_suez_company

				# has_company = company_type:company_estaleiro_maua

			}
		}

	}
	else = { scope:shipyards_state	= { effect_build_mil_industry_coastal_state			= yes } }

effect_overlord_war_industry = {
	OVERLORD.capital = {
		while = {
			count = 20
			start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_munition_plant
		}
	}
}
effect_fix_subjects = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_technology_researched = enclosure }
		every_subject_or_below = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = enclosure } }				add_technology_researched = enclosure }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_technology_researched = urban_planning }
		every_subject_or_below = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = urban_planning } }			add_technology_researched = urban_planning }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_technology_researched = shaft_mining }
		every_subject_or_below = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = shaft_mining } }			add_technology_researched = shaft_mining }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_technology_researched = prospecting }
		every_subject_or_below = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = prospecting } }			add_technology_researched = prospecting }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_technology_researched = intensive_agriculture }
		every_subject_or_below = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = intensive_agriculture } }	add_technology_researched = intensive_agriculture }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_technology_researched = improved_fertilizer }
		every_subject_or_below = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = improved_fertilizer } }	add_technology_researched = improved_fertilizer }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_technology_researched = nitrogen_fixation }
		every_subject_or_below = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = nitrogen_fixation } }		add_technology_researched = nitrogen_fixation }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_technology_researched = mechanized_farming }
		every_subject_or_below = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = mechanized_farming } }		add_technology_researched = mechanized_farming }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_technology_researched = nitroglycerin }
		every_subject_or_below = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = nitroglycerin } }			add_technology_researched = nitroglycerin }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_technology_researched = dynamite }
		every_subject_or_below = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = dynamite } }				add_technology_researched = dynamite }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_technology_researched = railways }
		every_subject_or_below = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = railways } }				add_technology_researched = railways }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_technology_researched = watertube_boiler }
		every_subject_or_below = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = watertube_boiler } }		add_technology_researched = watertube_boiler }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_technology_researched = rotary_valve_engine }
		every_subject_or_below = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = rotary_valve_engine } }	add_technology_researched = rotary_valve_engine }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_technology_researched = mechanized_workshops }
		every_subject_or_below = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = mechanized_workshops } }	add_technology_researched = mechanized_workshops }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_technology_researched = steel_railway_cars }
		every_subject_or_below = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = steel_railway_cars } }		add_technology_researched = steel_railway_cars }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_technology_researched = vacuum_canning }
		every_subject_or_below = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = vacuum_canning } }			add_technology_researched = vacuum_canning }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_technology_researched = electrical_generation }
		every_subject_or_below = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = electrical_generation } }	add_technology_researched = electrical_generation }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_technology_researched = steam_turbine }
		every_subject_or_below = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = steam_turbine } }			add_technology_researched = steam_turbine }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_technology_researched = electrical_capacitors }
		every_subject_or_below = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = electrical_capacitors } }	add_technology_researched = electrical_capacitors }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_technology_researched = pasteurization }
		every_subject_or_below = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = pasteurization } }			add_technology_researched = pasteurization }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_technology_researched = electric_railway }
		every_subject_or_below = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = electric_railway } }		add_technology_researched = electric_railway }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_technology_researched = aniline }
		every_subject_or_below = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = aniline } }				add_technology_researched = aniline }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_technology_researched = art_silk }
		every_subject_or_below = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = art_silk } }				add_technology_researched = art_silk }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_technology_researched = telephone }
		every_subject_or_below = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = telephone } }				add_technology_researched = telephone }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_technology_researched = radio }
		every_subject_or_below = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = radio } }				add_technology_researched = radio }
	}
	every_country = {
		limit = { is_subject_of = ROOT }
		# every_scope_state = {
			# limit = {
				# NOT = { has_building = building_government_administration }
				# trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = yes
			# }
			# create_building = { building = building_government_administration level = 1 }
			# OWNER = { add_treasury =-100000 }
		# }
		effect_admin_switch	= yes
		effect_calc_admins	= yes
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { has_variable = missing_admins }
			save_temporary_scope_value_as = {
				name = cost
				value = {
					value		= var:missing_admins
					multiply	= construction_cost_very_low
					multiply	= const_cost_factor
					multiply	=-1
				}
			}
			create_building = { building = building_government_administration level = total_queued_admin_levels }
			OWNER = { add_treasury = PREV.cost }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_railway } }
			effect_passenger_type_switch	= yes
			effect_train_type_switch		= yes
			effect_rail_type_switch			= yes
			effect_transport_switch			= yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_power_plant NOT = { has_active_production_method = pm_rail_transport_power_plant } }
				has_technology_researched = electric_railway
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_power_plant NOT = { has_active_production_method = pm_rail_transport_power_plant } } }
				activate_production_method = { building_type = building_power_plant	production_method = pm_rail_transport_power_plant }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_power_plant NOT = { has_active_production_method = pm_oil-fired_plant } }
				has_technology_researched = oil_turbine
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { can_activate_production_method = { building_type = building_power_plant production_method = pm_hydroelectric_plant } }
					any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_power_plant NOT = { has_active_production_method = pm_oil-fired_plant } }
				}
				activate_production_method = { building_type = building_power_plant	production_method = pm_oil-fired_plant }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_power_plant NOT = { has_active_production_method = pm_coal-fired_plant } }
				has_technology_researched = steam_turbine
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { can_activate_production_method = { building_type = building_power_plant production_method = pm_hydroelectric_plant } }
					any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_power_plant NOT = { has_active_production_method = pm_coal-fired_plant } }
				}
				activate_production_method = { building_type = building_power_plant	production_method = pm_coal-fired_plant }
			}
		}
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				can_activate_production_method = { building_type = building_power_plant production_method = pm_hydroelectric_plant }
				any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_power_plant NOT = { has_active_production_method = pm_hydroelectric_plant } }
			}
			activate_production_method = { building_type = building_power_plant	production_method = pm_hydroelectric_plant }
		}
		effect_build_infrastructure	= yes	# We first set the pm, then build the remaining buildings
	}
	every_state = {	# This puts them in our construction queue instead of theirs - Or it should
		limit = { OWNER = { is_subject_of = ROOT } }
		effect_delete_finite_agro_state		= yes
		# effect_delete_finite_plant_state	= yes
		# effect_seed_autarky_farms_state		= yes
		if = { limit = { free_arable_land > 0 } create_building = { building = building_autarky_farm level = arable_land } }
		effect_seed_resources_state			= yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				trigger_inhospitable_terrain = no
				trigger_if = { limit = { is_capital = no } NOT = { trigger_microstate = yes } }
			}
			effect_seed_greenhouses_state	= yes
		}
		# every_scope_building = { limit = { is_building_type = building_autarky_farm is_subsidized = no } set_subsidized = yes }
		every_scope_building = { limit = { is_building_group = bg_extraction is_subsidized = no } set_subsidized = yes }
	}
	every_country = {
		limit = { is_subject_of = ROOT }
		hidden_effect = { activate_production_method = { building_type = building_autarky_greenhouse		production_method = pm_tools_disabled_subsistence } }
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = effect_tool_fertilizer_switch.tt
			effect_reset_autarky_pm_state	= yes
			effect_fertilizer_switch		= yes
			effect_tools_switch				= yes
			effect_mining_pumps_switch		= yes
			effect_dynamite_switch			= yes
			effect_greenhouse_switch		= yes
		}
		# effect_replace_dye	= yes
		# effect_replace_silk	= yes
		# effect_build_autarky_farms	= yes
	}
}
effect_fix_customs_union = {
	if = {

			limit = {
				trigger_PREV_has_investment_rights = yes
				NOT = { has_technology_researched = enclosure }
			}
			add_technology_researched = enclosure
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_technology_researched = urban_planning }

effect_enslave_discriminated_pops = {
	custom_tooltip = {
		text = "All discriminated pops will be enslaved."
		every_scope_pop = {
			limit = {
				pop_acceptance < acceptance_status_4
				trigger_if		= { limit = { is_employed = yes } is_pop_type = workplace.type.slaves_role }
				trigger_else	= { is_pop_type = laborers }
			}
			change_poptype = pop_type:slaves
		}
	}
}
effect_delete_invalid_subsistence = {
	every_state = {
		limit = { free_arable_land = 0 }
		show_as_tooltip = {
			switch = {
				trigger = has_building
				building_subsistence_farm				= { remove_building = building_subsistence_farm }
				building_subsistence_orchard			= { remove_building = building_subsistence_orchard }
				building_subsistence_pasture			= { remove_building = building_subsistence_pasture }
				building_subsistence_fishing_village	= { remove_building = building_subsistence_fishing_village }
				building_subsistence_rice_farm		= { remove_building = building_subsistence_rice_farm }
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			remove_building = building_subsistence_farm
			remove_building = building_subsistence_orchard
			remove_building = building_subsistence_pasture
			remove_building = building_subsistence_fishing_village
			remove_building = building_subsistence_rice_farm
		}
	}
}

effect_merge_bic = {	# How did I mess this up?
	if = {
		limit = { any_country = { filter = { is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india } OR = { has_variable = india_subject_var THIS ?= c:BIC THIS ?= c:BHT } } }
		random_country = { limit = { is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india OR = { has_variable = india_subject_var THIS ?= c:BIC THIS ?= c:BHT } } save_scope_as = BIC_scope }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOR = {
				exists = c:BIC
				exists = c:BHT
			}
		}
		ROOT = { save_scope_as = BIC_scope }
	}
	else = { random_country = { limit = { is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india } save_scope_as = BIC_scope } }
	every_state_region = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india
				# THIS ?= s:STATE_MALAYA
				# is_homeland = cu:burmese
				# is_homeland = cu:kachin
				any_scope_state = {	# Due to OWNER scope
					OR = {
						AND = {
							OWNER ?= { NOT = { has_variable = indochina_subject_var } }
							region = sr:region_indochina
						}
						# AND = {
							# OWNER ?= {
								# NOR = {
									# has_variable = indonesia_subject_var
									# THIS ?= c:DEI
									# was_formed_from = DEI
								# }
							# }
							# region = sr:region_indonesia
						# }
					}
				}
			}
			any_scope_state = {
				OWNER ?= {
					NOT = { THIS ?= scope:BIC_scope }
					OR = {
						THIS ?= scope:BIC_scope.OVERLORD
						any_overlord_or_above = { THIS ?= scope:BIC_scope.OVERLORD }
					}
				}
			}
			NOT = { THIS ?= s:STATE_TENASSERIM }			# Split with SIAM
		}
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				OWNER ?= {
					OR = {
						THIS ?= scope:BIC_scope.OVERLORD
						any_overlord_or_above = { THIS ?= scope:BIC_scope.OVERLORD }
					}
				}
				NOR = {
					trigger_if = { limit = { exists = c:BIC } OWNER ?= c:BIC }
					trigger_if = { limit = { exists = c:BHT } OWNER ?= c:BHT }
					OWNER ?= { has_variable = india_subject_var }
				}
			}
			set_state_owner = scope:BIC_scope
		}
	}
}
effect_merge_dei = {
	every_state_region = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				is_homeland = cu:balinese
				is_homeland = cu:batak
				is_homeland = cu:bornean
				is_homeland = cu:dayak
				is_homeland = cu:javan
				is_homeland = cu:malay
				is_homeland = cu:moluccan
				is_homeland = cu:sumatran
				THIS ?= s:STATE_WESTERN_NEW_GUINEA
				THIS ?= s:STATE_EASTERN_NEW_GUINEA
			}
			NOT = { THIS ?= s:STATE_MALAYA }		# Belongs to Britain
		}
		if		= { limit = { exists = c:DEI } 						every_scope_state = { limit = { NOT = { OWNER = c:DEI } } set_state_owner = c:DEI } }
		else_if	= { limit = { exists = c:IDN } 						every_scope_state = { limit = { NOT = { OWNER = c:IDN } } set_state_owner = c:IDN } }
		else_if	= { limit = { c:BIC = { is_subject_of = ROOT } }	every_scope_state = { limit = { NOT = { OWNER = c:BIC } } set_state_owner = c:BIC } }
		else_if	= { limit = { exists = c:NET } 						every_scope_state = { limit = { NOT = { OWNER = c:NET } } set_state_owner = c:NET } }
		else_if	= { limit = { exists = c:BNL } 						every_scope_state = { limit = { NOT = { OWNER = c:BNL } } set_state_owner = c:BNL } }
		else_if	= { limit = { exists = c:UNL } 						every_scope_state = { limit = { NOT = { OWNER = c:UNL } } set_state_owner = c:UNL } }
	}
}
effect_tobacco_ban = {
	market = {
		every_scope_country = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_banning_goods = g:tobacco } }
			add_banned_goods = g:tobacco
			add_modifier = { name = tobacco_ban_authority }
		}
		every_scope_country = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_tobacco_plantation }
					any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_tobacco_greenhouse }
				}
			}
			show_as_tooltip = {
				every_scope_state = { limit = { has_building = building_tobacco_plantation }		remove_building = building_tobacco_plantation }
				every_scope_state = { limit = { has_building = building_tobacco_greenhouse }	remove_building = building_tobacco_greenhouse }
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				every_scope_state = {
					remove_building = building_tobacco_plantation
					remove_building = building_tobacco_greenhouse
				}
			}
		}
		every_scope_country = {
			limit = { any_primary_culture = { has_cultural_obsession = tobacco } }
			every_primary_culture = {
				limit = { has_cultural_obsession = tobacco }
				remove_cultural_obsession = tobacco
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_remove_tobacco_ban = {
	market = {
		every_scope_country = {
			limit = { is_banning_goods = g:tobacco }
			remove_banned_goods = g:tobacco
			remove_modifier = tobacco_ban_authority
		}
	}
}
effect_export_product = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_free_trade
			market.mg:$product$.market_goods_production > 0		# Don't try to export something we don't make.
		}
		set_import_tariff_level = { goods = g:$product$			level = no_tariffs_or_subventions }
		set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:$product$			level = high_subventions }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_free_trade }
		set_import_tariff_level = { goods = g:$product$			level = no_tariffs_or_subventions }
		set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:$product$			level = no_tariffs_or_subventions }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { market.mg:$product$.market_goods_production > 0 }		# Don't try to export something we don't make.
		set_import_tariff_level = { goods = g:$product$			level = high_tariffs }
		set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:$product$			level = high_subventions }
	}
	else = {
		set_import_tariff_level = { goods = g:$product$			level = no_tariffs_or_subventions }
		set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:$product$			level = no_tariffs_or_subventions }
	}
}
effect_import_product = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_free_trade }
		set_import_tariff_level = { goods = g:$product$			level = high_subventions }
		set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:$product$			level = no_tariffs_or_subventions }
	}
	else = {
		set_import_tariff_level = { goods = g:$product$			level = high_subventions }
		set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:$product$			level = max_tariffs }
	}
}
effect_export_product_conditional = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_free_trade
			market.mg:$product$ = { market_goods_cheaper >= 0.50 }
		}
		set_import_tariff_level = { goods = g:$product$			level = no_tariffs_or_subventions }
		set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:$product$			level = high_subventions }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_free_trade
			# market.mg:$product$ = { market_goods_cheaper >= 0.25 }
		}
		set_import_tariff_level = { goods = g:$product$			level = no_tariffs_or_subventions }
		set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:$product$			level = low_subventions }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { market.mg:$product$ = { market_goods_cheaper >= 0.50 } }
		set_import_tariff_level = { goods = g:$product$			level = max_tariffs }
		set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:$product$			level = high_subventions }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { market.mg:$product$ = { market_goods_cheaper >= 0.25 } }
		set_import_tariff_level = { goods = g:$product$			level = max_tariffs }
		set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:$product$			level = low_subventions }
	}
	else = {
		set_import_tariff_level = { goods = g:$product$			level = max_tariffs }
		set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:$product$			level = no_tariffs_or_subventions }
	}
}
effect_import_product_conditional = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_free_trade
			market.mg:$product$ = { market_goods_pricier >= 0.50 }
		}
		set_import_tariff_level = { goods = g:$product$			level = high_subventions }
		set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:$product$			level = no_tariffs_or_subventions }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_free_trade
			# market.mg:$product$ = { market_goods_pricier >= 0.25 }
		}
		set_import_tariff_level = { goods = g:$product$			level = low_subventions }
		set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:$product$			level = no_tariffs_or_subventions }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { market.mg:$product$ = { market_goods_pricier >= 0.50 } }
		set_import_tariff_level = { goods = g:$product$			level = high_subventions }
		set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:$product$			level = max_tariffs }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { market.mg:$product$ = { market_goods_pricier >= 0.25 } }
		set_import_tariff_level = { goods = g:$product$			level = low_subventions }
		set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:$product$			level = max_tariffs }
	}
	else = {
		set_import_tariff_level = { goods = g:$product$			level = no_tariffs_or_subventions }
		set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:$product$			level = max_tariffs }
	}
}

		set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:merchant_marine	level = max_tariffs }
