effect_default_import_export = {	# max_tariffs high_tariffs low_tariffs no_tariffs_or_subventions low_subventions high_subventions max_subventions
	custom_tooltip = {
		text = mercantilist_trading.tt	# TODO: Add variant for Free Trade
		# Military
		if = {
			limit = { market_capital.market.mg:small_arms = { market_goods_pricier >= 0.50 } }
			set_import_tariff_level = { goods = g:small_arms			level = high_subventions }
			set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:small_arms			level = no_tariffs_or_subventions }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { market_capital.market.mg:small_arms = { market_goods_pricier > 0 } }
			set_import_tariff_level = { goods = g:small_arms			level = low_subventions }
			set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:small_arms			level = no_tariffs_or_subventions }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_free_trade }
			set_import_tariff_level = { goods = g:small_arms			level = no_tariffs_or_subventions }
			set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:small_arms			level = high_subventions }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_building = building_arms_industry }
			set_import_tariff_level = { goods = g:small_arms			level = max_tariffs }
			set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:small_arms			level = high_subventions }
		}
		else = {
			set_import_tariff_level = { goods = g:small_arms			level = low_tariffs }
			set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:small_arms			level = low_subventions }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { market_capital.market.mg:artillery = { market_goods_pricier >= 0.50 } }
			set_import_tariff_level = { goods = g:artillery				level = high_subventions }
			set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:artillery				level = no_tariffs_or_subventions }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { market_capital.market.mg:artillery = { market_goods_pricier > 0 } }
			set_import_tariff_level = { goods = g:artillery				level = low_subventions }
			set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:artillery				level = no_tariffs_or_subventions }

		}

			set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:artillery				level = low_subventions }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_technology_researched = general_staff
				market_capital.market.mg:ammunition = { market_goods_pricier >= 0.50 }
			}
			set_import_tariff_level = { goods = g:ammunition			level = high_subventions }
			set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:ammunition			level = high_tariffs }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_technology_researched = general_staff

			}
			set_import_tariff_level = { goods = g:ammunition			level = low_subventions }
			set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:ammunition			level = high_tariffs }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_building = building_munition_plant

			}
			set_import_tariff_level = { goods = g:ammunition			level = max_tariffs }
			set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:ammunition			level = high_subventions }
		}

			set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:ammunition			level = low_subventions }

		else = {

			set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:ammunition			level = low_subventions }

			set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:ironclads				level = no_tariffs_or_subventions }
		}
		# Staple - high_tariffs instead of max_tariffs to prevent death spiral
		if = {
			limit = { has_journal_entry = je_set_up_grain_import }

			set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:grain					level = max_tariffs }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { market_capital.market.mg:groceries = { market_goods_cheaper >= 0.5 } }				# Don't bother with grain when you can have groceries
			set_import_tariff_level = { goods = g:grain					level = max_tariffs }
			set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:grain					level = max_tariffs }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { market_capital.market.mg:grain = { market_goods_pricier >= 0.50 } }					# Emergency grain import
			set_import_tariff_level = { goods = g:grain					level = max_subventions }			# No matter the cost
			set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:grain					level = max_tariffs }
		}

			set_import_tariff_level = { goods = g:coal					level = low_subventions }
		}
		else = { set_import_tariff_level = { goods = g:coal				level = high_tariffs } }
		set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:coal						level = no_tariffs_or_subventions }

			set_import_tariff_level = { goods = g:sulfur				level = low_subventions }

			set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:telephones			level = no_tariffs_or_subventions }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { market_capital.market.mg:telephones = { market_goods_cheaper >= 0.5 } }
			set_import_tariff_level = { goods = g:telephones			level = max_tariffs }
			set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:telephones			level = low_subventions }
		}
		else = {
			set_import_tariff_level = { goods = g:telephones			level = high_tariffs }
			set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:telephones			level = no_tariffs_or_subventions }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { market_capital.market.mg:radios = { market_goods_pricier > 0 } }
			set_import_tariff_level = { goods = g:radios				level = no_tariffs_or_subventions }
			set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:radios				level = no_tariffs_or_subventions }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { market_capital.market.mg:radios = { market_goods_cheaper >= 0.5 } }
			set_import_tariff_level = { goods = g:radios				level = max_tariffs }
			set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:radios				level = low_subventions }
		}
		else = {
			set_import_tariff_level = { goods = g:radios				level = high_tariffs }
			set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:radios				level = no_tariffs_or_subventions }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { market_capital.market.mg:fine_art = { market_goods_pricier > 0 } }
			set_import_tariff_level = { goods = g:fine_art				level = no_tariffs_or_subventions }
			set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:fine_art				level = no_tariffs_or_subventions }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { market_capital.market.mg:fine_art = { market_goods_cheaper >= 0.5 } }
			set_import_tariff_level = { goods = g:fine_art				level = max_tariffs }
			set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:fine_art				level = low_subventions }
		}
		else = {
			set_import_tariff_level = { goods = g:fine_art				level = high_tariffs }
			set_export_tariff_level = { goods = g:fine_art				level = no_tariffs_or_subventions }
		}
	}
}
effect_innovative_general = {
	if = { limit = { NOT = { exists = $IG$ } } IG = ig_armed_forces }
	create_character = {
		female = yes
		is_general = yes
		age = 18
		# religion = rel:atheist
		interest_group = ig:$IG$
		trait_generation = {
			add_trait = innovative
			add_trait = popular_commander			# Practically Required
			add_trait = basic_offensive_planner
			add_trait = basic_defensive_strategist
			add_trait = basic_artillery_commander
			if = {
				limit = { trait_value > 0 }
				set_variable = has_historical_traits
				add_experience = trait_value
			}
			else = { add_experience = 4 }
			add_random_trait = personality
			if = { limit = { has_trait = bigoted } remove_trait = bigoted add_random_trait = personality }
			# if = { limit = { OWNER = { has_technology_researched = breech_loading_artillery } }	add_trait = basic_artillery_commander }
			if = { limit = { OWNER = { has_technology_researched = defense_in_depth } }			add_trait = stalwart_defender }
			if = { limit = { has_trait = bigoted } remove_trait = bigoted add_random_trait = personality }
			if = {
				limit = { OR = { NOT = { has_variable = has_historical_traits } trait_value <= 3 } }
				add_random_trait = skill			
			}
			# if = { limit = { has_trait = traditionalist_commander } effect_upgrade_popularity_trait = yes }
			# if = { limit = { NOT = { has_modifier = modifier_character_extra_ten_percent_command } } add_modifier = { name = modifier_character_extra_ten_percent_command } }
		}
		on_created = {
			if = { limit = { NOT = { is_interest_group_type = ig_devout } }																	change_character_religion = rel:atheist }
			if = { limit = { NOT = { is_interest_group_type = ig_devout } has_ideology = ideology:ideology_traditionalist }					set_ideology = ideology:ideology_moderate }
			if = { limit = { NOT = { is_interest_group_type = ig_devout } has_ideology = ideology:ideology_traditionalist_minoritarian }	set_ideology = ideology:ideology_nihilist }
			if = { limit = { NOT = { is_interest_group_type = ig_devout } has_ideology = ideology:ideology_fascist }						set_ideology = ideology:ideology_humanitarian }
			if = {
				limit = {
					OWNER ?= { any_primary_culture = { has_discrimination_trait = language_anglophone } }
					culture = { has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_south_asian NOT = { THIS ?= cu:anglo_indian } }
				}
				change_character_culture = cu:anglo_indian
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					OWNER ?= { any_primary_culture = { has_discrimination_trait = tradition_russosphere } }
					culture = { has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_european NOT = { THIS ?= cu:east_german } }
				}
				change_character_culture = cu:east_german
			}
			if = { limit = { OWNER ?= { has_technology_researched = automatic_machine_guns } }									add_modifier = { name = machine_gun_expertise_high } }
			if = { limit = { OWNER ?= { has_technology_researched = military_aviation } }										add_modifier = { name = war_hero_fighter } }
		}
	}
}

effect_humanitarian_agitator = {
	if = { limit = { NOT = { exists = $IG$ } } IG = ig_armed_forces }
	create_character = {
		female = yes
		age = 18
		is_agitator = yes
		# religion = rel:atheist
		interest_group = ig:$IG$
		trait_generation = {
			add_trait = innovative
			add_trait = expert_political_operator
			add_trait = masterful_diplomat
			add_trait = expert_colonial_administrator
			add_trait = expert_entrepreneur
			add_trait = firebrand
			add_trait = popular_commander		# Practically Required
			if = {
				limit = { trait_value > 0 }
				set_variable = has_historical_traits
				add_experience = trait_value
			}
			else = { add_experience = 4 }
			add_random_trait = personality
			if = { limit = { has_trait = bigoted } remove_trait = bigoted add_random_trait = personality }
			if = {
				limit = { OR = { NOT = { has_variable = has_historical_traits } trait_value <= 3 } }
				add_random_trait = skill
			}
			# if = { limit = { has_trait = traditionalist_commander } effect_upgrade_popularity_trait = yes }
			# if = { limit = { NOT = { has_modifier = modifier_character_extra_ten_percent_command } } add_modifier = { name = modifier_character_extra_ten_percent_command } }
		}
		on_created = {
			if = { limit = { NOT = { is_interest_group_type = ig_devout } } change_character_religion = rel:atheist }
			add_modifier = { name = modifier_character_popular_adored }
			if		= { limit = { OWNER ?= { has_technology_researched = human_rights has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy } }	set_ideology = ideology:ideology_humanitarian_royalist }
			else_if	= { limit = { OWNER ?= { has_technology_researched = human_rights } }												set_ideology = ideology:ideology_humanitarian }
			# else_if	= { limit = { OWNER ?= { has_technology_researched = human_rights } }												set_ideology = ideology:ideology_sovereignist_leader }
			else	= {																													set_ideology = ideology:ideology_utopian }
			save_scope_as = humanitarian_agitator
		}
	}
}

		female = yes
		age = 18

			add_trait = expert_entrepreneur
			add_trait = firebrand

				set_variable = has_historical_traits
				add_experience = trait_value
			}

			if = { limit = { has_trait = bigoted } remove_trait = bigoted add_random_trait = personality }

				set_ideology = ideology:ideology_nihilist
			}
		}
	}

effect_autarky_ownership = {
	every_scope_building = {
		is_building_type = building_autarky_farm
		add_ownership = {
			building = {
				type	= building_manor_house
				country	= "OWNER"
				levels	= 1
				region	= STATE.state_region
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_add_company_resource_levels = {
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = {
			has_potential_resource = $building$
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = $building$ } }
				any_scope_building = { is_building_type = $building$ level < $levels$ }	# company_employed_levels doesn't work that way
			}
		}
		create_building = { building = $building$	add_ownership = { company = { type = $company$ country = $country$ levels = $levels$ } } }
		# ROOT = { change_variable = { name = loans_var add = $cost$ } }
		save_temporary_scope_value_as = { name = temp_cost value = $cost$ }	# TODO: Check whether this step is necessary.
		effect_handle_cost = { cost = temp_cost debtor = ROOT }
	}
}
effect_add_company_levels = {
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = $building$ } }
				any_scope_building = { is_building_type = $building$ level < $levels$ }	# company_employed_levels doesn't work that way
			}
		}
		create_building = { building = $building$	add_ownership = { company = { type = $company$ country = $country$ levels = $levels$ } } }
		# ROOT = { change_variable = { name = loans_var add = $cost$ } }
		save_temporary_scope_value_as = { name = temp_cost value = $cost$ }	# TODO: Check whether this step is necessary.
		effect_handle_cost = { cost = temp_cost debtor = ROOT }
	}
}
effect_add_private_levels_state = {	# self-owned: $owner$ == $building$
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = $building$ } }
				any_scope_building = { is_building_type = $building$ level < $levels$ }
			}
		}
		create_building = { building = $building$	add_ownership = { building = { type = $owner$ country = $country$ region = $state$ levels = $levels$ } } }
		# ROOT = { change_variable = { name = loans_var add = $cost$ } }
		save_temporary_scope_value_as = { name = temp_cost value = $cost$ }	# TODO: Check whether this step is necessary.
		effect_handle_cost = { cost = temp_cost debtor = ROOT }
	}
}
effect_add_private_levels = {	# self-owned: $owner$ == $building$
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = {

				any_scope_building = { is_building_type = $building$ level < $levels$ }
			}
		}
		create_building = { building = $building$	add_ownership = { building = { type = $owner$ country = $country$ region = $state$ levels = $levels$ } } }
		# ROOT = { change_variable = { name = loans_var add = $cost$ } }
		save_temporary_scope_value_as = { name = temp_cost value = $cost$ }	# TODO: Check whether this step is necessary.
		effect_handle_cost = { cost = temp_cost debtor = ROOT }
	}
}
effect_sovereign_resource_fund = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_company = company_type:company_sovereign_resource_fund }
		save_temporary_scope_as = country_scope
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					THIS ?= ROOT
					trigger_PREV_has_investment_rights = yes
				}
			}
			effect_add_company_resource_levels = {
				building	= building_logging_camp
				levels		= 1
				company		= company_sovereign_resource_fund
				cost		= 0.2	# Every building costs 200 points x 1k/point in Millions
				country		= "scope:country_scope"
			}
			effect_add_company_resource_levels = {
				building	= building_rubber_plantation
				levels		= 1
				company		= company_sovereign_resource_fund
				cost		= 0.2	# Every building costs 200 points x 1k/point in Millions
				country		= "scope:country_scope"
			}
			effect_add_company_resource_levels = {
				building	= building_fishing_wharf
				levels		= 1
				company		= company_sovereign_resource_fund
				cost		= 0.2	# Every building costs 200 points x 1k/point in Millions
				country		= "scope:country_scope"
			}
			effect_add_company_resource_levels = {
				building	= building_whaling_station
				levels		= 1
				company		= company_sovereign_resource_fund
				cost		= 0.2	# Every building costs 200 points x 1k/point in Millions
				country		= "scope:country_scope"
			}
			effect_add_company_resource_levels = {
				building	= building_coal_mine
				levels		= 1
				company		= company_sovereign_resource_fund
				cost		= 0.4	# Every building costs 400 points x 1k/point in Millions
				country		= "scope:country_scope"
			}
			effect_add_company_resource_levels = {
				building	= building_iron_mine
				levels		= 1
				company		= company_sovereign_resource_fund
				cost		= 0.4	# Every building costs 400 points x 1k/point in Millions
				country		= "scope:country_scope"
			}
			effect_add_company_resource_levels = {
				building	= building_lead_mine
				levels		= 1
				company		= company_sovereign_resource_fund
				cost		= 0.4	# Every building costs 400 points x 1k/point in Millions
				country		= "scope:country_scope"
			}
			effect_add_company_resource_levels = {
				building	= building_gold_mine
				levels		= 1
				company		= company_sovereign_resource_fund
				cost		= 0.4	# Every building costs 400 points x 1k/point in Millions
				country		= "scope:country_scope"
			}
			effect_add_company_resource_levels = {
				building	= building_oil_rig
				levels		= 1
				company		= company_sovereign_resource_fund
				cost		= 0.4	# Every building costs 400 points x 1k/point in Millions
				country		= "scope:country_scope"
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					is_sea_adjacent = yes
					OR = {
						NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type	= building_infres_fishing_wharf } }
						any_scope_building = { is_building_type	= building_infres_fishing_wharf company_employed_levels < 1 }
					}
				}
				create_building = { building = "building_infres_fishing_wharf"			add_ownership = { company = { type = company_sovereign_resource_fund country = "scope:country_scope" levels = 1 } } }
				ROOT = { change_variable = { name = loans_var add = 0.2 } }	# Every building costs 200 points x 1k/point in Millions
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					is_sea_adjacent = yes
					OR = {
						NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type	= building_infres_whaling_station } }
						any_scope_building = { is_building_type	= building_infres_whaling_station company_employed_levels < 1 }
					}
				}
				create_building = { building = "building_infres_whaling_station"		add_ownership = { company = { type = company_sovereign_resource_fund country = "scope:country_scope" levels = 1 } } }
				ROOT = { change_variable = { name = loans_var add = 0.2 } }	# Every building costs 200 points x 1k/point in Millions
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_subject_or_below = {
				is_subject_type = subject_type_chartered_company
				NOR = {	# Historical CCs
					THIS ?= c:BIC
					THIS ?= c:HBC
					THIS ?= c:ALK
				}
			}
		}
		ordered_subject_or_below = {
			limit = {
				is_subject_type = subject_type_chartered_company
				NOR = {	# Historical CCs
					THIS ?= c:BIC
					THIS ?= c:HBC
					THIS ?= c:ALK
				}
			}
			order_by = gdp
			position = 0
			save_temporary_scope_as = chartered_company_scope
		}
		company:company_sovereign_resource_fund = { add_owned_country = scope:chartered_company_scope }
	}
}
effect_rebuild_autarky_SRF_setup = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_company = company_type:company_sovereign_resource_fund }
		save_temporary_scope_as = country_scope
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_autarky_farm } }
			remove_building = building_autarky_farm
		}
		effect_add_company_levels = {
			building	= building_autarky_farm
			levels		= 1
			company		= company_sovereign_resource_fund
			cost		= 0.2	# Every building costs 200 points x 1k/point in Millions
			country		= "scope:country_scope"
		}
	}
}

effect_sovereign_growth_fund = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_company = company_type:company_sovereign_growth_fund }
		save_temporary_scope_as = country_scope
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					THIS ?= ROOT
					trigger_PREV_has_investment_rights = yes
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { always = yes }	# TODO: Figure out how to check for company charters
				effect_add_company_levels = {
					building	= building_autarky_greenhouse
					levels		= 5
					company		= company_sovereign_growth_fund
					cost		= 2.0	# Every building costs 400 points x 1k/point in Millions
					country		= "scope:country_scope"
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { any_scope_state = { trigger_vineyard_greenhouse_allowed = yes } }
				effect_add_company_levels = {
					building	= building_vineyard_greenhouse
					levels		= 5

effect_prussian_state_railways = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_company = company_type:company_prussian_state_railways }
		save_temporary_scope_as = country_scope
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_technology_researched = railways } }
			if		= { limit = { has_building = building_motor_industry }	set_variable = { name = old_level value = b:building_motor_industry.level } }
			else	= {														set_variable = { name = old_level value = 0 } }
			create_building = { building = "building_motor_industry" add_ownership = { company = { type = company_prussian_state_railways country = "scope:country_scope" levels = 1 } } }
			if = {
				limit = { b:building_motor_industry.level > var:old_level }	# Required to prevent division by zero errors
				set_variable = {
					name = temp_price
					value = {
						value		= b:building_motor_industry.level
						subtract	= var:old_level
						multiply = {
							value	= construction_cost_very_high	# Building price in construction points
							divide	= 1000							# 1k/point in Millions
						}
						min			= 0
					}
				}
				ROOT = { change_variable = { name = loans_var add = PREV.var:temp_price } }
			}
			remove_variable = old_level
			remove_variable = temp_price
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					THIS ?= ROOT
					trigger_PREV_has_investment_rights = yes
				}
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { ROOT = { has_technology_researched = railways } }
				if		= { limit = { has_building = building_railway }			set_variable = { name = old_level value = b:building_railway.level } }
				else	= {														set_variable = { name = old_level value = 0 } }
				create_building = { building = "building_railway" subsidized = yes add_ownership = { company = { type = company_prussian_state_railways country = "scope:country_scope" levels = 1 } } }
				if = {
					limit = { b:building_railway.level > var:old_level }	# Required to prevent division by zero errors
					set_variable = {
						name = temp_price
						value = {
							value		= b:building_railway.level
							subtract	= var:old_level
							multiply = {
								value	= construction_cost_very_high	# Building price in construction points
								divide	= 1000							# 1k/point in Millions
							}
							min			= 0
						}
					}
					ROOT = { change_variable = { name = loans_var add = PREV.var:temp_price } }
				}
				remove_variable = old_level
				remove_variable = temp_price
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { ROOT = { has_technology_researched = electrical_generation } }
				if		= { limit = { has_building = building_power_plant }		set_variable = { name = old_level value = b:building_power_plant.level } }
				else	= {														set_variable = { name = old_level value = 0 } }
				create_building = { building = "building_power_plant" subsidized = yes add_ownership = { company = { type = company_prussian_state_railways country = "scope:country_scope" levels = 1 } } }
				if = {
					limit = { b:building_power_plant.level > var:old_level }	# Required to prevent division by zero errors
					set_variable = {
						name = temp_price
						value = {
							value		= b:building_power_plant.level
							subtract	= var:old_level
							multiply = {
								value	= construction_cost_medium		# Building price in construction points
								divide	= 1000							# 1k/point in Millions
							}
							min			= 0
						}
					}
					ROOT = { change_variable = { name = loans_var add = PREV.var:temp_price } }
				}
				remove_variable = old_level
				remove_variable = temp_price
			}
		}
	}
}

			ROOT = { change_variable = { name = loans_var add = 0.6 } }	# Every building costs 200 points x 1k/point in Millions
		}
	}
	else_if = {

		add_company = company_type:company_east_india_company
		company:company_east_india_company = { set_company_establishment_date = 1600.12.31 set_company_state_region = s:STATE_WEST_BENGAL }

			limit = { OWNER = { has_technology_researched = urban_planning } is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india }

			create_building = { building = "building_tobacco_greenhouse"		add_ownership = { company = { type = company_east_india_company country = "scope:country_scope" levels = 1 } } }

# Staple
effect_calc_merchant_marine_delta = {	# Thx to Pzixel
	hidden_effect = {
		remove_variable = merchant_marine_delta
		remove_variable = merchant_marine_balance_right
		remove_variable = merchant_marine_balance_mid
		if = {
			# limit = { market.mg:merchant_marine = { market_goods_delta < 0 } }
			limit = { merchant_marine_production_delta < 0 }
			set_variable = { name = merchant_marine_delta			value =-1000000 }
			set_variable = { name = merchant_marine_balance_right	value =       0 }
			while = {		# use binary search to find the amount at which market_goods_delta is 0
				count = 21	# log2(1000000) + 1
				set_variable = {
					name = merchant_marine_balance_mid
					value = {
						add = {
							value = var:merchant_marine_delta
							add = var:merchant_marine_balance_right
							divide = 2
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					# limit = { market.mg:merchant_marine = { market_goods_delta > PREV.var:merchant_marine_balance_mid } }
					limit = { merchant_marine_production_delta > PREV.var:merchant_marine_balance_mid }
					set_variable = { name = merchant_marine_delta value = var:merchant_marine_balance_mid }
				}
				else = { set_variable = { name = merchant_marine_balance_right value = var:merchant_marine_balance_mid } }
			}
			# every_scope_state = {	# Moved to script value
				# limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_port is_under_construction = yes } }
				# OWNER = { change_variable = { name = merchant_marine_delta add = PREV.queued_merchant_marine } }
			# }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_trade_center is_under_construction = yes } }
				OWNER ?= { change_variable = { name = merchant_marine_delta subtract = PREV.queued_merchant_marine_trade_centers } }
			}
			# here merchant_marine_delta holds the exact value of market_goods_delta (up to integer precision)
		}
		else = { set_variable	= { name = merchant_marine_delta			value = 0 } }
		set_variable			= { name = missing_port_counter				value = missing_port }
		set_variable			= { name = convoys_delta					value = missing_convoys }	# TODO: Implement this the same way that bureaucracy works
	}
}
effect_build_merchant_marine = {	# country_scope
	custom_tooltip = {
		text = effect_build_merchant_marine.tt
		effect_calc_merchant_marine_delta = yes
		while = {
			limit = {
				var:missing_port_counter > 0
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					OWNER.var:missing_port_counter > 0
					modifier:building_port_throughput_add >= 0	# Namib Desert
					any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_port OR = { occupancy > 0.95 level_after_queued_constructions <= OWNER.economy_of_scale_target } }	# modifier:building_economy_of_scale_level_cap_add
				}
				start_building_construction = building_port
				OWNER ?= { change_variable = { name = merchant_marine_delta	add = PREV.merchant_marine_per_level } }
				OWNER ?= { change_variable = { name = convoys_delta			add = PREV.convoys_per_port } }
				OWNER ?= { change_variable = { name = missing_port_counter	subtract = 1 } }
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_build_merchant_marine_private = {	# country_scope
	custom_tooltip = {
		text = effect_build_merchant_marine.tt
		effect_calc_merchant_marine_delta = yes
		while = {
			limit = {
				var:missing_port_counter > 0
				# any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_port OR = { occupancy > 0.95 level_after_queued_constructions <= OWNER.economy_of_scale_target } }	# modifier:building_economy_of_scale_level_cap_add
			}
			# count = missing_port
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					OWNER.var:missing_port_counter > 0
					modifier:building_port_throughput_add >= 0	# Namib Desert
					any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_port OR = { occupancy > 0.95 level_after_queued_constructions <= OWNER.economy_of_scale_target } }	# modifier:building_economy_of_scale_level_cap_add
				}
				start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_port
				OWNER ?= { change_variable = { name = merchant_marine_delta	add = PREV.merchant_marine_per_level } }
				OWNER ?= { change_variable = { name = convoys_delta			add = PREV.convoys_per_port } }
				OWNER ?= { change_variable = { name = missing_port_counter	subtract = 1 } }
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_calc_groceries_delta = {	# Thx to Pzixel
	hidden_effect = {
		remove_variable = groceries_delta
		remove_variable = groceries_balance_right
		remove_variable = groceries_balance_mid
		if = {
			# limit = { market.mg:groceries = { market_goods_delta < 0 } }
			limit = { groceries_production_delta < 0 }
			set_variable = { name = groceries_delta			value =-1000000 }
			set_variable = { name = groceries_balance_right	value =       0 }
			while = {		# use binary search to find the amount at which market_goods_delta is 0
				count = 21	# log2(1000000) + 1
				set_variable = {
					name = groceries_balance_mid
					value = {
						add = {
							value = var:groceries_delta
							add = var:groceries_balance_right
							divide = 2
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					# limit = { market.mg:groceries = { market_goods_delta > PREV.var:groceries_balance_mid } }
					limit = { groceries_production_delta > PREV.var:groceries_balance_mid }
					set_variable = { name = groceries_delta value = var:groceries_balance_mid }
				}
				else = { set_variable = { name = groceries_balance_right value = var:groceries_balance_mid } }
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_food_industry is_under_construction = yes } }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = groceries_delta add = PREV.queued_groceries } }
			}
			# here groceries_delta holds the exact value of market_goods_delta (up to integer precision)
		}
		else = { set_variable = { name = groceries_delta			value = 0 } }
	}
}
effect_build_groceries = {	# country_scope
	custom_tooltip = {
		text = effect_build_groceries.tt
		effect_calc_groceries_delta = yes
		while = {
			limit = { var:groceries_delta < 0 }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					OWNER.var:groceries_delta < 0
					is_treaty_port = no	# The calculation only applies to your market, which treaty ports aren't a part of!
					any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_food_industry OR = { occupancy > 0.95 level_after_queued_constructions <= OWNER.economy_of_scale_target } }	# modifier:building_economy_of_scale_level_cap_add
				}
				start_building_construction = building_food_industry
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = groceries_delta add = PREV.groceries_per_level } }
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_build_groceries_private = {	# country_scope
	custom_tooltip = {
		text = effect_build_groceries.tt
		effect_calc_groceries_delta = yes
		while = {
			limit = { var:groceries_delta < 0 }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					OWNER.var:groceries_delta < 0
					is_treaty_port = no	# The calculation only applies to your market, which treaty ports aren't a part of!
					any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_food_industry OR = { occupancy > 0.95 level_after_queued_constructions <= OWNER.economy_of_scale_target } }	# modifier:building_economy_of_scale_level_cap_add
				}
				start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_food_industry
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = groceries_delta add = PREV.groceries_per_level } }
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_calc_clothes_delta = {	# Thx to Pzixel
	hidden_effect = {
		remove_variable = clothes_delta
		remove_variable = clothes_balance_right
		remove_variable = clothes_balance_mid
		if = {
			# limit = { market.mg:clothes = { market_goods_delta < 0 } }
			limit = { clothes_production_delta < 0 }
			set_variable = { name = clothes_delta			value =-1000000 }
			set_variable = { name = clothes_balance_right	value =       0 }
			while = {		# use binary search to find the amount at which market_goods_delta is 0
				count = 21	# log2(1000000) + 1
				set_variable = {
					name = clothes_balance_mid
					value = {
						add = {
							value = var:clothes_delta
							add = var:clothes_balance_right
							divide = 2
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					# limit = { market.mg:clothes = { market_goods_delta > PREV.var:clothes_balance_mid } }
					limit = { clothes_production_delta > PREV.var:clothes_balance_mid }
					set_variable = { name = clothes_delta value = var:clothes_balance_mid }
				}
				else = { set_variable = { name = clothes_balance_right value = var:clothes_balance_mid } }
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_textile_mill is_under_construction = yes } }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = clothes_delta add = PREV.queued_clothes } }
			}
			# here clothes_delta holds the exact value of market_goods_delta (up to integer precision)
		}
		else = { set_variable = { name = clothes_delta			value = 0 } }
	}
}

effect_calc_furniture_delta = {	# Thx to Pzixel
	hidden_effect = {
		remove_variable = furniture_delta
		remove_variable = furniture_balance_right
		remove_variable = furniture_balance_mid
		if = {
			# limit = { market.mg:furniture = { market_goods_delta < 0 } }
			limit = { furniture_production_delta < 0 }
			set_variable = { name = furniture_delta			value =-1000000 }
			set_variable = { name = furniture_balance_right	value =       0 }
			while = {		# use binary search to find the amount at which market_goods_delta is 0
				count = 21	# log2(1000000) + 1
				set_variable = {
					name = furniture_balance_mid
					value = {
						add = {
							value = var:furniture_delta
							add = var:furniture_balance_right
							divide = 2
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					# limit = { market.mg:furniture = { market_goods_delta > PREV.var:furniture_balance_mid } }
					limit = { furniture_production_delta > PREV.var:furniture_balance_mid }
					set_variable = { name = furniture_delta value = var:furniture_balance_mid }
				}
				else = { set_variable = { name = furniture_balance_right value = var:furniture_balance_mid } }
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_furniture_manufactory is_under_construction = yes } }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = furniture_delta add = PREV.queued_furniture } }
			}
			# here furniture_delta holds the exact value of market_goods_delta (up to integer precision)
		}
		else = { set_variable = { name = furniture_delta			value = 0 } }
	}
}

effect_calc_paper_delta = {	# Thx to Pzixel
	hidden_effect = {
		remove_variable = paper_delta
		remove_variable = paper_balance_right
		remove_variable = paper_balance_mid
		if = {
			# limit = { market.mg:paper = { market_goods_delta < 0 } }
			limit = { paper_production_delta < 0 }
			set_variable = { name = paper_delta			value =-1000000 }
			set_variable = { name = paper_balance_right	value =       0 }
			while = {		# use binary search to find the amount at which market_goods_delta is 0
				count = 21	# log2(1000000) + 1
				set_variable = {
					name = paper_balance_mid
					value = {
						add = {
							value = var:paper_delta
							add = var:paper_balance_right
							divide = 2
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					# limit = { market.mg:paper = { market_goods_delta > PREV.var:paper_balance_mid } }
					limit = { paper_production_delta > PREV.var:paper_balance_mid }
					set_variable = { name = paper_delta value = var:paper_balance_mid }
				}
				else = { set_variable = { name = paper_balance_right value = var:paper_balance_mid } }
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_paper_mill is_under_construction = yes } }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = paper_delta add = PREV.queued_paper } }
			}
			# here paper_delta holds the exact value of market_goods_delta (up to integer precision)
		}
		else = { set_variable = { name = paper_delta			value = 0 } }
		every_scope_state = {	# To ensure that it happens even if paper_delta > 0
			limit = { OR = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_government_administration is_under_construction = yes } var:missing_admins > 0 } }
			OWNER = { change_variable = { name = paper_delta subtract = PREV.queued_admin_paper } }
		}
		every_scope_state = {	# To ensure that it happens even if paper_delta > 0
			limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_university is_under_construction = yes } }
			OWNER = { change_variable = { name = paper_delta subtract = PREV.queued_uni_paper } }
		}
	}
}

	hidden_effect = {
		remove_variable = wood_delta
		remove_variable = wood_balance_right
		remove_variable = wood_balance_mid
		if = {
			# limit = { market.mg:wood = { market_goods_delta < 0 } }
			limit = { wood_production_delta < 0 }
			set_variable = { name = wood_delta			value =-1000000 }
			set_variable = { name = wood_balance_right	value =       0 }
			while = {		# use binary search to find the amount at which market_goods_delta is 0
				count = 21	# log2(1000000) + 1
				set_variable = {
					name = wood_balance_mid
					value = {
						add = {
							value = var:wood_delta
							add = var:wood_balance_right
							divide = 2
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					# limit = { market.mg:wood = { market_goods_delta > PREV.var:wood_balance_mid } }
					limit = { wood_production_delta > PREV.var:wood_balance_mid }
					set_variable = { name = wood_delta value = var:wood_balance_mid }
				}
				else = { set_variable = { name = wood_balance_right value = var:wood_balance_mid } }
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_logging_camp is_under_construction = yes } }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = wood_delta add = PREV.queued_wood } }
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_logging_greenhouse is_under_construction = yes } }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = wood_delta add = PREV.queued_wood_greenhouse } }
			}
			# here wood_delta holds the exact value of market_goods_delta (up to integer precision)
		}
		else = { set_variable = { name = wood_delta	value = 0 } }
		every_scope_state = {	# To ensure that it happens even if wood_delta > 0
			limit = { OR = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_construction_sector is_under_construction = yes } var:const_to_seed > 0 } }
			OWNER = { change_variable = { name = wood_delta subtract = PREV.queued_construction_wood } }
		}
	}
}

effect_calc_tools_delta = {	# Thx to Pzixel
	hidden_effect = {
		remove_variable = tools_delta
		remove_variable = tools_balance_right
		remove_variable = tools_balance_mid
		if = {
			# limit = { market.mg:tools = { market_goods_delta < 0 } }
			limit = { tools_production_delta < 0 }
			set_variable = { name = tools_delta			value =-1000000 }
			set_variable = { name = tools_balance_right	value =       0 }
			while = {		# use binary search to find the amount at which market_goods_delta is 0
				count = 21	# log2(1000000) + 1
				set_variable = {
					name = tools_balance_mid
					value = {
						add = {
							value = var:tools_delta
							add = var:tools_balance_right
							divide = 2
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					# limit = { market.mg:tools = { market_goods_delta > PREV.var:tools_balance_mid } }
					limit = { tools_production_delta > PREV.var:tools_balance_mid }
					set_variable = { name = tools_delta value = var:tools_balance_mid }
				}
				else = { set_variable = { name = tools_balance_right value = var:tools_balance_mid } }
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_tooling_workshop is_under_construction = yes } }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = tools_delta add = PREV.queued_tools } }
			}
			# here tools_delta holds the exact value of market_goods_delta (up to integer precision)
		}
		else = { set_variable = { name = tools_delta			value = 0 } }
		every_scope_state = {	# To ensure that it happens even if tools_delta > 0
			limit = { OR = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_construction_sector is_under_construction = yes } var:const_to_seed > 0 } }
			OWNER = { change_variable = { name = tools_delta subtract = PREV.queued_construction_tools } }
		}
		every_scope_state = {	# To ensure that it happens even if tools_delta > 0
			limit = { is_production_method_active = { building_type = building_construction_sector	production_method = pm_wooden_buildings } }
			OWNER = { change_variable = { name = tools_delta subtract = PREV.potential_construction_tools } }
		}
	}
}

			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_glassworks is_under_construction = yes } }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = glass_delta add = PREV.queued_glass } }
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { OR = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_construction_sector is_under_construction = yes } var:const_to_seed > 0 } }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = glass_delta subtract = PREV.queued_construction_glass } }
			}
			# here glass_delta holds the exact value of market_goods_delta (up to integer precision)
		}
		else = { set_variable = { name = glass_delta			value = 0 } }
	}
}
effect_build_glass = {	# country_scope
	custom_tooltip = {
		text = effect_build_glass.tt
		effect_calc_glass_delta		= yes
		effect_calc_porcelain_delta	= yes
		while = {
			limit = { var:glass_delta < 0 }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					OWNER.var:glass_delta < 0
					is_treaty_port = no	# The calculation only applies to your market, which treaty ports aren't a part of!
					any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_glassworks OR = { occupancy > 0.95 level_after_queued_constructions <= OWNER.economy_of_scale_target } }	# modifier:building_economy_of_scale_level_cap_add
				}
				start_building_construction = building_glassworks
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = glass_delta		add = PREV.glass_per_level } }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = porcelain_delta	add = PREV.porcelain_per_level } }
			}
		}
	}
}

				}
				start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_glassworks
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = glass_delta		add = PREV.glass_per_level } }

			}
		}
	}
}
effect_calc_steel_delta = {	# Thx to Pzixel
	hidden_effect = {
		remove_variable = steel_delta
		remove_variable = steel_balance_right
		remove_variable = steel_balance_mid
		if = {
			# limit = { market.mg:steel = { market_goods_delta < 0 } }
			limit = { steel_production_delta < 0 }
			set_variable = { name = steel_delta			value =-1000000 }
			set_variable = { name = steel_balance_right	value =       0 }
			while = {		# use binary search to find the amount at which market_goods_delta is 0
				count = 21	# log2(1000000) + 1
				set_variable = {
					name = steel_balance_mid
					value = {
						add = {
							value = var:steel_delta
							add = var:steel_balance_right
							divide = 2
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					# limit = { market.mg:steel = { market_goods_delta > PREV.var:steel_balance_mid } }
					limit = { steel_production_delta > PREV.var:steel_balance_mid }
					set_variable = { name = steel_delta value = var:steel_balance_mid }
				}
				else = { set_variable = { name = steel_balance_right value = var:steel_balance_mid } }
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_steel_mill is_under_construction = yes } }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = steel_delta add = PREV.queued_steel } }
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { OR = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_construction_sector is_under_construction = yes } var:const_to_seed > 0 } }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = steel_delta subtract = PREV.queued_construction_steel } }
			}
			# here steel_delta holds the exact value of market_goods_delta (up to integer precision)
		}
		else = { set_variable = { name = steel_delta			value = 0 } }
	}
}

				}
				start_building_construction = building_steel_mill

effect_calc_coal_delta = {	# Thx to Pzixel
	hidden_effect = {
		remove_variable = coal_delta
		remove_variable = coal_balance_right
		remove_variable = coal_balance_mid
		if = {
			# limit = { market.mg:coal = { market_goods_delta < 0 } }
			limit = { coal_production_delta < 0 }
			set_variable = { name = coal_delta			value =-1000000 }
			set_variable = { name = coal_balance_right	value =       0 }
			while = {		# use binary search to find the amount at which market_goods_delta is 0
				count = 21	# log2(1000000) + 1
				set_variable = {
					name = coal_balance_mid
					value = {
						add = {
							value = var:coal_delta
							add = var:coal_balance_right
							divide = 2
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					# limit = { market.mg:coal = { market_goods_delta > PREV.var:coal_balance_mid } }
					limit = { coal_production_delta > PREV.var:coal_balance_mid }
					set_variable = { name = coal_delta value = var:coal_balance_mid }
				}
				else = { set_variable = { name = coal_balance_right value = var:coal_balance_mid } }
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_charcoal_burner is_under_construction = yes } }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = coal_delta add = PREV.queued_coal } }
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_steel_mill is_under_construction = yes } }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = coal_delta subtract = PREV.queued_steel_coal } }
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { any_scope_building = { trigger_is_private_infrastructure = yes is_under_construction = yes } }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = coal_delta subtract = PREV.queued_infrastructure_coal } }
			}
			# here coal_delta holds the exact value of market_goods_delta (up to integer precision)
		}
		else = { set_variable = { name = coal_delta	value = 0 } }
	}
}

			}
		}
	}

effect_calc_sulfur_delta = {	# Thx to Pzixel
	hidden_effect = {
		remove_variable = sulfur_delta
		remove_variable = sulfur_balance_right
		remove_variable = sulfur_balance_mid
		if = {
			# limit = { market.mg:sulfur = { market_goods_delta < 0 } }
			limit = { sulfur_production_delta < 0 }
			set_variable = { name = sulfur_delta			value =-1000000 }
			set_variable = { name = sulfur_balance_right	value =       0 }
			while = {		# use binary search to find the amount at which market_goods_delta is 0
				count = 21	# log2(1000000) + 1
				set_variable = {
					name = sulfur_balance_mid
					value = {
						add = {
							value = var:sulfur_delta
							add = var:sulfur_balance_right
							divide = 2
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					# limit = { market.mg:sulfur = { market_goods_delta > PREV.var:sulfur_balance_mid } }
					limit = { sulfur_production_delta > PREV.var:sulfur_balance_mid }
					set_variable = { name = sulfur_delta value = var:sulfur_balance_mid }
				}
				else = { set_variable = { name = sulfur_balance_right value = var:sulfur_balance_mid } }
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { any_scope_building = { OR = { is_building_type = building_charcoal_burner is_building_type = building_steel_mill } is_under_construction = yes } }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = sulfur_delta add = PREV.queued_sulfur } }
			}
			# here sulfur_delta holds the exact value of market_goods_delta (up to integer precision)
		}
		else = { set_variable = { name = sulfur_delta	value = 0 } }
	}
}

effect_calc_explosives_delta = {	# Thx to Pzixel
	hidden_effect = {
		remove_variable = explosives_delta
		remove_variable = explosives_balance_right
		remove_variable = explosives_balance_mid
		if = {
			# limit = { market.mg:explosives = { market_goods_delta < 0 } }
			limit = { explosives_production_delta < 0 }
			set_variable = { name = explosives_delta			value =-1000000 }
			set_variable = { name = explosives_balance_right	value =       0 }
			while = {		# use binary search to find the amount at which market_goods_delta is 0

				set_variable = {

					value = {
						add = {
							value = var:explosives_delta
							add = var:explosives_balance_right
							divide = 2
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					# limit = { market.mg:explosives = { market_goods_delta > PREV.var:explosives_balance_mid } }
					limit = { explosives_production_delta > PREV.var:explosives_balance_mid }
					set_variable = { name = explosives_delta value = var:explosives_balance_mid }
				}
				else = { set_variable = { name = explosives_balance_right value = var:explosives_balance_mid } }

				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = explosives_delta subtract = PREV.queued_construction_explosives } }
			}
			# here explosives_delta holds the exact value of market_goods_delta (up to integer precision)
		}

effect_calc_dye_delta = {	# Thx to Pzixel
	hidden_effect = {
		remove_variable = dye_delta
		remove_variable = dye_balance_right
		remove_variable = dye_balance_mid
		if = {
			# limit = { market.mg:dye = { market_goods_delta < 0 } }
			limit = { dye_production_delta < 0 }
			set_variable = { name = dye_delta			value =-1000000 }
			set_variable = { name = dye_balance_right	value =       0 }
			while = {		# use binary search to find the amount at which market_goods_delta is 0
				count = 21	# log2(1000000) + 1
				set_variable = {
					name = dye_balance_mid
					value = {
						add = {
							value = var:dye_delta
							add = var:dye_balance_right
							divide = 2
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					# limit = { market.mg:dye = { market_goods_delta > PREV.var:dye_balance_mid } }
					limit = { dye_production_delta > PREV.var:dye_balance_mid }
					set_variable = { name = dye_delta value = var:dye_balance_mid }
				}
				else = { set_variable = { name = dye_balance_right value = var:dye_balance_mid } }
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_synthetics_plant is_under_construction = yes } }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = dye_delta add = PREV.queued_synth_dye } }
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_dye_greenhouse is_under_construction = yes } }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = dye_delta add = PREV.queued_natural_dye } }
			}
			# here dye_delta holds the exact value of market_goods_delta (up to integer precision)
		}
		else = { set_variable = { name = dye_delta			value = 0 } }
		every_scope_state = {	# Replaces calc_dye
			limit = { trigger_dye_greenhouse_allowed = no }
			OWNER ?= { change_variable = { name = dye_delta subtract = PREV.natural_dye_production } }
		}
	}
}
effect_build_dye = {	# country_scope
	if = {
		limit = {
			trigger_forbidden_heavy_industry = no
			trigger_has_prestige_silk_company = no
			has_technology_researched = art_silk
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = effect_build_dye_synth.tt
			effect_calc_dye_delta	= yes
			effect_calc_silk_delta	= yes
			while = {
				limit = { var:dye_delta < 0 }
				every_scope_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							OWNER.var:dye_delta < 0
							OWNER.var:silk_delta < 0
						}
						is_treaty_port = no	# The calculation only applies to your market, which treaty ports aren't a part of!
						any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_synthetics_plant OR = { occupancy > 0.95 level_after_queued_constructions <= OWNER.economy_of_scale_target } }	# modifier:building_economy_of_scale_level_cap_add
					}
					start_building_construction = building_synthetics_plant
					OWNER = { change_variable = { name = dye_delta	add = PREV.dye_per_level } }
					OWNER = { change_variable = { name = silk_delta	add = PREV.silk_per_level } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			trigger_forbidden_heavy_industry = no
			has_technology_researched = aniline
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = effect_build_dye_synth.tt
			effect_calc_dye_delta = yes
			while = {
				limit = { var:dye_delta < 0 }
				every_scope_state = {
					limit = {
						OWNER.var:dye_delta < 0
						is_treaty_port = no	# The calculation only applies to your market, which treaty ports aren't a part of!
						any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_synthetics_plant OR = { occupancy > 0.95 level_after_queued_constructions <= OWNER.economy_of_scale_target } }	# modifier:building_economy_of_scale_level_cap_add
					}
					start_building_construction = building_synthetics_plant
					OWNER = { change_variable = { name = dye_delta	add = PREV.dye_per_level } }
					OWNER = { change_variable = { name = silk_delta	add = PREV.silk_per_level } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		# limit = { trigger_modern_agrarian_economy = no NOT = { has_technology_researched = aniline } }
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = effect_build_dye_natural.tt
			effect_calc_dye_delta = yes
			while = {
				limit = { var:dye_delta < 0 }
				every_scope_state = {
					limit = {
						OWNER.var:dye_delta < 0
						is_treaty_port = no	# The calculation only applies to your market, which treaty ports aren't a part of!
						any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_dye_greenhouse OR = { occupancy > 0.95 level_after_queued_constructions <= OWNER.economy_of_scale_target } }	# modifier:building_economy_of_scale_level_cap_add
					}
					start_building_construction = building_dye_greenhouse
					OWNER = { change_variable = { name = dye_delta add = PREV.dye_per_greenhouse_level } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

					}
					start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_synthetics_plant
					OWNER = { change_variable = { name = dye_delta	add = PREV.dye_per_level } }
					OWNER = { change_variable = { name = silk_delta	add = PREV.silk_per_level } }
				}
			}
		}

						any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_synthetics_plant OR = { occupancy > 0.95 level_after_queued_constructions <= OWNER.economy_of_scale_target } }	# modifier:building_economy_of_scale_level_cap_add

				}
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		# limit = { trigger_modern_agrarian_economy = no NOT = { has_technology_researched = aniline } }

						any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_dye_greenhouse OR = { occupancy > 0.95 level_after_queued_constructions <= OWNER.economy_of_scale_target } }	# modifier:building_economy_of_scale_level_cap_add
					}
					start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_dye_greenhouse
					OWNER = { change_variable = { name = dye_delta add = PREV.dye_per_greenhouse_level } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_calc_silk_delta = {	# Thx to Pzixel
	hidden_effect = {
		remove_variable = silk_delta
		remove_variable = silk_balance_right
		remove_variable = silk_balance_mid
		if = {
			# limit = { market.mg:silk = { market_goods_delta < 0 } }
			limit = { silk_production_delta < 0 }
			set_variable = { name = silk_delta			value =-1000000 }
			set_variable = { name = silk_balance_right	value =       0 }
			while = {		# use binary search to find the amount at which market_goods_delta is 0
				count = 21	# log2(1000000) + 1
				set_variable = {
					name = silk_balance_mid
					value = {
						add = {
							value = var:silk_delta
							add = var:silk_balance_right
							divide = 2
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					# limit = { market.mg:silk = { market_goods_delta > PREV.var:silk_balance_mid } }
					limit = { silk_production_delta > PREV.var:silk_balance_mid }
					set_variable = { name = silk_delta value = var:silk_balance_mid }
				}
				else = { set_variable = { name = silk_balance_right value = var:silk_balance_mid } }
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_synthetics_plant is_under_construction = yes } }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = silk_delta add = PREV.queued_synth_silk } }
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_silk_greenhouse is_under_construction = yes } }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = silk_delta add = PREV.queued_natural_silk } }
			}
			# here silk_delta holds the exact value of market_goods_delta (up to integer precision)
		}
		else = { set_variable = { name = silk_delta			value = 0 } }
		every_scope_state = {	# Replaces calc_silk
			limit = { trigger_silk_greenhouse_allowed = no }
			OWNER ?= { change_variable = { name = silk_delta subtract = PREV.natural_silk_production } }
		}
	}
}

	if = {
		limit = {
			trigger_forbidden_heavy_industry = no

				limit = { OR = { var:dye_delta < 0 var:silk_delta < 0 } }
				every_scope_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							OWNER.var:dye_delta < 0
							OWNER.var:silk_delta < 0
						}
						is_treaty_port = no	# The calculation only applies to your market, which treaty ports aren't a part of!
						any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_synthetics_plant OR = { occupancy > 0.95 level_after_queued_constructions <= OWNER.economy_of_scale_target } }	# modifier:building_economy_of_scale_level_cap_add
					}
					start_building_construction = building_synthetics_plant
					OWNER = { change_variable = { name = dye_delta	add = PREV.dye_per_level } }
					OWNER = { change_variable = { name = silk_delta	add = PREV.silk_per_level } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		# limit = { trigger_modern_agrarian_economy = no NOT = { has_technology_researched = aniline } }
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = effect_build_silk_natural.tt

effect_calc_oil_delta = {	# Thx to Pzixel
	hidden_effect = {
		remove_variable = oil_delta
		remove_variable = oil_balance_right
		remove_variable = oil_balance_mid
		if = {
			# limit = { market.mg:oil = { market_goods_delta < 0 } }
			limit = { oil_production_delta < 0 }
			set_variable = { name = oil_delta			value =-1000000 }
			set_variable = { name = oil_balance_right	value =       0 }
			while = {		# use binary search to find the amount at which market_goods_delta is 0
				count = 21	# log2(1000000) + 1
				set_variable = {
					name = oil_balance_mid
					value = {
						add = {
							value = var:oil_delta
							add = var:oil_balance_right
							divide = 2
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					# limit = { market.mg:oil = { market_goods_delta > PREV.var:oil_balance_mid } }
					limit = { oil_production_delta > PREV.var:oil_balance_mid }
					set_variable = { name = oil_delta value = var:oil_balance_mid }
				}
				else = { set_variable = { name = oil_balance_right value = var:oil_balance_mid } }
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_synthetic_oil_plant is_under_construction = yes } }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = oil_delta add = PREV.queued_oil } }
			}
			# here oil_delta holds the exact value of market_goods_delta (up to integer precision)
		}
		else = { set_variable = { name = oil_delta	value = 0 } }
	}
}
effect_build_oil = {	# country_scope
	custom_tooltip = {
		text = effect_build_oil.tt
		effect_calc_oil_delta		= yes
		effect_calc_rubber_delta	= yes
		while = {
			limit = { var:oil_delta < 0 }	# We only check for oil, becasue oil and rubber can be independent
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					OWNER.var:oil_delta < 0	# We only check for oil, becasue oil and rubber can be independent
					is_treaty_port = no	# The calculation only applies to your market, which treaty ports aren't a part of!
					any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_synthetic_oil_plant OR = { occupancy > 0.95 level_after_queued_constructions <= OWNER.economy_of_scale_target } }	# modifier:building_economy_of_scale_level_cap_add
				}
				start_building_construction = building_synthetic_oil_plant
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = oil_delta		add = PREV.oil_per_synth_level } }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = rubber_delta	add = PREV.rubber_per_synth_level } }
			}
		}
	}
}

effect_calc_engines_delta = {	# Thx to Pzixel
	hidden_effect = {
		remove_variable = engines_delta
		remove_variable = engines_balance_right
		remove_variable = engines_balance_mid
		if = {
			# limit = { market.mg:engines = { market_goods_delta < 0 } }
			limit = { engines_production_delta < 0 }
			set_variable = { name = engines_delta			value =-1000000 }
			set_variable = { name = engines_balance_right	value =       0 }
			while = {		# use binary search to find the amount at which market_goods_delta is 0
				count = 21	# log2(1000000) + 1
				set_variable = {
					name = engines_balance_mid
					value = {
						add = {
							value = var:engines_delta
							add = var:engines_balance_right
							divide = 2
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					# limit = { market.mg:engines = { market_goods_delta > PREV.var:engines_balance_mid } }
					limit = { engines_production_delta > PREV.var:engines_balance_mid }
					set_variable = { name = engines_delta value = var:engines_balance_mid }
				}
				else = { set_variable = { name = engines_balance_right value = var:engines_balance_mid } }
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_motor_industry is_under_construction = yes } }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = engines_delta add = PREV.queued_engines } }
			}
			# here engines_delta holds the exact value of market_goods_delta (up to integer precision)
		}
		else = { set_variable = { name = engines_delta	value = 0 } }
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { any_scope_building = { trigger_is_private_infrastructure = yes is_under_construction = yes } }
			OWNER = { change_variable = { name = engines_delta subtract = PREV.queued_infrastructure_engines } }
		}
	}
}
effect_build_engines = {	# country_scope
	custom_tooltip = {
		text = effect_build_engines.tt
		effect_calc_engines_delta = yes
		while = {
			limit = { var:engines_delta < 0 }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					OWNER.var:engines_delta < 0
					is_treaty_port = no	# The calculation only applies to your market, which treaty ports aren't a part of!
					any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_motor_industry OR = { occupancy > 0.95 level_after_queued_constructions <= OWNER.economy_of_scale_target } }	# modifier:building_economy_of_scale_level_cap_add
				}
				start_building_construction = building_motor_industry
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = engines_delta add = PREV.engines_per_level } }
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_build_engines_private = {	# country_scope
	custom_tooltip = {
		text = effect_build_engines.tt
		effect_calc_engines_delta = yes
		while = {
			limit = { var:engines_delta < 0 }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					OWNER.var:engines_delta < 0
					is_treaty_port = no	# The calculation only applies to your market, which treaty ports aren't a part of!
					any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_motor_industry OR = { occupancy > 0.95 level_after_queued_constructions <= OWNER.economy_of_scale_target } }	# modifier:building_economy_of_scale_level_cap_add
				}
				start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_motor_industry
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = engines_delta add = PREV.engines_per_level } }
			}
		}
	}
}
# Luxury
effect_calc_porcelain_delta = {	# Thx to Pzixel
	hidden_effect = {
		remove_variable = porcelain_delta
		remove_variable = porcelain_balance_right
		remove_variable = porcelain_balance_mid
		if = {
			# limit = { market.mg:porcelain = { market_goods_delta < 0 } }
			limit = { porcelain_production_delta < 0 }
			set_variable = { name = porcelain_delta			value =-1000000 }
			set_variable = { name = porcelain_balance_right	value =       0 }
			while = {		# use binary search to find the amount at which market_goods_delta is 0
				count = 21	# log2(1000000) + 1
				set_variable = {
					name = porcelain_balance_mid
					value = {
						add = {
							value = var:porcelain_delta
							add = var:porcelain_balance_right
							divide = 2
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					# limit = { market.mg:porcelain = { market_goods_delta > PREV.var:porcelain_balance_mid } }
					limit = { porcelain_production_delta > PREV.var:porcelain_balance_mid }
					set_variable = { name = porcelain_delta value = var:porcelain_balance_mid }
				}
				else = { set_variable = { name = porcelain_balance_right value = var:porcelain_balance_mid } }
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_glassworks is_under_construction = yes } }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = porcelain_delta add = PREV.queued_porcelain } }
			}
			# here porcelain_delta holds the exact value of market_goods_delta (up to integer precision)
		}
		else = { set_variable = { name = porcelain_delta	value = 0 } }
	}
}
effect_build_porcelain = {	# country_scope
	custom_tooltip = {
		text = effect_build_porcelain.tt
		effect_calc_glass_delta		= yes
		effect_calc_porcelain_delta	= yes
		while = {
			limit = { OR = { var:glass_delta < 0 var:porcelain_delta < 0 } }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					porcelain_per_level > 0
					OR = {
						OWNER.var:glass_delta < 0
						OWNER.var:porcelain_delta < 0
					}
					is_treaty_port = no	# The calculation only applies to your market, which treaty ports aren't a part of!
					any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_glassworks OR = { occupancy > 0.95 level_after_queued_constructions <= OWNER.economy_of_scale_target } }	# modifier:building_economy_of_scale_level_cap_add
				}
				start_building_construction = building_glassworks
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = glass_delta		add = PREV.glass_per_level } }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = porcelain_delta	add = PREV.porcelain_per_level } }
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_build_porcelain_private = {	# country_scope
	custom_tooltip = {
		text = effect_build_porcelain.tt
		effect_calc_glass_delta		= yes
		effect_calc_porcelain_delta	= yes
		while = {
			limit = { OR = { var:glass_delta < 0 var:porcelain_delta < 0 } }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					porcelain_per_level > 0
					OR = {
						OWNER.var:glass_delta < 0
						OWNER.var:porcelain_delta < 0
					}
					is_treaty_port = no	# The calculation only applies to your market, which treaty ports aren't a part of!
					any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_glassworks OR = { occupancy > 0.95 level_after_queued_constructions <= OWNER.economy_of_scale_target } }	# modifier:building_economy_of_scale_level_cap_add
				}
				start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_glassworks
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = glass_delta		add = PREV.glass_per_level } }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = porcelain_delta	add = PREV.porcelain_per_level } }
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_calc_fruit_delta = {	# Thx to Pzixel
	hidden_effect = {
		remove_variable = fruit_delta
		remove_variable = fruit_balance_right
		remove_variable = fruit_balance_mid
		if = {
			# limit = { market.mg:fruit = { market_goods_delta < 0 } }
			limit = { fruit_production_delta < 0 }
			set_variable = { name = fruit_delta			value =-1000000 }
			set_variable = { name = fruit_balance_right	value =       0 }
			while = {		# use binary search to find the amount at which market_goods_delta is 0
				count = 21	# log2(1000000) + 1
				set_variable = {
					name = fruit_balance_mid
					value = {
						add = {
							value = var:fruit_delta
							add = var:fruit_balance_right
							divide = 2
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					# limit = { market.mg:fruit = { market_goods_delta > PREV.var:fruit_balance_mid } }
					limit = { fruit_production_delta > PREV.var:fruit_balance_mid }
					set_variable = { name = fruit_delta value = var:fruit_balance_mid }
				}
				else = { set_variable = { name = fruit_balance_right value = var:fruit_balance_mid } }
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_banana_greenhouse is_under_construction = yes } }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = fruit_delta add = PREV.queued_fruit } }
			}
			# here fruit_delta holds the exact value of market_goods_delta (up to integer precision)
		}
		else = { set_variable = { name = fruit_delta	value = 0 } }
	}
}

			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					OWNER.var:sugar_delta < 0
					is_treaty_port = no	# The calculation only applies to your market, which treaty ports aren't a part of!
					any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_sugar_greenhouse OR = { occupancy > 0.95 level_after_queued_constructions <= OWNER.economy_of_scale_target } }	# modifier:building_economy_of_scale_level_cap_add
				}
				start_building_construction = building_sugar_greenhouse
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = sugar_delta add = PREV.sugar_per_level } }
			}
		}
	}
}

effect_calc_tobacco_delta = {	# Thx to Pzixel
	hidden_effect = {
		remove_variable = tobacco_delta
		remove_variable = tobacco_balance_right
		remove_variable = tobacco_balance_mid
		if = {
			# limit = { market.mg:tobacco = { market_goods_delta < 0 } }
			limit = { tobacco_production_delta < 0 }
			set_variable = { name = tobacco_delta			value =-1000000 }
			set_variable = { name = tobacco_balance_right	value =       0 }
			while = {		# use binary search to find the amount at which market_goods_delta is 0
				count = 21	# log2(1000000) + 1
				set_variable = {
					name = tobacco_balance_mid
					value = {
						add = {
							value = var:tobacco_delta
							add = var:tobacco_balance_right
							divide = 2
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					# limit = { market.mg:tobacco = { market_goods_delta > PREV.var:tobacco_balance_mid } }
					limit = { tobacco_production_delta > PREV.var:tobacco_balance_mid }
					set_variable = { name = tobacco_delta value = var:tobacco_balance_mid }
				}
				else = { set_variable = { name = tobacco_balance_right value = var:tobacco_balance_mid } }
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_tobacco_greenhouse is_under_construction = yes } }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = tobacco_delta add = PREV.queued_tobacco } }
			}
			# here tobacco_delta holds the exact value of market_goods_delta (up to integer precision)
		}
		else = { set_variable = { name = tobacco_delta	value = 0 } }
	}
}

			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_university is_under_construction = yes } }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = luxury_clothes_delta subtract = PREV.queued_uni_luxury_clothes } }

effect_build_luxury_clothes = {	# country_scope
	custom_tooltip = {
		text = effect_build_luxury_clothes.tt
		effect_calc_clothes_delta			= yes
		effect_calc_luxury_clothes_delta	= yes
		while = {
			limit = { OR = { var:clothes_delta < 0 var:luxury_clothes_delta < 0 } }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					luxury_clothes_per_level > 0
					OR = {
						OWNER.var:clothes_delta < 0
						OWNER.var:luxury_clothes_delta < 0
					}
					is_treaty_port = no	# The calculation only applies to your market, which treaty ports aren't a part of!
					any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_textile_mill OR = { occupancy > 0.95 level_after_queued_constructions <= OWNER.economy_of_scale_target } }	# modifier:building_economy_of_scale_level_cap_add
				}
				start_building_construction = building_textile_mill
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = clothes_delta			add = PREV.clothes_per_level } }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = luxury_clothes_delta	add = PREV.luxury_clothes_per_level } }
			}
		}
	}
}

				count = 21	# log2(1000000) + 1
				set_variable = {
					name = luxury_furniture_balance_mid
					value = {
						add = {
							value = var:luxury_furniture_delta
							add = var:luxury_furniture_balance_right
							divide = 2
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					# limit = { market.mg:luxury_furniture = { market_goods_delta > PREV.var:luxury_furniture_balance_mid } }
					limit = { luxury_furniture_production_delta > PREV.var:luxury_furniture_balance_mid }
					set_variable = { name = luxury_furniture_delta value = var:luxury_furniture_balance_mid }
				}
				else = { set_variable = { name = luxury_furniture_balance_right value = var:luxury_furniture_balance_mid } }
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_furniture_manufactory is_under_construction = yes } }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = luxury_furniture_delta add = PREV.queued_luxury_furniture } }
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_university is_under_construction = yes } }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = luxury_furniture_delta subtract = PREV.queued_uni_luxury_furniture } }
			}
			# here luxury_furniture_delta holds the exact value of market_goods_delta (up to integer precision)
		}
		else = { set_variable = { name = luxury_furniture_delta	value = 0 } }
	}
}
effect_build_luxury_furniture = {	# country_scope
	custom_tooltip = {
		text = effect_build_luxury_furniture.tt
		effect_calc_furniture_delta			= yes
		effect_calc_luxury_furniture_delta	= yes
		while = {
			limit = { OR = { var:furniture_delta < 0 var:luxury_furniture_delta < 0 } }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					luxury_furniture_per_level > 0
					OR = {
						OWNER.var:furniture_delta < 0
						OWNER.var:luxury_furniture_delta < 0
					}
					is_treaty_port = no	# The calculation only applies to your market, which treaty ports aren't a part of!
					any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_furniture_manufactory OR = { occupancy > 0.95 level_after_queued_constructions <= OWNER.economy_of_scale_target } }	# modifier:building_economy_of_scale_level_cap_add
				}
				start_building_construction = building_furniture_manufactory
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = furniture_delta		add = PREV.furniture_per_level } }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = luxury_furniture_delta	add = PREV.luxury_furniture_per_level } }
			}
		}
	}
}

				}
				start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_furniture_manufactory
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = furniture_delta		add = PREV.furniture_per_level } }

effect_calc_fine_art_delta = {	# Thx to Pzixel
	hidden_effect = {
		remove_variable = fine_art_delta
		remove_variable = fine_art_balance_right
		remove_variable = fine_art_balance_mid
		if = {
			# limit = { market.mg:fine_art = { market_goods_delta < 0 } }
			limit = { fine_art_production_delta < 0 }
			set_variable = { name = fine_art_delta			value =-1000000 }
			set_variable = { name = fine_art_balance_right	value =       0 }
			while = {		# use binary search to find the amount at which market_goods_delta is 0
				count = 21	# log2(1000000) + 1
				set_variable = {
					name = fine_art_balance_mid
					value = {
						add = {
							value = var:fine_art_delta
							add = var:fine_art_balance_right
							divide = 2
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					# limit = { market.mg:fine_art = { market_goods_delta > PREV.var:fine_art_balance_mid } }
					limit = { fine_art_production_delta > PREV.var:fine_art_balance_mid }
					set_variable = { name = fine_art_delta value = var:fine_art_balance_mid }
				}
				else = { set_variable = { name = fine_art_balance_right value = var:fine_art_balance_mid } }
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_art_academy is_under_construction = yes } }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = fine_art_delta add = PREV.queued_fine_art } }
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_university is_under_construction = yes } }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = fine_art_delta subtract = PREV.queued_uni_fine_art } }
			}
			# here fine_art_delta holds the exact value of market_goods_delta (up to integer precision)
		}
		else = { set_variable = { name = fine_art_delta	value = 0 } }
	}
}

	custom_tooltip = {
		text = effect_build_fine_art.tt
		effect_calc_fine_art_delta = yes
		while = {
			limit = { var:fine_art_delta < 0 }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					OWNER.var:fine_art_delta < 0
					is_treaty_port = no	# The calculation only applies to your market, which treaty ports aren't a part of!
					any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_art_academy OR = { occupancy > 0.95 level_after_queued_constructions <= OWNER.economy_of_scale_target } }	# modifier:building_economy_of_scale_level_cap_add
				}
				start_building_construction = building_art_academy
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = fine_art_delta add = PREV.fine_art_per_level } }
			}
		}
	}
}

					is_treaty_port = no	# The calculation only applies to your market, which treaty ports aren't a part of!

				}
				start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_automotive_industry
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = tanks_delta add = PREV.tanks_per_level } }
			}
		}
	}
}

effect_build_construction_goods_state = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			var:wood_target > 0
			OR = {
				any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_logging_camp			can_queue_building_levels = 1 }
				AND = {
					has_potential_resource = building_logging_camp
					NOT = { has_building = building_logging_camp }
				}
			}
		}
		start_building_construction = building_logging_camp
		change_variable = { name = wood_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:fabric_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_cotton_greenhouse
		change_variable = { name = fabric_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:tools_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_tooling_workshop
		change_variable = { name = tools_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:glass_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_glassworks
		change_variable = { name = glass_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:steel_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_steel_mill
		change_variable = { name = steel_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:explosives_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_explosives_factory
		change_variable = { name = explosives_target subtract = 1 }
	}
}
effect_build_construction_goods_private_state = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			var:wood_target > 0
			OR = {
				any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_logging_camp			can_queue_building_levels = 1 }
				AND = {
					has_potential_resource = building_logging_camp
					NOT = { has_building = building_logging_camp }
				}
			}
		}
		start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_logging_camp
		change_variable = { name = wood_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:fabric_target > 0 }
		start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_cotton_greenhouse
		change_variable = { name = fabric_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:tools_target > 0 }
		start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_tooling_workshop
		change_variable = { name = tools_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
