effect_add_trade_privilege = {
	every_diplomatic_play = {
		limit = { trigger_ROOT_committed_participant = yes }
		save_temporary_scope_as = play_scope
		every_scope_play_involved = {
			limit = {
				is_diplomatic_play_enemy_of = ROOT
				market.owner = THIS
				NOR = {
					is_country_type = decentralized
					is_subject_of = ROOT
				}
				ROOT = {
					NOR = {
						play_side_has_war_goal_of_type_against			= { type = annex_country		target = PREV }
						war_side_has_war_goal_of_type_against			= { type = annex_country		target = PREV }
						play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_tributary		target = PREV }
						war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_tributary		target = PREV }
						play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_dominion		target = PREV }
						war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_dominion		target = PREV }
						play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_protectorate	target = PREV }
						war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_protectorate	target = PREV }
						play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = transfer_subject		target = PREV }
						war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = transfer_subject		target = PREV }
						play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = reduce_autonomy		target = PREV }
						war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = reduce_autonomy		target = PREV }
					}
				}
			}
			save_temporary_scope_as = enemy_scope
			scope:play_scope = {
				if = {	# Only fire if enemy is already involved - Prevents incorrect play merging!
					limit = { any_scope_play_involved = { THIS ?= scope:enemy_scope } }
					add_war_goal = {
						holder = ROOT
						target_country = scope:enemy_scope
						type = enforce_treaty_article
						article = {
							article = trade_privilege
							source_country = scope:enemy_scope	# source is the country commiting to the article
							target_country = ROOT
						}
						primary_demand = no
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

effect_add_treaty_port_in_state_region = {
	random_diplomatic_play = {
		limit = { trigger_ROOT_committed_participant = yes }
		random_scope_play_involved = {
			limit = {
				is_diplomatic_play_enemy_of = ROOT
				NOR = {
					is_country_type = decentralized
					is_subject_of = ROOT
				}
				ROOT = {
					NOR = {
						play_side_has_war_goal_of_type_against			= { type = annex_country		target = PREV }
						war_side_has_war_goal_of_type_against			= { type = annex_country		target = PREV }
						play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_tributary		target = PREV }
						war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_tributary		target = PREV }
						play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_dominion		target = PREV }
						war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_dominion		target = PREV }
						play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_protectorate	target = PREV }
						war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_protectorate	target = PREV }
						play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = transfer_subject		target = PREV }
						war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = transfer_subject		target = PREV }
						play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = reduce_autonomy		target = PREV }
						war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = reduce_autonomy		target = PREV }
					}
				}
				OR = {
					owns_entire_state_region	= $state_region$												# The entire state_region
					owns_treaty_port_in			= $state_region$												# treaty_port
					any_scope_state = { state_region = s:$state_region$ controls_treaty_port_province = yes }	# Just the treaty port province - Breaks when occupied!
				}
			}
			save_temporary_scope_as = enemy_scope
			random_scope_state = {
				limit = { state_region = s:$state_region$ controls_treaty_port_province = yes }
				save_temporary_scope_as = state_scope
			}
		}
		add_war_goal = {
			holder = ROOT
			target_country = scope:enemy_scope
			type = enforce_treaty_article
			article = {
				article = treaty_port
				source_country = scope:enemy_scope	# source is the country commiting to the article
				target_country = ROOT
				inputs = { { state = scope:state_scope } }
			}
			primary_demand = yes
		}
	}
}
effect_add_treaty_port_in_market_capital = {
	random_diplomatic_play = {
		limit = { trigger_ROOT_committed_participant = yes }
		random_scope_play_involved = {
			limit = {
				is_diplomatic_play_enemy_of = ROOT
				NOR = {
					is_country_type = decentralized
					is_subject_of = ROOT
				}
				THIS ?= MARKET.OWNER
				ROOT = {
					NOR = {
						play_side_has_war_goal_of_type_against			= { type = annex_country		target = PREV }
						war_side_has_war_goal_of_type_against			= { type = annex_country		target = PREV }
						play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_tributary		target = PREV }
						war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_tributary		target = PREV }
						play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_dominion		target = PREV }
						war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_dominion		target = PREV }
						play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_protectorate	target = PREV }
						war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_protectorate	target = PREV }
						play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = transfer_subject		target = PREV }
						war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = transfer_subject		target = PREV }
						play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = reduce_autonomy		target = PREV }
						war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = reduce_autonomy		target = PREV }
					}
				}
				# OR = {
					# owns_entire_state_region	= $state_region$												# The entire state_region
					# owns_treaty_port_in			= $state_region$												# treaty_port
					# any_scope_state = { state_region = s:$state_region$ controls_treaty_port_province = yes }	# Just the treaty port province - Breaks when occupied!
				# }
			}
			save_temporary_scope_as = enemy_scope
			if = {
				limit = { market_capital ?= { is_coastal = yes controls_treaty_port_province = yes } }
				market_capital ?= { save_temporary_scope_as = state_scope }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { capital ?= { is_coastal = yes controls_treaty_port_province = yes } }
				capital ?= { save_temporary_scope_as = state_scope }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { any_scope_state = { is_coastal = yes controls_treaty_port_province = yes } }
				ordered_scope_state = {
					limit = { is_coastal = yes controls_treaty_port_province = yes }
					order_by = gdp
					position = 0
					save_temporary_scope_as = state_scope
				}
			}
		}
		add_war_goal = {
			holder = ROOT
			target_country = scope:enemy_scope
			type = enforce_treaty_article
			article = {
				article = treaty_port
				source_country = scope:enemy_scope	# source is the country commiting to the article
				target_country = ROOT
				inputs = { { state = scope:state_scope } }
			}
			primary_demand = yes
		}
	}
}

			limit = {
				is_diplomatic_play_enemy_of = ROOT
				NOT = { is_country_type = decentralized }

				ROOT = {
					NOR = {
						play_side_has_war_goal_of_type_against			= { type = annex_country		target = PREV }
						war_side_has_war_goal_of_type_against			= { type = annex_country		target = PREV }

						war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_dominion		target = PREV }
						play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_protectorate	target = PREV }
						war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_protectorate	target = PREV }
						play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = transfer_subject		target = PREV }
						war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = transfer_subject		target = PREV }
						play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = reduce_autonomy		target = PREV }
						war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = reduce_autonomy		target = PREV }
					}
				}
			}
			save_temporary_scope_as = enemy_scope

			scope:play_scope = {
				if = {	# Only fire if enemy is already involved - Prevents incorrect play merging!
					limit = { any_scope_play_involved = { THIS ?= scope:enemy_scope } }
					if = {
						limit = { ROOT = { is_country_type = unrecognized } }
						add_war_goal = {
							holder = ROOT
							type = make_tributary
							target_country = scope:enemy_scope
							primary_demand = no
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { scope:enemy_scope = {  country_is_colonial_or_company = yes } }
						add_war_goal = {
							holder = ROOT
							type = make_dominion
							target_country = scope:enemy_scope
							primary_demand = no
						}
					}
					else = {
						add_war_goal = {
							holder = ROOT
							type = make_protectorate
							target_country = scope:enemy_scope
							primary_demand = no
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_reduce_autonomy_in_play = {	# TODO: increase_autonomy
	every_diplomatic_play = {
		limit = { trigger_ROOT_committed_participant = yes }
		save_temporary_scope_as = play_scope
		every_scope_play_involved = {
			limit = {
				is_diplomatic_play_enemy_of = ROOT
				is_subject_of = ROOT
				ROOT = {
					NOR = {
						play_side_has_war_goal_of_type_against			= { type = annex_country		target = PREV }
						war_side_has_war_goal_of_type_against			= { type = annex_country		target = PREV }
						play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_tributary		target = PREV }
						war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_tributary		target = PREV }
						play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_dominion		target = PREV }
						war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_dominion		target = PREV }
						play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_protectorate	target = PREV }
						war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_protectorate	target = PREV }
						play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = transfer_subject		target = PREV }
						war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = transfer_subject		target = PREV }
						play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = reduce_autonomy		target = PREV }
						war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = reduce_autonomy		target = PREV }
					}
				}
			}
			save_temporary_scope_as = enemy_scope
			scope:play_scope = {
				if = {	# Only fire if enemy is already involved - Prevents incorrect play merging!
					limit = { any_scope_play_involved = { THIS ?= scope:enemy_scope } }
					add_war_goal = {
						holder = ROOT
						type = reduce_autonomy
						target_country = scope:enemy_scope
						primary_demand = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_transfer_subject_in_play = {
	every_diplomatic_play = {
		limit = { trigger_ROOT_committed_participant = yes }
		save_temporary_scope_as = play_scope
		every_scope_play_involved = {
			limit = {
				is_diplomatic_play_enemy_of = ROOT
				NOR = {
					is_country_type = decentralized
					is_subject_of = ROOT
				}
				ROOT = {
					NOR = {
						play_side_has_war_goal_of_type_against			= { type = annex_country		target = PREV }
						war_side_has_war_goal_of_type_against			= { type = annex_country		target = PREV }
						play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_tributary		target = PREV }
						war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_tributary		target = PREV }
						play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_dominion		target = PREV }
						war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_dominion		target = PREV }
						play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_protectorate	target = PREV }
						war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_protectorate	target = PREV }
						play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = transfer_subject		target = PREV }
						war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = transfer_subject		target = PREV }
						play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = reduce_autonomy		target = PREV }
						war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = reduce_autonomy		target = PREV }
					}
				}
			}
			save_temporary_scope_as = overlord_scope
			every_subject_or_below = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { is_diplomatic_play_ally_of = ROOT }
					is_subject = yes
					country_is_colonial_or_company = yes
					NOT = { is_subject_of = ROOT }
					ROOT = {
						NOR = {
							play_side_has_war_goal_of_type_against			= { type = annex_country		target = PREV }
							war_side_has_war_goal_of_type_against			= { type = annex_country		target = PREV }
							play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_tributary		target = PREV }
							war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_tributary		target = PREV }
							play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_dominion		target = PREV }
							war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_dominion		target = PREV }
							play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_protectorate	target = PREV }
							war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_protectorate	target = PREV }
							play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = transfer_subject		target = PREV }
							war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = transfer_subject		target = PREV }
							play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = reduce_autonomy		target = PREV }
							war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = reduce_autonomy		target = PREV }
						}
					}
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = enemy_scope
				scope:play_scope = {
					if = {	# Add subject to the play
						limit = { NOT = { any_scope_play_involved = { THIS ?= scope:enemy_scope } } }
						if = {
							limit = { trigger_ROOT_initiator = yes }
							add_target_backers = { scope:enemy_scope }
						}
						else_if = {
							limit = { trigger_ROOT_target = yes }
							add_initiator_backers = { scope:enemy_scope }
						}
					}
					if = {	# Only fire if enemy is already involved - Prevents incorrect play merging!
						limit = { any_scope_play_involved = { THIS ?= scope:overlord_scope } }
						add_war_goal = {
							holder = ROOT
							type = transfer_subject
							target_country = scope:enemy_scope
							primary_demand = no
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

				is_diplomatic_play_enemy_of = ROOT
				NOR = {

					is_subject_of = ROOT
				}
				ROOT = {
					NOR = {
						play_side_has_war_goal_of_type_against			= { type = annex_country		target = PREV }
						war_side_has_war_goal_of_type_against			= { type = annex_country		target = PREV }
						play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_tributary		target = PREV }
						war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_tributary		target = PREV }
						play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_dominion		target = PREV }
						war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_dominion		target = PREV }
						play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_protectorate	target = PREV }
						war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_protectorate	target = PREV }
						play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = transfer_subject		target = PREV }
						war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = transfer_subject		target = PREV }
						play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = reduce_autonomy		target = PREV }
						war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = reduce_autonomy		target = PREV }
					}
				}
			}
			save_temporary_scope_as = overlord_scope
			every_subject_or_below = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { is_diplomatic_play_ally_of = ROOT }
					is_subject = yes
					is_country_type = recognized
					NOT = { is_subject_of = ROOT }
					ROOT = {
						NOR = {
							play_side_has_war_goal_of_type_against			= { type = annex_country		target = PREV }
							war_side_has_war_goal_of_type_against			= { type = annex_country		target = PREV }
							play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_tributary		target = PREV }
							war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_tributary		target = PREV }
							play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_dominion		target = PREV }
							war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_dominion		target = PREV }
							play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_protectorate	target = PREV }
							war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_protectorate	target = PREV }
							play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = transfer_subject		target = PREV }
							war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = transfer_subject		target = PREV }
							play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = reduce_autonomy		target = PREV }
							war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = reduce_autonomy		target = PREV }
						}
					}
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = enemy_scope
				scope:play_scope = {
					if = {	# Add subject to the play
						limit = { NOT = { any_scope_play_involved = { THIS ?= scope:enemy_scope } } }
						if = {
							limit = { trigger_ROOT_initiator = yes }
							add_target_backers = { scope:enemy_scope }
						}
						else_if = {
							limit = { trigger_ROOT_target = yes }
							add_initiator_backers = { scope:enemy_scope }
						}
					}
					if = {	# Only fire if enemy is already involved - Prevents incorrect play merging!
						limit = { any_scope_play_involved = { THIS ?= scope:overlord_scope } }
						add_war_goal = {
							holder = ROOT
							type = transfer_subject
							target_country = scope:enemy_scope
							primary_demand = no
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

effect_add_slavery_ban = {
	every_diplomatic_play = {
		limit = { trigger_ROOT_committed_participant = yes }
		every_scope_play_involved = {
			limit = {
				is_diplomatic_play_enemy_of = ROOT
				NOT = { is_country_type = decentralized }
				NOT = { has_law = law_type:law_slavery_banned }
				ROOT = {
					NOR = {
						play_side_has_war_goal_of_type_against			= { type = annex_country		target = PREV }
						war_side_has_war_goal_of_type_against			= { type = annex_country		target = PREV }
					}
				}
			}
			save_temporary_scope_as = enemy_scope
			PREV = {
				if = {	# Only fire if enemy is already involved - Prevents incorrect play merging!
					limit = { any_scope_play_involved = { THIS ?= scope:enemy_scope } }
					add_war_goal = {
						holder = ROOT
						target_country = scope:enemy_scope
						type = enforce_treaty_article	# ban_slavery wargoal is broken
						article = {
							article = law_commitment
							inputs = { { law_type = law_type:law_slavery_banned } }
							source_country = scope:enemy_scope	# source is the country commiting to the article
							target_country = ROOT
						}
						primary_demand = no
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_add_slavery_enforcement = {
	every_diplomatic_play = {
		limit = { trigger_ROOT_committed_participant = yes }
		every_scope_play_involved = {
			limit = {
				is_diplomatic_play_enemy_of = ROOT
				OR = { is_subject = no is_subject_of = ROOT }
				NOT = { is_country_type = decentralized }
				NOT = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_debt_slavery }
				ROOT = {
					NOR = {
						play_side_has_war_goal_of_type_against			= { type = annex_country		target = PREV }
						war_side_has_war_goal_of_type_against			= { type = annex_country		target = PREV }
					}
				}
			}
			save_temporary_scope_as = enemy_scope
			PREV = {
				if = {	# Only fire if enemy is already involved - Prevents incorrect play merging!
					limit = { any_scope_play_involved = { THIS ?= scope:enemy_scope } }
					add_war_goal = {
						holder = ROOT
						target_country = scope:enemy_scope
						type = enforce_treaty_article
						article = {
							article = law_commitment
							inputs = { { law_type = law_type:law_debt_slavery } }
							source_country = scope:enemy_scope	# source is the country commiting to the article
							target_country = ROOT
						}
						primary_demand = no
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_revoke_enemy_claims_in_play = {
	every_diplomatic_play = {
		limit = { trigger_ROOT_committed_participant = yes }
		save_temporary_scope_as = play_scope
		every_scope_play_involved = {
			limit = {
				is_diplomatic_play_enemy_of = ROOT
				save_temporary_scope_as = enemy_scope
				ROOT = { any_scope_state = { has_claim_by = scope:enemy_scope } }
				ROOT = {
					NOR = {
						play_side_has_war_goal_of_type_against			= { type = annex_country		target = PREV }
						war_side_has_war_goal_of_type_against			= { type = annex_country		target = PREV }
					}
				}
			}
			ROOT = {
				every_scope_state = {
					limit = { has_claim_by = scope:enemy_scope }
					save_temporary_scope_as = state_scope
					scope:play_scope = {
						if = {	# Only fire if enemy is already involved - Prevents incorrect play merging!
							limit = { any_scope_play_involved = { THIS ?= scope:enemy_scope } }
							add_war_goal = {
								holder = ROOT
								type = revoke_claim
								target_state = scope:state_scope
								target_country = scope:enemy_scope
								primary_demand = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_transfer_homeland_in_play = {	# add_take_homeland return_homeland
	every_diplomatic_play = {
		limit = { trigger_ROOT_committed_participant = yes }
		save_temporary_scope_as = play_scope
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				# any_scope_play_involved = {	# TODO: Make this work.
					# OR = {
						# THIS ?= PREV
						# trigger_if = { limit = { is_subject = yes } THIS ?= PREV.OVERLORD }
					# }
				# }
				is_diplomatic_play_enemy_of = ROOT
				any_scope_state = {
					OR = {
						trigger_if = {
							limit = { NOR = { ROOT ?= c:LIB country_is_colonial_or_company = yes } }
							is_homeland_of_country_cultures = ROOT
						}
						has_claim_by = ROOT
						trigger_should_claim = { claimant = ROOT }
					}
				}
				ROOT = {
					NOR = {
						play_side_has_war_goal_of_type_against			= { type = annex_country		target = PREV }
						war_side_has_war_goal_of_type_against			= { type = annex_country		target = PREV }
					}
				}
			}
			save_temporary_scope_as = enemy_scope
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						trigger_if = {
							limit = { NOR = { ROOT ?= c:LIB country_is_colonial_or_company = yes } }
							is_homeland_of_country_cultures = ROOT
						}
						has_claim_by = ROOT
						trigger_should_claim = { claimant = ROOT }
					}
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = state_scope
				scope:play_scope = {
					if = {	# Only fire if enemy is already involved - Prevents incorrect play merging!
						limit = { any_scope_play_involved = { THIS ?= scope:enemy_scope } }
						if = {
							limit = { scope:state_scope = { has_claim_by = ROOT } }
							add_war_goal = {
								holder = ROOT
								type = return_state
								target_state = scope:state_scope
								primary_demand = yes
							}
						}
						else = {
							add_war_goal = {
								holder = ROOT
								type = conquer_state
								target_state = scope:state_scope
								primary_demand = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_claim_US_breakaways = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = c:ki_TTC
			any_state = {
				trigger_ki_TTC_state = yes
				NOR = {
					OWNER ?= c:ki_TTC
					has_claim_by = c:ki_TTC
				}
			}
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				trigger_ki_TTC_state = yes
				NOR = {
					OWNER ?= c:ki_TTC
					has_claim_by = c:ki_TTC
				}
			}
			state_region = { add_claim = c:ki_TTC }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = c:ki_DIX
			any_state = {
				trigger_ki_DIX_state = yes
				NOR = {
					OWNER ?= c:ki_DIX
					has_claim_by = c:ki_DIX
				}
			}
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				trigger_ki_DIX_state = yes
				NOR = {
					OWNER ?= c:ki_DIX
					has_claim_by = c:ki_DIX
				}
			}
			state_region = { add_claim = c:ki_DIX }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = c:ki_PAS
			any_state = {
				trigger_ki_PAS_state = yes
				NOR = {
					OWNER ?= c:ki_PAS
					has_claim_by = c:ki_PAS
				}
			}
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				trigger_ki_PAS_state = yes
				NOR = {
					OWNER ?= c:ki_PAS
					has_claim_by = c:ki_PAS
				}
			}
			state_region = { add_claim = c:ki_PAS }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = c:ki_GLR
			any_state = {
				trigger_ki_GLR_state = yes
				NOR = {
					OWNER ?= c:ki_GLR
					has_claim_by = c:ki_GLR
				}
			}
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				trigger_ki_GLR_state = yes
				NOR = {
					OWNER ?= c:ki_GLR
					has_claim_by = c:ki_GLR
				}
			}
			state_region = { add_claim = c:ki_GLR }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = c:ki_ROK
			any_state = {
				trigger_ki_ROK_state = yes
				NOR = {
					OWNER ?= c:ki_ROK
					has_claim_by = c:ki_ROK
				}
			}
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				trigger_ki_ROK_state = yes
				NOR = {
					OWNER ?= c:ki_ROK
					has_claim_by = c:ki_ROK
				}
			}
			state_region = { add_claim = c:ki_ROK }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = c:ki_MWR
			any_state = {
				trigger_ki_MWR_state = yes
				NOR = {
					OWNER ?= c:ki_MWR
					has_claim_by = c:ki_MWR
				}
			}
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				trigger_ki_MWR_state = yes
				NOR = {
					OWNER ?= c:ki_MWR
					has_claim_by = c:ki_MWR
				}
			}
			state_region = { add_claim = c:ki_MWR }
		}
	}
}
effect_transfer_homeland_in_play_to_ally = {
	every_diplomatic_play = {
		limit = { trigger_ROOT_committed_participant = yes }
		save_temporary_scope_as = play_scope
		every_scope_play_involved = {
			limit = {
				trigger_diplomatic_alliance = { TARGET = ROOT }	# is_diplomatic_play_ally_of = ROOT
				NOT = { is_diplomatic_play_enemy_of = ROOT }	# It is possible to end up in opposite sides of a war despite being allies.
			}
			save_temporary_scope_as = ally_scope
			scope:play_scope = {	# Needs to be executed for every ally country, but in play_scope
				every_scope_play_involved = {
					limit = {
						is_diplomatic_play_enemy_of = ROOT	# Could theoretically also use ally_scope here, but allegiances can shift during the play
						any_scope_state = { trigger_ally_state = yes }
						ROOT = {
							NOR = {
								play_side_has_war_goal_of_type_against			= { type = annex_country		target = PREV }
								war_side_has_war_goal_of_type_against			= { type = annex_country		target = PREV }
							}
						}
					}
					every_scope_state = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								trigger_if		= { limit = { country_is_colonial_or_company = no }															is_homeland_of_country_cultures	= ROOT }
								trigger_if		= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { NAND = { is_subject_of = ROOT country_is_colonial_or_company = yes } } }	has_claim_by					= ROOT }
							}
							OR = {
								has_claim_by = scope:ally_scope
								trigger_if		= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { THIS ?= c:CHI } }																							is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_china }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { THIS ?= c:LAN } }																							region = sr:region_indonesia }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { THIS ?= c:MON } }																							state_region = s:STATE_MONTENEGRO }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { THIS ?= c:ki_TTC } }																							trigger_ki_TTC_state = yes }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { THIS ?= c:ki_DIX } }																							trigger_ki_DIX_state = yes }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { THIS ?= c:ki_PAS } }																							trigger_ki_PAS_state = yes }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { THIS ?= c:ki_GLR } }																							trigger_ki_GLR_state = yes }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { THIS ?= c:ki_ROK } }																							trigger_ki_ROK_state = yes }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { THIS ?= c:ki_MWR } }																							trigger_ki_MWR_state = yes }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { any_primary_culture = { THIS ?= cu:japanese } country_is_colonial_or_company = no } }						region = sr:region_japan }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { is_subject_of = ROOT country_is_colonial_or_company = yes has_variable = senegal_subject_var } }				region = sr:region_senegal }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { is_subject_of = ROOT country_is_colonial_or_company = yes has_variable = niger_subject_var } }				region = sr:region_niger }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { is_subject_of = ROOT country_is_colonial_or_company = yes has_variable = ethiopia_subject_var } }			region = sr:region_ethiopia }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { is_subject_of = ROOT country_is_colonial_or_company = yes has_variable = zanj_subject_var } }				region = sr:region_zanj }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { is_subject_of = ROOT country_is_colonial_or_company = yes has_variable = southern_africa_subject_var } }		region = sr:region_southern_africa }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { is_subject_of = ROOT country_is_colonial_or_company = yes has_variable = congo_subject_var } }				region = sr:region_congo }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { is_subject_of = ROOT country_is_colonial_or_company = yes has_variable = indonesia_subject_var } }			region = sr:region_indonesia }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { is_subject_of = ROOT country_is_colonial_or_company = yes has_variable = indochina_subject_var } }			region = sr:region_indochina }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { is_subject_of = ROOT country_is_colonial_or_company = yes has_variable = arabic_subject_var } }				region = sr:region_arabic }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { is_subject_of = ROOT country_is_colonial_or_company = yes has_variable = india_subject_var } }				is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { is_subject_of = ROOT country_is_colonial_or_company = yes has_variable = china_subject_var } }				is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_china }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { is_subject_of = ROOT country_is_colonial_or_company = yes has_variable = north_america_subject_var } }		is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_north_america }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { is_subject_of = ROOT country_is_colonial_or_company = yes has_variable = central_america_subject_var } }		is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_central_america }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { is_subject_of = ROOT country_is_colonial_or_company = yes has_variable = south_america_subject_var } }		is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_south_america }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { country_is_colonial_or_company = no } }																		is_homeland_of_country_cultures = scope:ally_scope }
							}
						}
						save_temporary_scope_as = state_scope
						OWNER ?= { save_temporary_scope_as = enemy_scope }
						scope:play_scope = {	# Needs to be executed for every state, but in play_scope
							if = {	# Only fire if enemy is already involved - Prevents incorrect play merging!
								limit = { any_scope_play_involved = { THIS ?= scope:enemy_scope } }
								if = {
									limit = { scope:state_scope = { has_claim_by = scope:ally_scope } }
									add_war_goal = {
										holder = scope:ally_scope
										type = return_state
										target_state = scope:state_scope
										primary_demand = yes
									}
								}
								else_if = {
									limit = { scope:state_scope = { is_treaty_port = yes } }
									# add_war_goal = {	# Deprecated
										# holder = scope:ally_scope
										# type = take_treaty_port
										# target_state = scope:state_scope
										# primary_demand = yes
									# }
									add_war_goal = {
										holder = scope:ally_scope
										target_country = scope:enemy_scope
										type = enforce_treaty_article
										article = {
											article = treaty_port
											source_country = scope:enemy_scope	# source is the country commiting to the article
											target_country = scope:ally_scope
											inputs = { { state = scope:state_scope } }
										}
										primary_demand = yes
									}
								}
								else = {
									add_war_goal = {
										holder = scope:ally_scope
										type = conquer_state
										target_state = scope:state_scope
										primary_demand = yes
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	# clear_saved_scope = ally_scope
}
effect_transfer_homeland_in_play_to_subject = {
	every_diplomatic_play = {
		limit = { trigger_ROOT_committed_participant = yes }
		save_temporary_scope_as = play_scope
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { is_diplomatic_play_enemy_of = ROOT }
				is_subject_of = ROOT
			}
			save_temporary_scope_as = ally_scope
			scope:play_scope = {	# Needs to be executed for every ally country, but in play_scope
				every_scope_play_involved = {
					limit = {
						is_diplomatic_play_enemy_of = ROOT	# Could theoretically also use ally_scope here, but allegiances can shift during the play
						is_diplomatic_play_committed_participant = yes	# Only fire if enemy is committed - Prevents incorrect play merging!
						any_scope_state = { trigger_ally_state = yes }
						ROOT = {
							NOR = {
								play_side_has_war_goal_of_type_against			= { type = annex_country		target = PREV }
								war_side_has_war_goal_of_type_against			= { type = annex_country		target = PREV }
								play_side_has_war_goal_of_type_against			= { type = secession			target = PREV }
								war_side_has_war_goal_of_type_against			= { type = secession			target = PREV }
							}
						}
					}
					every_scope_state = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								trigger_if		= { limit = { country_is_colonial_or_company = no }															is_homeland_of_country_cultures	= ROOT }
								trigger_if		= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { NAND = { is_subject_of = ROOT country_is_colonial_or_company = yes } } }	has_claim_by					= ROOT }
							}
							OR = {
								has_claim_by = scope:ally_scope
								trigger_if		= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { THIS ?= c:CHI } }																							is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_china }

								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { THIS ?= c:ki_DIX } }																							trigger_ki_DIX_state = yes }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { THIS ?= c:ki_PAS } }																							trigger_ki_PAS_state = yes }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { THIS ?= c:ki_GLR } }																							trigger_ki_GLR_state = yes }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { THIS ?= c:ki_ROK } }																							trigger_ki_ROK_state = yes }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { THIS ?= c:ki_MWR } }																							trigger_ki_MWR_state = yes }

								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { is_subject_of = ROOT country_is_colonial_or_company = yes has_variable = niger_subject_var } }				region = sr:region_niger }

										primary_demand = yes
									}
								}

									}
								}

						}

		}

effect_transfer_homeland_in_play_to_temp_ally = {	# temporary_ally
	every_diplomatic_play = {
		limit = { trigger_ROOT_committed_participant = yes }
		save_temporary_scope_as = play_scope
		every_scope_play_involved = {
			limit = {
				is_diplomatic_play_ally_of = ROOT
				NOT = { is_subject_of = ROOT }
			}
			save_temporary_scope_as = ally_scope
			scope:play_scope = {	# Needs to be executed for every ally country, but in play_scope
				every_scope_play_involved = {
					limit = {
						is_diplomatic_play_enemy_of = ROOT	# Could theoretically also use ally_scope here, but allegiances can shift during the play
						is_diplomatic_play_committed_participant = yes	# Only fire if enemy is committed - Prevents incorrect play merging!
						any_scope_state = { trigger_ally_state = yes }
						ROOT = {
							NOR = {
								play_side_has_war_goal_of_type_against			= { type = annex_country		target = PREV }
								war_side_has_war_goal_of_type_against			= { type = annex_country		target = PREV }
							}
						}
					}
					every_scope_state = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								trigger_if		= { limit = { country_is_colonial_or_company = no }															is_homeland_of_country_cultures	= ROOT }
								trigger_if		= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { NAND = { is_subject_of = ROOT country_is_colonial_or_company = yes } } }	has_claim_by					= ROOT }
							}
							OR = {
								has_claim_by = scope:ally_scope
								trigger_if		= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { THIS ?= c:CHI } }																							is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_china }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { THIS ?= c:LAN } }																							region = sr:region_indonesia }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { THIS ?= c:MON } }																							state_region = s:STATE_MONTENEGRO }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { THIS ?= c:ki_TTC } }																							trigger_ki_TTC_state = yes }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { THIS ?= c:ki_DIX } }																							trigger_ki_DIX_state = yes }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { THIS ?= c:ki_PAS } }																							trigger_ki_PAS_state = yes }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { THIS ?= c:ki_GLR } }																							trigger_ki_GLR_state = yes }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { THIS ?= c:ki_ROK } }																							trigger_ki_ROK_state = yes }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { THIS ?= c:ki_MWR } }																							trigger_ki_MWR_state = yes }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { any_primary_culture = { THIS ?= cu:japanese } country_is_colonial_or_company = no } }						region = sr:region_japan }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { is_subject_of = ROOT country_is_colonial_or_company = yes has_variable = senegal_subject_var } }				region = sr:region_senegal }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { is_subject_of = ROOT country_is_colonial_or_company = yes has_variable = niger_subject_var } }				region = sr:region_niger }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { is_subject_of = ROOT country_is_colonial_or_company = yes has_variable = ethiopia_subject_var } }			region = sr:region_ethiopia }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { is_subject_of = ROOT country_is_colonial_or_company = yes has_variable = zanj_subject_var } }				region = sr:region_zanj }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { is_subject_of = ROOT country_is_colonial_or_company = yes has_variable = southern_africa_subject_var } }		region = sr:region_southern_africa }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { is_subject_of = ROOT country_is_colonial_or_company = yes has_variable = congo_subject_var } }				region = sr:region_congo }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { is_subject_of = ROOT country_is_colonial_or_company = yes has_variable = indonesia_subject_var } }			region = sr:region_indonesia }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { is_subject_of = ROOT country_is_colonial_or_company = yes has_variable = indochina_subject_var } }			region = sr:region_indochina }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { is_subject_of = ROOT country_is_colonial_or_company = yes has_variable = arabic_subject_var } }				region = sr:region_arabic }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { is_subject_of = ROOT country_is_colonial_or_company = yes has_variable = india_subject_var } }				is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { is_subject_of = ROOT country_is_colonial_or_company = yes has_variable = china_subject_var } }				is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_china }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { is_subject_of = ROOT country_is_colonial_or_company = yes has_variable = north_america_subject_var } }		is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_north_america }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { is_subject_of = ROOT country_is_colonial_or_company = yes has_variable = central_america_subject_var } }		is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_central_america }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { is_subject_of = ROOT country_is_colonial_or_company = yes has_variable = south_america_subject_var } }		is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_south_america }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { country_is_colonial_or_company = no } }																		is_homeland_of_country_cultures = scope:ally_scope }
							}
						}
						save_temporary_scope_as = state_scope
						OWNER ?= { save_temporary_scope_as = enemy_scope }
						scope:play_scope = {	# Needs to be executed for every state, but in play_scope
							if = {	# Only fire if enemy is already involved - Prevents incorrect play merging!
								limit = { any_scope_play_involved = { THIS ?= scope:enemy_scope } }
								if = {
									limit = { scope:state_scope = { has_claim_by = scope:ally_scope } }
									add_war_goal = {
										holder = scope:ally_scope
										type = return_state
										target_state = scope:state_scope
										primary_demand = yes
									}
								}
								else_if = {
									limit = { scope:state_scope = { is_treaty_port = yes } }
									# add_war_goal = {	# Deprecated
										# holder = scope:ally_scope
										# type = take_treaty_port
										# target_state = scope:state_scope
										# primary_demand = yes
									# }
									add_war_goal = {
										holder = scope:ally_scope
										target_country = scope:enemy_scope
										type = enforce_treaty_article
										article = {
											article = treaty_port
											source_country = scope:enemy_scope	# source is the country commiting to the article
											target_country = scope:ally_scope
											inputs = { { state = scope:state_scope } }
										}
										primary_demand = yes
									}
								}
								else = {
									add_war_goal = {
										holder = scope:ally_scope
										type = conquer_state
										target_state = scope:state_scope
										primary_demand = yes
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	# clear_saved_scope = ally_scope
}
effect_crusade_against_communism = {
	ordered_country = {
		limit = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_council_republic
			is_revolutionary = no
			is_subject = no
		}
		order_by = prestige
		position = 0
		save_temporary_scope_as = communist_country
	}
	create_diplomatic_play = {  
		name = anti_communist_crusade
		escalation = 0
		war = no
		initiator = ROOT
		target_country = scope:communist_country
		type = dp_regime_change
		save_temporary_scope_as = play_scope
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_council_republic
				is_revolutionary = no
				is_subject = no
				NOT = { THIS ?= scope:communist_country }
			}
			save_temporary_scope_as = enemy_scope
			scope:play_scope = {
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = ROOT
					type = regime_change
					target_country = scope:enemy_scope
					primary_demand = yes
				}
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = scope:enemy_scope
					type = humiliation
					target_country = ROOT
					primary_demand = yes
				}
			}
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_council_republic
					is_revolutionary = no
					is_subject_of = PREV
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = subject_scope
				scope:play_scope = {
					add_war_goal = {
						holder = ROOT
						type = regime_change
						target_country = scope:subject_scope
						primary_demand = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

effect_annex_indian_subjects = {
	if = {
		limit = { THIS ?= c:GBR }
		random_country = {
			limit = { is_subject_of = ROOT has_variable = india_subject_var }
			save_scope_as = consolidator_scope
		}
	}
	else = {
		save_scope_as = consolidator_scope
	}
	ordered_country = {
		limit = {
			is_in_geographic_region	= geographic_region_india
			is_subject_of			= scope:consolidator_scope
			is_revolutionary		= no
			NOT = { has_variable = india_subject_var }
		}
		order_by = prestige
		position = 0
		save_temporary_scope_as = indian_country
	}
	scope:consolidator_scope ?= {
		create_diplomatic_play = {
			name = indian_consolidation
			escalation = 0
			war = no
			initiator = scope:consolidator_scope
			target_country = scope:indian_country
			type = dp_annex_subject
			# save_scope_as = play_scope
			# add_war_goal = {
				# holder = ROOT
				# type = annex_country
				# target_country = scope:enemy_scope
			# }
		}
	}
	random_diplomatic_play = {	# We cannot save the scope inside the create_diplomatic_play effect.
		limit = {
			any_scope_play_involved = { THIS ?= scope:consolidator_scope }
			any_scope_play_involved = { THIS ?= scope:indian_country is_diplomatic_play_enemy_of = scope:consolidator_scope }
		}
		save_temporary_scope_as = play_scope
	}
	if = {
		limit = { scope:consolidator_scope ?= { is_subject_of = ROOT } }
		scope:play_scope = { add_initiator_backers = { scope:consolidator_scope.OVERLORD } }
	}
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			is_in_geographic_region	= geographic_region_india
			is_subject_of			= scope:consolidator_scope
			is_revolutionary		= no
			NOT = { THIS ?= scope:indian_country }
			# ROOT = {
				# NOR = {
					# play_side_has_war_goal_of_type_against			= { type = annex_country		target = PREV }
					# war_side_has_war_goal_of_type_against			= { type = annex_country		target = PREV }
				# }
			# }
		}
		save_temporary_scope_as = enemy_scope
		scope:play_scope = {
			add_target_backers = { scope:enemy_scope }
			add_war_goal = {
				holder = scope:consolidator_scope
				type = annex_country
				target_country = scope:enemy_scope
				primary_demand = yes
			}
			add_war_goal = {
				holder = scope:enemy_scope
				type = independence
				target_country = scope:consolidator_scope
				primary_demand = yes
			}
		}
	}
	clear_variable = annex_indian_subjects_setup
	clear_saved_scope = play_scope
	clear_saved_scope = consolidator_scope
}
effect_liberate_siberia = {
	every_diplomatic_play = {
		limit = {
			trigger_ROOT_committed_participant = yes
			any_scope_play_involved = {
				is_diplomatic_play_enemy_of = ROOT
				any_scope_state = { state_region = { is_homeland = cu:siberian } }
				NOT = { any_primary_culture = { THIS ?= cu:siberian } }
				ROOT = {
					NOR = {
						play_side_has_war_goal_of_type_against			= { type = annex_country		target = PREV }
						war_side_has_war_goal_of_type_against			= { type = annex_country		target = PREV }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		save_temporary_scope_as = play_scope
		ordered_scope_play_involved = {
			limit = {
				is_diplomatic_play_enemy_of = ROOT
				any_scope_state = { state_region = { is_homeland = cu:siberian } }
				NOT = { any_primary_culture = { THIS ?= cu:siberian } }
			}
			order_by = total_population
			position = 0
			save_temporary_scope_as = enemy_scope
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = international_relations } }	add_technology_researched = international_relations }
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = mass_communication } }			add_technology_researched = mass_communication }
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = nationalism } }				add_technology_researched = nationalism }
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = pan-nationalism } }			add_technology_researched = pan-nationalism }
			scope:play_scope = {
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = ROOT
					type = liberate_country
					country_definition = SIB
					target_country = scope:enemy_scope
					primary_demand = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

effect_liberate_US_breakaways = {	# ki_PAS ki_GLR ki_ROK ki_MWR ki_DIX ki_TTC	# Use scripted_triggers !
	effect_claim_US_breakaways = yes
	every_diplomatic_play = {
		limit = {
			trigger_ROOT_committed_participant = yes
			any_scope_play_involved = {
				is_diplomatic_play_enemy_of = ROOT
				any_scope_state = { 
					OR = {
						trigger_ki_TTC_state	= yes
						trigger_ki_DIX_state	= yes
						trigger_ki_PAS_state	= yes
						trigger_ki_GLR_state	= yes
						trigger_ki_ROK_state	= yes
						trigger_ki_MWR_state	= yes
					}
				}
				ROOT = {
					NOR = {
						play_side_has_war_goal_of_type_against			= { type = annex_country		target = PREV }
						war_side_has_war_goal_of_type_against			= { type = annex_country		target = PREV }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		save_temporary_scope_as = play_scope
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { any_country = { THIS ?= c:ki_TTC is_subject_of = ROOT } } }
			every_scope_play_involved = {
				limit = {
					is_diplomatic_play_enemy_of = ROOT
					any_scope_state = { trigger_ki_TTC_state = yes }
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = enemy_scope

					}
				}
			}
		}

				}
			}
		}
		if = {

			every_scope_play_involved = {
				limit = {
					is_diplomatic_play_enemy_of = ROOT

				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = enemy_scope
				scope:play_scope = {

						country_definition = ki_PAS
						target_country = scope:enemy_scope
						primary_demand = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { any_country = { THIS ?= c:ki_GLR is_subject_of = ROOT } } }
			every_scope_play_involved = {
				limit = {
					is_diplomatic_play_enemy_of = ROOT
					any_scope_state = { trigger_ki_GLR_state = yes }
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = enemy_scope

				}

						country_definition = ki_ROK
						target_country = scope:enemy_scope
						primary_demand = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { any_country = { THIS ?= c:ki_MWR is_subject_of = ROOT } } }
			every_scope_play_involved = {
				limit = {
					is_diplomatic_play_enemy_of = ROOT
					any_scope_state = { trigger_ki_MWR_state = yes }
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = enemy_scope
				scope:play_scope = {
					add_war_goal = {
						holder = ROOT
						type = liberate_country
						country_definition = ki_MWR
						target_country = scope:enemy_scope
						primary_demand = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

effect_reset_cultural_acceptance = {	# TODO: Rewrite to accept arguments?
	ordered_scope_pop = {
		limit = {
			state = { save_temporary_scope_as = state_scope }
			culture = {
				NOT = { is_primary_culture_of = ROOT }
				scope:state_scope = { "cultural_acceptance_delta(PREV)" < 0 }
				save_scope_as = culture_scope
			}
		}
		order_by = "cultural_acceptance_delta(culture)"
		culture = { save_scope_as = culture_scope }
	}
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = { any_scope_pop = { culture = { THIS ?= scope:culture_scope } } }
		add_acceptance = {
			target = scope:culture_scope
			value = {
				add = 100
				max = {
					add = "cultural_acceptance_delta(scope:culture_scope)"
					multiply = -1 # cannot add more delta than what gets you to 0
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_annex_indochina_subjects = {
	every_state = {
		limit = {
			OWNER = {
				NOR = {
					THIS ?= ROOT
					# has_variable = indochina_subject_var
				}
				target_is_our_or_fellow_subject = yes
			}
			region = sr:region_indochina 
		}
		set_state_owner = ROOT
	}
}
effect_add_anglo_indian_acceptance = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_primary_culture = { THIS ?= cu:british }
			NOT = { any_primary_culture = { THIS ?= cu:anglo_indian } }
			trigger_anglo_indian_country = yes	# Causes issues with colonial_administration
		}
		add_primary_culture = cu:anglo_indian
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			any_primary_culture = { THIS ?= cu:anglo_indian }
			any_primary_culture = { count > 1 }
			trigger_anglo_indian_country = no
		}
		remove_primary_culture = cu:anglo_indian
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_modifier = anglo_indian_cultural_acceptance_modifier_negative }
		remove_modifier = anglo_indian_cultural_acceptance_modifier_negative
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_modifier = heritage_group_south_asian_cultural_acceptance_modifier_negative }
		remove_modifier = heritage_group_south_asian_cultural_acceptance_modifier_negative
	}
	hidden_effect = {	# Would otherwise use the value of the executing scope.
		set_local_variable = { name = acceptance_multiplier_anglo_indian	value = pop_acceptance_multiplier_shared_language }
		set_local_variable = { name = acceptance_multiplier_heritage		value = pop_acceptance_multiplier_no_shared_heritage }
	}
	cu:anglo_indian = { save_temporary_scope_as = culture_scope }
	if = {
		limit = {
			trigger_anglo_indian_country = yes
			OR = {
				has_law_or_variant	= law_type:law_multicultural
				is_enacting_law		= law_type:law_multicultural
			}
		}
		add_culture_acceptance_modifier = {
			culture = scope:culture_scope
			multiplier = local_var:acceptance_multiplier_anglo_indian
			days = long_modifier_time	# 10 years
			is_decaying = yes
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = heritage_group_south_asian_cultural_acceptance_modifier_negative
			multiplier = local_var:acceptance_multiplier_heritage
			days = long_modifier_time	# 10 years
			is_decaying = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { trigger_anglo_indian_country = yes }
		add_culture_acceptance_modifier = {
			culture = scope:culture_scope
			multiplier = local_var:acceptance_multiplier_anglo_indian
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = heritage_group_south_asian_cultural_acceptance_modifier_negative
			multiplier = local_var:acceptance_multiplier_heritage
		}
	}
	hidden_effect = {
		remove_local_variable = acceptance_multiplier_anglo_indian
		remove_local_variable = acceptance_multiplier_heritage
	}
}

effect_replace_reactionary_leaders = {
	every_interest_group = {
		limit = { leader ?= { trigger_reactionary_character = yes } }
		save_temporary_scope_as = ig_scope
		OWNER = {
			create_character = {
				female = yes
				age = 18
				ig_leader = yes
				# religion = rel:atheist
				interest_group = scope:ig_scope
				trait_generation = {
					add_trait = innovative
					add_trait = popular_commander
					if = {
						limit = { trait_value > 0 }
						set_variable = has_historical_traits
						add_experience = trait_value
					}
					else = { add_experience = 4 }
					add_random_trait = personality
					if = { limit = { has_trait = bigoted } remove_trait = bigoted add_random_trait = personality }
					if = {
						limit = { OR = { NOT = { has_variable = has_historical_traits } trait_value <= 3 } }
						add_random_trait = skill
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_trait = traditionalist_commander }
						remove_trait = traditionalist_commander
					}
					if = { limit = { NOT = { has_modifier = modifier_character_extra_ten_percent_command } } add_modifier = { name = modifier_character_extra_ten_percent_command } }
				}
				on_created = {
					if = { limit = { NOT = { is_interest_group_type = ig_devout } } change_character_religion = rel:atheist }
					add_modifier = { name = modifier_character_popular_adored }
					if		= { limit = { OWNER = { has_technology_researched = feminism has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy } }	set_ideology = ideology:ideology_humanitarian_royalist }
					else_if	= { limit = { OWNER = { has_technology_researched = feminism } }											set_ideology = ideology:ideology_humanitarian }
					# else_if	= { limit = { OWNER = { has_technology_researched = egalitarianism } }										set_ideology = ideology:ideology_utopian }
					else	= {																											set_ideology = ideology:ideology_modernizer_leader }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_rebuild = {
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = $building$ } }
		save_temporary_scope_value_as = { name = $building$_level value = b:$building$.level }
		OWNER = { save_temporary_scope_as = owner_scope }
		remove_building = $building$
		create_building = { building = $building$ level = scope:$building$_level }
		# create_building = { building = $building$ add_ownership = { building = { type = building_financial_district country = "scope:owner_scope" levels = scope:$building$_level } } }
	}
}

		if = { limit = { has_building = building_rubber_plantation }	remove_building = building_rubber_plantation	create_building = { building = building_rubber_plantation	level = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_building = building_coal_mine }			remove_building = building_coal_mine			create_building = { building = building_coal_mine			level = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_building = building_iron_mine }			remove_building = building_iron_mine			create_building = { building = building_iron_mine			level = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_building = building_lead_mine }			remove_building = building_lead_mine			create_building = { building = building_lead_mine			level = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_building = building_sulfur_mine }			remove_building = building_sulfur_mine			create_building = { building = building_sulfur_mine			level = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_building = building_gold_mine }			remove_building = building_gold_mine			create_building = { building = building_gold_mine			level = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_building = building_oil_rig }				remove_building = building_oil_rig				create_building = { building = building_oil_rig				level = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_building = building_fishing_wharf }		remove_building = building_fishing_wharf		create_building = { building = building_fishing_wharf		level = 1 } }

	}

effect_unmodded_game_setup = {
	effect_historical_obsessions = yes
	effect_setup_add_amendment_apprenticeships = yes
	every_state = {
		effect_setup_replace_agro_state		= yes
		effect_setup_replace_plant_state	= yes
	}
	every_country = {
		limit = { NOT = { is_country_type = decentralized } }
		effect_recalc_build_options			= yes
		effect_seed_setup					= yes
	}
	effect_kickstart_trade = yes
	s:STATE_HAWAIIAN_ISLANDS		= { add_state_trait = state_trait_pearl_harbor }
	s:STATE_ERITREA					= { add_state_trait = state_trait_natural_harbors_djibouti }
	s:STATE_ANHALT					= { add_state_trait = state_trait_good_soils }
	s:STATE_BRUNSWICK				= { add_state_trait = state_trait_good_soils }
	s:STATE_RHINELAND				= { add_state_trait = state_trait_saar_coalfield }
	s:STATE_DISTRICT_OF_COLUMBIA	= { if = { limit = { NOT = { is_homeland = cu:yankee } } add_homeland = cu:yankee } }
	s:STATE_PALESTINE				= {
		if = { limit = { NOT = { is_homeland = cu:sephardic } } add_homeland = cu:sephardic }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { is_homeland = cu:ashkenazi } } add_homeland = cu:ashkenazi }
	}
	s:STATE_HEREROLAND				= { add_state_trait = state_trait_namib_desert }
	s:STATE_NAMAQUALAND				= { add_state_trait = state_trait_namib_desert }
	s:STATE_SOUTH_ANGOLA			= { add_state_trait = state_trait_namib_desert }
	s:STATE_ALTO_PARAGUAY			= { remove_state_trait = state_trait_gran_chaco }
	effect_republican_paternalistic = yes
	# every_country = { IPB_non_monarchistizer_effect = yes }	# Removed - Reactionary means Reactionary.
	every_country = {
		limit = { has_technology_researched = empiricism }
		ig:ig_landowners = { if = { limit = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_hierarchic } remove_ideology = ideology_hierarchic add_ideology = ideology_hierarchic_modern } }
	}
	every_country = { effect_reorder_landowner_traits = yes }
	every_state_region = {
		limit = {
			is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india
			NOT = { is_homeland = cu:anglo_indian }
		}
		add_homeland = cu:anglo_indian
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = c:BIC }
		c:BIC = {
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = india_subject_var } } set_variable = india_subject_var }
			effect_add_anglo_indian_acceptance = yes	# TODO: Potentially makes the game too easy.
			every_scope_pop = {
				limit = {
					culture = {
						has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_south_asian
						NOT = { THIS ?= cu:anglo_indian }
					}
					OR = {
						pop_employment_building_group = bg_government
						is_pop_type = bureaucrats
						is_pop_type = clerks
					}
				}
				change_pop_culture = { target = cu:anglo_indian value = 1 }
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = c:DEI c:DEI = { NOT = { has_variable = indonesia_subject_var } } }
		c:DEI = { set_variable = indonesia_subject_var }
	}
	effect_decentralize_depopulated_countries = yes
	effect_rebuild_industry = yes
	effect_rebuild_capped = yes
	effect_rebuild_infrastructure = yes
	every_country = {
		switch = {
			trigger = has_technology_researched
			conveyors			= { effect_assembly_lines_switch		= yes }
			rotary_valve_engine	= { effect_rotary_valve_engine_switch	= yes }
			watertube_boiler	= { effect_watertube_boiler_switch		= yes }
		}
		effect_reset_transport_switch	= yes
		effect_transport_switch			= yes
		effect_reset_autarky_pm_state	= yes
		effect_fertilizer_switch		= yes
		effect_tools_switch				= yes
		effect_mining_pumps_switch		= yes
		effect_dynamite_switch			= yes
		effect_greenhouse_switch		= yes
		every_scope_state = {
			effect_destroy_building = { building = building_barrack }
			effect_destroy_building = { building = building_conscription_center }
			effect_destroy_building = { building = building_naval_base }
		}
		effect_capital_army = yes
	}
}
sepoy_mutiny_apply_laws_effect = {	# Hijacking this to remove subject variables
	set_country_type = unrecognized
	activate_law = law_type:law_monarchy
	activate_law = law_type:law_autocracy
	effect_remove_subject_var_country = yes
}
effect_advanced_copy_laws = {
	if = {
		limit = { $source_country$ = { has_law = law_type:law_state_atheism } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = academia } }			add_technology_researched = academia	}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = romanticism } }		add_technology_researched = romanticism	}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = empiricism } }			add_technology_researched = empiricism	}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = realism } }			add_technology_researched = realism		}
		ig:ig_devout ?= { if = { limit = { is_in_government = yes }					remove_ruling_interest_group = yes } }
		activate_law = law_type:law_state_atheism
	}
	if = {
		limit = { $source_country$ = { has_law = law_type:law_graduated_taxation } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = rationalism } }		add_technology_researched = rationalism			}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = democracy } }			add_technology_researched = democracy			}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = academia } }			add_technology_researched = academia			}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = mass_communication } }	add_technology_researched = mass_communication	}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = empiricism } }			add_technology_researched = empiricism			}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = egalitarianism } }		add_technology_researched = egalitarianism		}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = dialectics } }			add_technology_researched = dialectics			}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = labor_movement } }		add_technology_researched = labor_movement		}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = socialism } }			add_technology_researched = socialism			}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { $source_country$ = { has_law = law_type:law_proportional_taxation } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = rationalism } }		add_technology_researched = rationalism		}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = democracy } }			add_technology_researched = democracy		}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = egalitarianism } }		add_technology_researched = egalitarianism	}
	}
	copy_laws = $source_country$
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_is_colonial_or_company = yes
			NOT = { has_law = law_type:law_colonial_administration }
		}
		activate_law = law_type:law_colonial_administration
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_is_colonial_or_company = yes
			$source_country$ = { NOR = { has_law = law_type:law_autocracy has_law = law_type:law_oligarchy has_law = law_type:law_technocracy } }
		}
		activate_law = law_type:law_oligarchy
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
			$source_country$ = { NOT = { has_law = law_type:law_multicultural } }
		}
		activate_law = law_type:law_subjecthood
		if = {
			limit = { country_is_colonial_or_company = yes }
			active_law:lawgroup_citizenship ?= { add_amendment = { type = amendment_racialized_subjecthood sponsor = PREV.ig:ig_armed_forces } }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { $source_country$ = { has_law = law_type:law_slave_trade } }
		activate_law = law_type:law_debt_slavery
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_is_colonial_or_company = yes
			$source_country$ = { has_law = law_type:law_elected_bureaucrats }
		}
		activate_law = law_type:law_appointed_bureaucrats
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_is_colonial_or_company = yes
			$source_country$ = { has_law = law_type:law_right_to_associate }
		}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = urbanization } }				add_technology_researched = urbanization			}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = democracy } }					add_technology_researched = democracy				}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = mass_communication } }			add_technology_researched = mass_communication		}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = tech_bureaucracy } }			add_technology_researched = tech_bureaucracy		}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = international_relations } }	add_technology_researched = international_relations	}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = nationalism } }				add_technology_researched = nationalism				}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = egalitarianism } }				add_technology_researched = egalitarianism			}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = labor_movement } }				add_technology_researched = labor_movement			}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = corporatism } }				add_technology_researched = corporatism				}
		activate_law = law_type:law_corporatized_unions
	}
}
effect_shared_colonial_creation = {
	if = {
		limit = { any_scope_state		= { region = sr:region_$name$	has_decree = decree_greener_grass_campaign } }
		random_scope_state = { limit	= { region = sr:region_$name$	has_decree = decree_greener_grass_campaign }	save_scope_as = newly_formed_colonial_nation_capital_scope }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { any_subject_or_below = { has_variable = $name$_subject_var	NOT = { any_primary_culture = { NOT = { is_primary_culture_of = ROOT } } } } }
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = $name$_subject_var
				is_subject_of = ROOT
				NOT = { any_primary_culture = { NOT = { is_primary_culture_of = ROOT } } }
			}
			capital = { save_scope_as = newly_formed_colonial_nation_capital_scope }
		}
	}
	else = { random_scope_state = { limit = { region = sr:region_$name$ } save_scope_as = newly_formed_colonial_nation_capital_scope } }
	every_scope_state = { limit = { region = sr:region_$name$ is_treaty_port = no } set_variable = state_to_cede }
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_subject_or_below = {
				has_variable = $name$_subject_var
				NOT = { any_primary_culture = { NOT = { is_primary_culture_of = ROOT } } }
			}
		}
		every_subject_or_below = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = $name$_subject_var
				is_subject_of = ROOT
				NOT = { any_primary_culture = { NOT = { is_primary_culture_of = ROOT } } }
			}
			every_scope_state = { set_variable = state_to_cede }
		}
	}
	create_dynamic_country = {
		origin = ROOT
		country_type = colonial
		tier = principality
		capital = scope:newly_formed_colonial_nation_capital_scope
		cede_state_trigger = { has_variable = state_to_cede }
		on_created = { 
			set_variable = $name$_subject_var
			set_variable = { name = newly_formed_colonial_nation_var months = 3 }
			every_scope_state = { remove_variable = state_to_cede }
			activate_law = law_type:law_frontier_colonization
			activate_law = law_type:law_colonial_administration
			add_modifier = { name = colonial_administration }
		}
	}
	hidden_effect = { create_diplomatic_pact = { country = scope:newly_formed_colonial_nation_capital_scope.owner	type = colony } }
	trigger_event = { id = colonial_administration_events.1 days = 1 popup = yes }
	custom_tooltip = je_colonial_administration_button_tt_1
	custom_tooltip = je_colonial_administration_button_tt_2
}
effect_shared_colonial_merge = {
	custom_tooltip = { text = transfer_stuff_to_subject_tt }
	ordered_subject_or_below = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = $name$_subject_var
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_primary_culture = { NOT = { is_primary_culture_of = ROOT } } }
				AND = {
					THIS ?= c:SIL	# African Culture
					c:SIL.OVERLORD ?= c:GBR
				}
			}
		}
		order_by		= gdp
		position		= 0
		save_scope_as	= colonial_transfer_subject_scope
	}
	if = {
		limit = { any_state = { region = sr:region_$name$	is_under_colonization = no is_treaty_port = no OWNER ?= { trigger_$name$_subject = no } } }
		every_state = {
			limit = { region = sr:region_$name$ is_under_colonization = no is_treaty_port = no OWNER ?= { trigger_$name$_subject = no } }
			set_state_owner = scope:colonial_transfer_subject_scope
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { any_subject_or_below = { has_variable = $name$_subject_var	count >= 2 } }
		random_subject_or_below = {
			limit = { has_variable = $name$_subject_var NOT = { any_primary_culture = { NOT = { is_primary_culture_of = ROOT } } }  }
			save_scope_as = $name$_subject_scope
		}
		random_subject_or_below = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = $name$_subject_var
				NOT = { THIS ?= scope:$name$_subject_scope }
			}
			save_scope_as = $name$_subject_scope_2
		}
		scope:$name$_subject_scope = { annex = scope:$name$_subject_scope_2 }
	}
}

	custom_tooltip = je_colonial_administration_button_tt_2

effect_shared_colonial_merge_geographic = {
	custom_tooltip = { text = transfer_stuff_to_subject_tt }
	random_subject_or_below = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = $name$_subject_var
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_primary_culture = { NOT = { is_primary_culture_of = ROOT } } }
				THIS ?= c:BIC	# Anglo-Indian Culture
				THIS ?= c:BHT	# Anglo-Indian Culture
			}
		}
		save_scope_as = colonial_transfer_subject_scope
	}
	if = {
		limit = { any_state = { is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_$name$	is_under_colonization = no is_treaty_port = no OWNER ?= { trigger_$name$_subject = no } } }
		every_state = {
			limit = { is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_$name$ is_under_colonization = no is_treaty_port = no OWNER ?= { trigger_$name$_subject = no } }
			set_state_owner = scope:colonial_transfer_subject_scope
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { any_subject_or_below = { has_variable = $name$_subject_var	count >= 2 } }
		random_subject_or_below = {
			limit = { has_variable = $name$_subject_var NOT = { any_primary_culture = { NOT = { is_primary_culture_of = ROOT } } } }
			save_scope_as = $name$_subject_scope
		}
		random_subject_or_below = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = $name$_subject_var
				NOT = { THIS ?= scope:$name$_subject_scope }
			}
			save_scope_as = $name$_subject_scope_2
		}
		scope:$name$_subject_scope = { annex = scope:$name$_subject_scope_2 }
	}
}
effect_colonial_LIB = {
	c:LIB = {
		if = {
			limit = { is_subject = yes }
			OVERLORD = { save_temporary_scope_as = OVERLORD_scope }
			save_temporary_scope_value_as = { name = LIB_liberty value = c:LIB.liberty_desire }
			if		= { limit = { is_subject_type = subject_type_protectorate }	set_variable = level_2 }
			else_if	= { limit = { is_subject_type = subject_type_puppet }		set_variable = level_1 }
		}
		if		= {
			limit = { country_is_colonial_or_company = no }
			set_country_type = colonial
			set_variable = senegal_subject_var
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = level_2 }
			scope:OVERLORD_scope = { create_diplomatic_pact = { country = c:LIB type = dominion } }
			add_liberty_desire = -50
			add_liberty_desire = scope:LIB_liberty
			remove_variable = level_2
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_variable = level_1 }
			scope:OVERLORD_scope = { create_diplomatic_pact = { country = c:LIB type = colony } }
			add_liberty_desire = -50
			add_liberty_desire = scope:LIB_liberty
			remove_variable = level_1
		}
	}
}
effect_handle_recognition = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = SUBJECT_scope
	if = {
		limit = { trigger_colonial_subject = yes }
		remove_variable = senegal_subject_var
		remove_variable = niger_subject_var
		remove_variable = ethiopia_subject_var
		remove_variable = zanj_subject_var
		remove_variable = southern_africa_subject_var
		remove_variable = congo_subject_var
		remove_variable = indonesia_subject_var
		remove_variable = indochina_subject_var
		remove_variable = arabic_subject_var
		remove_variable = india_subject_var
		remove_variable = china_subject_var
		remove_variable = north_america_subject_var
		remove_variable = central_america_subject_var
		remove_variable = south_america_subject_var
	}
	if = {
		limit = { is_subject = yes }
		OVERLORD ?= { save_temporary_scope_as = OVERLORD_scope }
		save_temporary_scope_value_as = { name = liberty_val value = liberty_desire }
		if		= {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_subject_type = subject_type_protectorate
					is_subject_type = subject_type_dominion
					is_subject_type = subject_type_tributary
				}
			}
			set_variable = level_2
		}
		else_if	= {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_subject_type = subject_type_puppet
					is_subject_type = subject_type_colony
					is_subject_type = subject_type_vassal
				}
			}
			set_variable = level_1
		}
	}
	set_country_type = recognized
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = level_2 }
		scope:OVERLORD_scope = { create_diplomatic_pact = { country = scope:SUBJECT_scope type = protectorate } }
		add_liberty_desire = -50
		add_liberty_desire = scope:liberty_val
		remove_variable = level_2
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_variable = level_1 }
		scope:OVERLORD_scope = { create_diplomatic_pact = { country = scope:SUBJECT_scope type = puppet } }
		add_liberty_desire = -50
		add_liberty_desire = scope:liberty_val
		remove_variable = level_1
	}
}
effect_annex_us_states = {
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			is_subject_of = ROOT
			is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_north_america	# Liberia
			# OR = {	# Lousianna
				# any_primary_culture = { THIS ?= cu:yankee }
				# any_primary_culture = { THIS ?= cu:dixie }
				# any_primary_culture = { THIS ?= cu:afro_american }
			# }
		}
		ROOT = { annex = PREV }
	}
}

effect_create_transit_treaty = {
	ordered_country = {
		limit = { trigger_could_supply_transit_rights_for = { target = ROOT } }
		order_by = relations:root	# Don't try with our rival(s)
		position = 0
		save_temporary_scope_as = neighbour_country
	}
	custom_tooltip = {
		text = create_transit_treaty.tt
		create_treaty = {
			first_country = ROOT
			second_country = scope:neighbour_country
			is_draft = no
			binding_period = { years = 25 }
			articles_to_create = {
				{
					article = trade_privilege
					source_country = ROOT
					target_country = scope:neighbour_country
				}
				{
					article = transit_rights
					source_country = scope:neighbour_country
					target_country = ROOT
				}
				{
					source_country = scope:neighbour_country
					target_country = ROOT
					article = goods_transfer
					inputs = {
						{ quantity = 50 }
						{ goods = g:merchant_marine }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_create_investment_rights_treaty_tp = {
	random_country = {
		limit = {
			ROOT ?= { owns_treaty_port_in = PREV }
			NOT = {
				any_scope_treaty = {
					binds = ROOT
					binds = PREV
					any_scope_article = { has_type = foreign_investment_rights }
				}
			}
		}
		save_temporary_scope_as = tp_country
	}
	custom_tooltip = {
		text = create_investment_treaty.tt
		create_treaty = {
			first_country = ROOT
			second_country = scope:tp_country
			is_draft = no
			binding_period = { years = 99 }
			articles_to_create = {
				{
					article = foreign_investment_rights
					source_country = scope:tp_country
					target_country = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_upgrade_offense_trait = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OWNER ?= { has_technology_researched = napoleonic_warfare }
			NOR = {
				has_trait = basic_offensive_planner
				has_trait = experienced_offensive_planner
				has_trait = expert_offensive_planner
			}
		}
		add_trait = basic_offensive_planner
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OWNER ?= { has_technology_researched = napoleonic_warfare }
			NOR = {
				has_trait = experienced_offensive_planner
				has_trait = expert_offensive_planner
			}
		}
		add_trait = experienced_offensive_planner
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OWNER ?= { has_technology_researched = napoleonic_warfare }
			NOT = { has_trait = expert_offensive_planner }
		}
		add_trait = expert_offensive_planner
	}
	hidden_effect = {
		if		= {
			limit = { has_trait = expert_offensive_planner }
			remove_trait = basic_offensive_planner
			remove_trait = experienced_offensive_planner
		}
		else_if	= {
			limit = { has_trait = experienced_offensive_planner }
			remove_trait = basic_offensive_planner
		}
	}
}

		remove_trait = experienced_convoy_raider
		add_trait = expert_convoy_raider
	}
	# hidden_effect = {
		# if		= {
			# limit = { has_trait = expert_naval_commander }
			# remove_trait = convoy_raider_commander
			# remove_trait = experienced_convoy_raider
		# }
