			# limit = { has_trait = experienced_convoy_raider }
			# remove_trait = convoy_raider_commander
		# }
	# }
}
effect_upgrade_popularity_trait = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_modifier = modifier_character_extra_ten_percent_command }
		remove_modifier = modifier_character_extra_ten_percent_command
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOR = {
				has_trait = innovative
				has_trait = traditionalist_commander
				has_trait = popular_commander
				has_trait = celebrity_commander
			}
		}
		add_trait = traditionalist_commander
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOR = {
				has_trait = popular_commander
				has_trait = celebrity_commander
			}
		}
		add_trait = popular_commander
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_trait = celebrity_commander }
		}
		add_trait = celebrity_commander
	}
	hidden_effect = {
		if		= {
			limit = { has_trait = celebrity_commander }
			remove_trait = traditionalist_commander
			remove_trait = popular_commander
		}
		else_if	= {
			limit = { has_trait = popular_commander }
			remove_trait = traditionalist_commander
		}
	}
}
effect_match_upgrade = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OWNER ?= {
				any_scope_character = {
					has_role = general
					has_trait = innovative
					has_trait = expert_offensive_planner
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_role = general
				has_trait = innovative
				has_trait = basic_offensive_planner
			}
			remove_trait	= basic_offensive_planner
			add_trait		= expert_offensive_planner
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_role = general
				has_trait = innovative
				has_trait = experienced_offensive_planner
			}
			remove_trait	= experienced_offensive_planner
			add_trait		= expert_offensive_planner
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OWNER ?= {
				any_scope_character = {
					has_role = general
					has_trait = innovative
					has_trait = experienced_offensive_planner
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_role = general
				has_trait = innovative
				has_trait = basic_offensive_planner
			}
			remove_trait	= basic_offensive_planner
			add_trait		= experienced_offensive_planner
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OWNER ?= {
				any_scope_character = {
					has_role = general
					has_trait = innovative
					has_trait = basic_offensive_planner
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_role = general
				has_trait = innovative
				NOT = { has_trait = basic_offensive_planner }
			}
			add_trait		= basic_offensive_planner
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OWNER ?= {
				any_scope_character = {
					has_role = general
					has_trait = innovative
					has_trait = expert_defensive_strategist
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_role = general
				has_trait = innovative
				has_trait = basic_defensive_strategist
			}
			remove_trait	= basic_defensive_strategist
			add_trait		= expert_defensive_strategist
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_role = general
				has_trait = innovative
				has_trait = experienced_defensive_strategist
			}
			remove_trait	= experienced_defensive_strategist
			add_trait		= expert_defensive_strategist
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OWNER ?= {
				any_scope_character = {
					has_role = general
					has_trait = innovative
					has_trait = experienced_defensive_strategist
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_role = general
				has_trait = innovative
				has_trait = basic_defensive_strategist
			}
			remove_trait	= basic_defensive_strategist
			add_trait		= experienced_defensive_strategist
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OWNER ?= {
				any_scope_character = {
					has_role = general
					has_trait = innovative
					has_trait = basic_defensive_strategist
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_role = general
				has_trait = innovative
				NOT = { has_trait = basic_defensive_strategist }
			}
			add_trait		= basic_defensive_strategist
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OWNER ?= {
				any_scope_character = {
					has_role = general
					has_trait = innovative
					has_trait = expert_artillery_commander
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_role = general
				has_trait = innovative
				has_trait = basic_artillery_commander
			}
			remove_trait	= basic_artillery_commander
			add_trait		= expert_artillery_commander
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_role = general
				has_trait = innovative
				has_trait = experienced_artillery_commander
			}
			remove_trait	= experienced_artillery_commander
			add_trait		= expert_artillery_commander
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OWNER ?= {
				any_scope_character = {
					has_role = general
					has_trait = innovative
					has_trait = experienced_artillery_commander
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_role = general
				has_trait = innovative
				has_trait = basic_artillery_commander
			}
			remove_trait	= basic_artillery_commander
			add_trait		= experienced_artillery_commander
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OWNER ?= {
				any_scope_character = {
					has_role = general
					has_trait = innovative
					has_trait = basic_artillery_commander
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_role = general
				has_trait = innovative
				NOT = { has_trait = basic_artillery_commander }
			}
			add_trait		= basic_artillery_commander
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OWNER ?= {
				any_scope_character = {
					has_role = general
					has_trait = innovative
					has_trait = defense_in_depth_specialist
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_role = general
				has_trait = innovative
				has_trait = stalwart_defender
			}
			remove_trait	= stalwart_defender
			add_trait		= defense_in_depth_specialist
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_role = general
				has_trait = innovative
				has_trait = trench_rat
			}
			remove_trait	= trench_rat
			add_trait		= defense_in_depth_specialist
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OWNER ?= {
				any_scope_character = {
					has_role = general
					has_trait = innovative
					has_trait = trench_rat
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_role = general
				has_trait = innovative
				has_trait = stalwart_defender
			}
			remove_trait	= stalwart_defender
			add_trait		= trench_rat
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OWNER ?= {
				any_scope_character = {
					has_role = general
					has_trait = innovative
					has_trait = stalwart_defender
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_role = general
				has_trait = innovative
				NOT = { has_trait = stalwart_defender }
			}
			add_trait		= stalwart_defender
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OWNER ?= {
				any_scope_character = {
					has_role = admiral
					has_trait = innovative
					has_trait = expert_naval_commander
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_role = admiral
				has_trait = innovative
				has_trait = basic_naval_commander
			}
			remove_trait	= basic_naval_commander
			add_trait		= expert_naval_commander
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_role = admiral
				has_trait = innovative
				has_trait = experienced_naval_commander
			}
			remove_trait	= experienced_naval_commander
			add_trait		= expert_naval_commander
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OWNER ?= {
				any_scope_character = {
					has_role = admiral
					has_trait = innovative
					has_trait = experienced_naval_commander
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_role = admiral
				has_trait = innovative
				has_trait = basic_naval_commander
			}
			remove_trait	= basic_naval_commander
			add_trait		= experienced_naval_commander
		}
	}
}
effect_fix_infrastructure_worldwide = {
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			has_technology_researched = railways
			any_scope_state = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_railway } } }
		}
		every_scope_state = { limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_railway } } is_incorporated = yes }		start_privately_funded_building_construction	= building_railway }
		every_scope_state = { limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_railway } } is_incorporated = no }		start_building_construction						= building_railway }
		effect_transport_switch	= yes
	}
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			has_technology_researched = electrical_generation
			any_scope_state = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_power_plant } } }
		}
		every_scope_state = { limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_power_plant } } is_incorporated = yes }	start_privately_funded_building_construction	= building_power_plant }
		every_scope_state = { limit = { NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_power_plant } } is_incorporated = no }	start_building_construction						= building_power_plant }
	}
	every_country = {
		limit = { any_scope_building = { trigger_is_private_infrastructure = yes is_subsidized = no } }
		every_scope_building = {
			limit = { trigger_is_private_infrastructure = yes is_subsidized = no }
			set_subsidized = yes
		}
	}
	every_country = {
		limit = { NOT = { is_country_type = decentralized } }
		effect_admin_switch			= yes
		effect_mining_pumps_switch	= yes
		effect_dynamite_switch		= yes
		effect_greenhouse_switch	= yes
		every_scope_building = { limit = { is_building_group = bg_mining			is_subsidized = no } set_subsidized = yes }
		every_scope_building = { limit = { is_building_group = bg_oil_extraction	is_subsidized = no } set_subsidized = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_railway } }
			effect_rail_type_switch			= yes
			effect_train_type_switch		= yes
			effect_passenger_type_switch	= yes
		}
	}
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { THIS ?= ROOT }
			any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_power_plant NOT = { has_active_production_method = pm_rail_transport_power_plant } }
			has_technology_researched = electric_railway
		}
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_power_plant NOT = { has_active_production_method = pm_rail_transport_power_plant } } }
			activate_production_method = { building_type = building_power_plant	production_method = pm_rail_transport_power_plant }
		}
	}
}
effect_advanced_fix_infrastructure_worldwide = {
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			has_technology_researched = tech_bureaucracy
			any_scope_state = {
				filter = { trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = yes }
				tax_capacity < tax_capacity_usage
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = "Fix bureacracy shortage for [THIS.GetCountry.GetName]"
			effect_make_admins = yes
		}
	}
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					has_technology_researched = railways
					any_scope_state = { sg:transportation = { state_goods_pricier > 0.50 } }
				}
				AND = {
					has_technology_researched = electrical_generation
					any_scope_state = { sg:electricity = { state_goods_pricier > 0.50 } }
				}
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = "Fix transportation and/or electricity shortage for [THIS.GetCountry.GetName]"
			effect_build_infrastructure_mixed = yes
		}
	}
	every_country = {
		limit = { any_scope_building = { trigger_is_private_infrastructure = yes is_subsidized = no } }
		every_scope_building = {
			limit = { trigger_is_private_infrastructure = yes is_subsidized = no }
			set_subsidized = yes
		}
	}
}
effect_add_all_good_laws = {
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_law = law_type:law_technocracy } }					activate_law = law_type:law_technocracy }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_law = law_type:law_slavery_banned } }				activate_law = law_type:law_slavery_banned }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_law = law_type:law_multicultural } }				activate_law = law_type:law_multicultural }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_law = law_type:law_state_atheism } }				activate_law = law_type:law_state_atheism }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_law = law_type:law_appointed_bureaucrats } }		activate_law = law_type:law_appointed_bureaucrats }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_law = law_type:law_professional_army } }			activate_law = law_type:law_professional_army }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_law = law_type:law_guaranteed_liberties } }		activate_law = law_type:law_guaranteed_liberties }
	if = {
		limit = { NOR = { has_law = law_type:law_interventionism has_law = law_type:law_cooperative_ownership } }
		activate_law = law_type:law_interventionism
	}
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_law = law_type:law_free_trade } }					activate_law = law_type:law_free_trade }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_law = law_type:law_graduated_taxation } }			activate_law = law_type:law_graduated_taxation }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_law = law_type:law_commercialized_agriculture } }	activate_law = law_type:law_commercialized_agriculture }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_law = law_type:law_colonial_exploitation } }		activate_law = law_type:law_colonial_exploitation }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_law = law_type:law_militarized_police } }			activate_law = law_type:law_militarized_police }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_law = law_type:law_public_schools } }				activate_law = law_type:law_public_schools }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_law = law_type:law_public_health_insurance } }		activate_law = law_type:law_public_health_insurance }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_law = law_type:law_protected_speech } }			activate_law = law_type:law_protected_speech }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_law = law_type:law_worker_protections } }			activate_law = law_type:law_worker_protections }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_law = law_type:law_compulsory_primary_school } }	activate_law = law_type:law_compulsory_primary_school }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_law = law_type:law_womens_suffrage } }				activate_law = law_type:law_womens_suffrage }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_law = law_type:law_old_age_pension } }				activate_law = law_type:law_old_age_pension }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_law = law_type:law_no_migration_controls } }		activate_law = law_type:law_no_migration_controls }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_law = law_type:law_right_to_associate } }			activate_law = law_type:law_right_to_associate }
}
effect_add_amendment_veteran_pensions = {
	if = {
		limit = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_old_age_pension }
		currently_enacting_law ?= {
			add_amendment = {
				type = amendment_veterans_pensions
				sponsor = PREV.ig:ig_armed_forces
			}
		}
		ig:ig_armed_forces = { improve_law_stance = yes }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_old_age_pension }
		active_law:lawgroup_welfare ?= {
			add_amendment = {
				type = amendment_veterans_pensions
				sponsor = PREV.ig:ig_armed_forces
			}
		}
	}
}

effect_remove_amendment_short_term_tax_cuts = {	# TODO: Implement for political concessions
	if = {
		limit = { currently_enacting_law = { has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_short_term_tax_cuts } }
		currently_enacting_law ?= {
			every_scope_amendment = {
				limit = { type = amendment_type:amendment_short_term_tax_cuts }
				remove_amendment = yes
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { any_law = { has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_short_term_tax_cuts } }
		random_law = {
			limit = { has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_short_term_tax_cuts }
			every_scope_amendment = {
				limit = { type = amendment_type:amendment_short_term_tax_cuts }
				remove_amendment = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

effect_add_amendment_prussian_education = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				is_enacting_law = law_type:law_religious_schools
				is_enacting_law = law_type:law_private_schools
				is_enacting_law = law_type:law_public_schools
			}
		}
		currently_enacting_law ?= {
			add_amendment = {
				type = amendment_prussian_education
				sponsor = PREV.ig:ig_armed_forces
			}
		}
		ig:ig_armed_forces = { improve_law_stance = yes }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_no_schools } }
		active_law:lawgroup_education_system ?= {
			add_amendment = {
				type = amendment_prussian_education
				sponsor = PREV.ig:ig_armed_forces
			}
		}
	}
}

effect_add_amendment_apprenticeships = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				is_enacting_law = law_type:law_religious_schools
				is_enacting_law = law_type:law_private_schools
				is_enacting_law = law_type:law_public_schools
			}
			currently_enacting_law ?= { NOT = { has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_apprenticeships } }
		}
		currently_enacting_law ?= {
			add_amendment = {
				type = amendment_apprenticeships
				sponsor = PREV.ig:ig_petty_bourgeoisie
				cooldown = 60
			}
		}
		set_variable = apprenticeships_var
		ig:ig_petty_bourgeoisie = { improve_law_stance = yes }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_no_schools }
			active_law:lawgroup_education_system ?= { NOT = { has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_apprenticeships } }
		}
		active_law:lawgroup_education_system ?= {
			add_amendment = {
				type = amendment_apprenticeships
				sponsor = PREV.ig:ig_petty_bourgeoisie
				cooldown = 60
			}
		}
		# set_variable = apprenticeships_var
	}
}
effect_inherit_apprenticeships = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = apprenticeships_var
			scope:relevant_education_law ?= { NOT = { has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_apprenticeships } }
		}
		scope:relevant_education_law ?= {	# active_law:lawgroup_education_system
			add_amendment = {
				type = amendment_apprenticeships
				sponsor = PREV.ig:ig_petty_bourgeoisie
				cooldown = 60
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_setup_add_amendment_apprenticeships = {
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				any_primary_culture = { has_discrimination_trait = language_germanophone }
				has_variable = apprenticeships_var
			}
			NOT = { active_law:lawgroup_education_system ?= { has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_apprenticeships } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_no_schools } }
			active_law:lawgroup_education_system ?= {
				add_amendment = {
					type = amendment_apprenticeships
					sponsor = PREV.ig:ig_petty_bourgeoisie
					cooldown = 60
				}
			}
		}
		# effect_add_amendment_apprenticeships = yes
	}
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			active_law:lawgroup_education_system ?= { has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_apprenticeships }
			NOT = { has_variable = apprenticeships_var }
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = "Add the apprenticeship variable"
			set_variable = apprenticeships_var
		}
	}
}

effect_add_amendment_tradition_of_free_elections = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				is_enacting_law = law_type:law_oligarchy
				is_enacting_law = law_type:law_landed_voting
				is_enacting_law = law_type:law_wealth_voting
				is_enacting_law = law_type:law_census_voting
				is_enacting_law = law_type:law_universal_suffrage
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					THIS ?= c:SWI
					was_formed_from = SWI
					ig:ig_rural_folk ?= { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_agrarian_jeffersonian }
				}
			}
			currently_enacting_law ?= {
				add_amendment = {
					type = amendment_tradition_of_free_elections
					sponsor = PREV.ig:ig_rural_folk
				}
			}
			ig:ig_rural_folk = { improve_law_stance = yes }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= { NOT = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_technocratic } } }
			currently_enacting_law ?= {
				add_amendment = {
					type = amendment_tradition_of_free_elections
					sponsor = PREV.ig:ig_intelligentsia
				}
			}
			ig:ig_intelligentsia = { improve_law_stance = yes }
		}
		else = {
			currently_enacting_law ?= {
				add_amendment = {
					type = amendment_tradition_of_free_elections
					sponsor = PREV.ig:ig_trade_unions
				}
			}
			ig:ig_trade_unions = { improve_law_stance = yes }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_oligarchy
				has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_landed_voting
				has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_wealth_voting
				has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_census_voting
				has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_universal_suffrage
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					THIS ?= c:SWI
					was_formed_from = SWI
					ig:ig_rural_folk ?= { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_agrarian_jeffersonian }
				}
			}
			active_law:lawgroup_distribution_of_power ?= {
				add_amendment = {
					type = amendment_tradition_of_free_elections
					sponsor = PREV.ig:ig_rural_folk
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= { NOT = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_technocratic } } }
			active_law:lawgroup_distribution_of_power ?= {
				add_amendment = {
					type = amendment_tradition_of_free_elections
					sponsor = PREV.ig:ig_intelligentsia
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			active_law:lawgroup_distribution_of_power ?= {
				add_amendment = {
					type = amendment_tradition_of_free_elections
					sponsor = PREV.ig:ig_trade_unions
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

effect_add_amendment_upper_strata_tax_cuts_large = {	# TODO: Make them replace each other
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				is_enacting_law = law_type:law_proportional_taxation
				is_enacting_law = law_type:law_graduated_taxation
			}
			NOR = {
				currently_enacting_law = { has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_upper_strata_tax_cuts_small }
				currently_enacting_law = { has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_upper_strata_tax_cuts_medium }
				currently_enacting_law = { has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_upper_strata_tax_cuts_large }
			}
		}
		currently_enacting_law ?= {
			add_amendment = {
				type = amendment_upper_strata_tax_cuts_large
				sponsor = PREV.ig:ig_industrialists
			}
		}
		ig:ig_industrialists = { improve_law_stance = yes }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_proportional_taxation
				has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_graduated_taxation
			}
			NOR = {
				active_law:lawgroup_taxation ?= { has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_upper_strata_tax_cuts_small }
				active_law:lawgroup_taxation ?= { has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_upper_strata_tax_cuts_medium }
				active_law:lawgroup_taxation ?= { has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_upper_strata_tax_cuts_large }
			}
		}
		active_law:lawgroup_taxation ?= {
			add_amendment = {
				type = amendment_upper_strata_tax_cuts_large
				sponsor = PREV.ig:ig_industrialists
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_give_all_GBR_african_colonies_to_SAF = {
	every_state = {
		limit = {
			is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_africa
			OWNER = {
				NOT = { THIS ?= c:SAF }
				OR = {
					THIS ?= c:GBR
					is_subject_of = c:GBR
				}
			}
		}
		set_state_owner = c:SAF
	}
}
effect_opium_wars_aggressor = {
	save_scope_as = opium_wars_aggressor_country
	set_variable = opium_wars_aggressor
	change_relations = { country = scope:opium_wars_target_country value = -30 }
	add_journal_entry = { type = je_opium_wars target = scope:opium_wars_target_country }
	hidden_effect = {
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { THIS ?= scop:opium_wars_aggressor_country }
				OR = {
					THIS ?= scope:opium_wars_target_country
					has_overlapping_interests = scope:opium_wars_target_country
					has_overlapping_interests = root
				}
			}
			post_notification = opium_wars_british_response_angry
		}
		scope:opium_wars_target_country = { set_secret_goal = { country = root secret_goal = antagonize } }
		set_secret_goal = { country = scope:opium_wars_target_country secret_goal = conquer }
	}
}
effect_tech_era_5 = {
	custom_tooltip = {
		text = "Execute"
		every_country = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_country_type = decentralized } }
			add_era_researched = era_1
			add_era_researched = era_2
			add_era_researched = era_3
			add_era_researched = era_4
			add_era_researched = era_5
			effect_assembly_lines_switch	= yes
			effect_reset_transport_switch	= yes
			effect_transport_switch			= yes
			effect_reset_autarky_pm_state	= yes
			effect_fertilizer_switch		= yes
			effect_tools_switch				= yes
			effect_mining_pumps_switch		= yes
			effect_dynamite_switch			= yes
			effect_greenhouse_switch		= yes
			effect_modern_hierarchic		= yes
			every_interest_group = {
				limit = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_liberal }
				remove_ideology = ideology_liberal
				add_ideology = ideology_liberal_modern
			}
			every_interest_group = {
				limit = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_egalitarian }
				remove_ideology = ideology_egalitarian
				add_ideology = ideology_egalitarian_modern
			}
		}
	}
}

			}
		}

			building	= building_autarky_greenhouse
			levels		= 5

			OR = {
				NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_cotton_greenhouse } }
				any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_cotton_greenhouse self_ownership_fraction = 0 }
			}
		}
		effect_add_private_levels_state = {
			building	= building_cotton_greenhouse
			levels		= 5
			owner		= building_cotton_greenhouse
			cost		= 2.0	# Every building costs 400 points x 1k/point in Millions
			country		= ROOT
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			trigger_dye_greenhouse_allowed = yes
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_dye_greenhouse } }
				any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_dye_greenhouse self_ownership_fraction = 0 }
			}
		}
		effect_add_private_levels_state = {
			building	= building_dye_greenhouse
			levels		= 5
			owner		= building_dye_greenhouse
			cost		= 2.0	# Every building costs 400 points x 1k/point in Millions
			country		= ROOT
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			trigger_silk_greenhouse_allowed = yes
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_silk_greenhouse } }
				any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_silk_greenhouse self_ownership_fraction = 0 }
			}
		}
		effect_add_private_levels_state = {
			building	= building_silk_greenhouse
			levels		= 5
			owner		= building_silk_greenhouse
			cost		= 2.0	# Every building costs 400 points x 1k/point in Millions
			country		= ROOT
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			trigger_logging_greenhouse_allowed = yes
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_logging_greenhouse } }
				any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_logging_greenhouse self_ownership_fraction = 0 }
			}
		}
		effect_add_private_levels_state = {
			building	= building_logging_greenhouse
			levels		= 5
			owner		= building_logging_greenhouse
			cost		= 2.0	# Every building costs 400 points x 1k/point in Millions
			country		= ROOT
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			trigger_rubber_greenhouse_allowed = yes
			OR = {
				NOT = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_rubber_greenhouse } }
				any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_rubber_greenhouse self_ownership_fraction = 0 }
			}
		}
		effect_add_private_levels_state = {
			building	= building_rubber_greenhouse
			levels		= 5
			owner		= building_rubber_greenhouse
			cost		= 2.0	# Every building costs 400 points x 1k/point in Millions
			country		= ROOT
		}
	}
}
effect_treat_affliction = {
	if = { limit = { has_trait = psychological_affliction }						remove_trait	= psychological_affliction }
	if = { limit = { has_trait = shellshocked }									remove_trait	= shellshocked }
	if = { limit = { has_trait = alcoholic }									remove_trait	= alcoholic }
	if = { limit = { has_trait = opium_addiction trigger_ruler_or_heir = no }	remove_trait	= opium_addiction }
	if = { limit = { has_trait = cancer is_historical = no }					remove_trait	= cancer }
	if = { limit = { has_trait = kidney_stones is_historical = no }				remove_trait	= kidney_stones }
	if = { limit = { has_trait = inept is_historical = no }						remove_trait	= inept }
	if = { limit = { has_trait = sickly is_historical = no }					remove_trait	= sickly }
	if = { limit = { has_trait = syphilis }										remove_trait	= syphilis }
	if = { limit = { has_modifier = modifier_commander_with_addiction }			remove_modifier	= modifier_commander_with_addiction }
	if = { limit = { has_modifier = public_shame_modifier }						remove_modifier	= public_shame_modifier }
	if = {
		limit = {	# Well, which one is it?
			trigger_ruler_or_heir = yes
			has_trait = bigoted
			has_modifier = modifier_scandalous_lover
		}
		remove_trait = bigoted
	}
	if = { limit = { trigger_historical_grifter = no			has_trait = grifter }			remove_trait = grifter }
	if = { limit = { trigger_historical_expensive_tastes = no	has_trait = expensive_tastes }	remove_trait = expensive_tastes }
}
effect_kill_unemployed = {
	# every_scope_pop = {
		# limit = {
			# is_employed = no
			# is_in_severe_starvation = yes
		# }
		
	# }
	kill_population_in_state = {
		value = 10000000
		culture = cu:north_german
		religion = rel:atheist
		pop_type = pop_type:laborers
	}
}
effect_transfer_indian_subjects_to_BIC = {
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			is_subject_of = c:BIC.OVERLORD
			NOR = {
				THIS ?= c:BIC
				is_subject_of = c:BIC
			}
			OR = {
				is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_india
				is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_south_east_asia
			}
		}
		c:BIC = { transfer_subject = PREV }
	}
}
